# Core Loops And Game Design

_core loop, progression systems, game feel, what makes gameplay sticky._

Evidence base: 1473 extracted items from 545 videos by 1Coal, 1_Bruce, AlexDev, AlvinBlox, Amora, ArTheDev, B Ricey, BloxxedDev, ByteBlox, CORSO, CaberJaber, CallMeDogey, Creators on the Blox, Cumin, Daiorg, Deconstructor of Fun, DitchyDevelopments, Duckable, Erthlord, GDC Festival of Gaming, Game Maker's Toolkit, GameMakers, GamesBeat, Generalist Programmer, GnomeCode, GosuGamers, ItsZyonic, Itz_FloppyFish, JustDucksey, Last Level Studios, Leif, Lnor Roblox, LovJak, MonarchDev, MonzterDEV, MoseyTube, Mulch, PineappleDev, Platform Aeronaut (transcript), Player.One, PolarBear, ProjectSupreme, Raul, RoDev, RoWatcher (tracker blog), Roblox, Roblox Creator Docs, Roblox Creators, Roblox DevForum, Roblox public API, Roox4, RoyalDev, SimTek Game Development, SmartyRBX, Stewiepfing, StudioKrew (agency blog, GGAID data), Suphi Kaner, TheDankDeveloper, TheDevBacon, TheDevKing, Tizzy, Ventures, VuukStudios, Yann, bubDEV, eGamers, janisjanis01, kra_DeZ, mats-dept2, rasdev, revolver agz, tef, waveshine (2015-01-03 to 2026-11-01). Roblox changes quickly: check the date on each source and see Date context below.

## Overview
Creators and Roblox staff agree on one thing above the rest: make the core loop fun first, then layer progression, monetisation and polish on top. The core loop has three parts: the minute-to-minute interaction, the most repeated action, and a progression engine. Every new system should feed that loop. Tumors (systems that compete with the loop) and parallel loops split the player base. Goals are layered as short-term (one session), mid-term (days) and long-term (weeks or months), with fast early rewards. Stickiness comes from instant feedback and juice, visible growth, rarity and RNG, risk and reward, social and PvP interaction, and replayability. Pitfalls: grind walls, fake exponential progression, unbalanced economies and soft-locks. Design mobile-first and aim for sessions above about 10 minutes.

## Rules and findings
- **Define one core loop and make it fun before anything else** (multiple-creators): The core loop is the most repeated minute-to-minute action. If it isn't fun, nothing else saves the game, and doubling retention of an unfun game still gives zero. Prototype it first and test it with friends. Write it as action, reward, repeat. If you can't spot it in about 10 seconds, the game lacks one. Sources: [Roblox Creators 2021-07 9:41](https://youtu.be/UL4QaSbfyC4?t=581), [Roblox Creators 2021-07 35:55](https://youtu.be/UL4QaSbfyC4?t=2155), [Roblox Creators 2022-01 0:18](https://youtu.be/bROouMt1b3g?t=18), [Roblox Creators 2022-02 9:03](https://youtu.be/iI2t7Yd7Byc?t=543), [Roblox Creators 2022-06 0:23](https://youtu.be/Mep4_w8qxeE?t=23). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Break the core loop into three parts** (multiple-creators): Roblox defines it as minute-to-minute interaction, the most repeated set of actions, and a progression engine that drives the player forward. Progression must feed back into earlier steps, and every role or team should share the engine. Always include progression, or the game gets repetitive and shallow. Sources: [Roblox Creators 2023-08 0:08](https://youtu.be/gkFKF9A-snY?t=8), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/game-design/core-loops), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/game-design/core-loops), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/game-design/core-loops), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/game-design/core-loops). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Tie every new system and update back to the core loop** (multiple-creators): Before adding a feature, ask how it feeds or augments the loop; if it doesn't, cut it. Integrate content into the loop rather than alongside it. This applies to quests, LiveOps, cadence content and progression layers. Build core systems first, since dependent systems (shops, inventory) rely on them. Sources: [Roblox Creators 2021-07 8:51](https://youtu.be/UL4QaSbfyC4?t=531), [Roblox Creators 2022-01 5:33](https://youtu.be/bROouMt1b3g?t=333), [Roblox Creators 2022-06 13:28](https://youtu.be/Mep4_w8qxeE?t=808), [Roblox Creators 2023-08 3:16](https://youtu.be/kNE757v3Bwg?t=196), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/game-design/liveops-essentials). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Avoid tumors and parallel loops** (multiple-creators): Systems that compete with the core loop split the player base and create update debt. Don't offer an opt-out alternative to the core activity (a 50/50 split tends to drift to 10/90). Aim for one loop per experience, and don't build dedicated content for a minority's emergent behaviours. Sources: [Roblox Creators 2021-07 15:54](https://youtu.be/UL4QaSbfyC4?t=954), [Roblox Creators 2021-07 16:38](https://youtu.be/UL4QaSbfyC4?t=998), [Roblox Creators 2021-07 40:43](https://youtu.be/UL4QaSbfyC4?t=2443), [Roblox Creators 2021-07 41:24](https://youtu.be/UL4QaSbfyC4?t=2484), [Roblox Creators 2022-06 38:48](https://youtu.be/Mep4_w8qxeE?t=2328). Newest source 2022-06-11.
- **Make the core loop simple, readable and instantly hooking** (multiple-creators): MoseyTube says players should grasp the loop in about 30 seconds with no tutorial; a DevForum creator says to explain it in one sentence. Keep the core simple, layer systems on top for depth, make cause and effect obvious, and show real gameplay from the first second. Trigger tutorials just in time. Sources: [MoseyTube 2026-06 0:41](https://youtu.be/2rLYGu0v490?t=41), [MoseyTube 2026-06 2:01](https://youtu.be/Kh_XKYw8ocU?t=121), [MoseyTube 2026-06 2:29](https://youtu.be/Kh_XKYw8ocU?t=149), [Roblox DevForum 2026-03](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/why-your-technically-superior-game-isn%E2%80%99t-succeeding-on-roblox-and-why-the-algorithm-ignores-it/4461606), [Roblox Creators 2021-07 39:19](https://youtu.be/UL4QaSbfyC4?t=2359). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Give instant feedback and juice on every action** (multiple-creators): Add VFX, sound, particles, number pop-ups, orbs flying to the player, camera shake, bounce tweens and a roughly 0.1s hit-stop. Feedback acts as reward and confirmation. Reuse genre-standard simulator effects. Small juice takes a beginner 20-30 minutes. Sources: [SmartyRBX 2024-02 14:17](https://youtu.be/kUNFgrvUkj4?t=857), [SmartyRBX 2024-02 14:17](https://youtu.be/kUNFgrvUkj4?t=857), [Roblox DevForum 2026-03](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/why-your-technically-superior-game-isn%E2%80%99t-succeeding-on-roblox-and-why-the-algorithm-ignores-it/4461606), [Stewiepfing 2024-02 6:28](https://youtu.be/v8tFfBTr6M8?t=388), [AlvinBlox 2025-06 3:21](https://youtu.be/Y93DRfB-Deo?t=201). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Layer goals by horizon and show the long-term goal early** (multiple-creators): Provide short-term (one session, usually the core loop), mid-term (days to a week) and long-term (weeks to months) goals; each tier feeds the next. Design from the long-term goal downward, make goals feel distant but achievable, and wrap them in lore. Mix ~10-minute goals (mobile) with 1-2+ hour goals (console). Sources: [Roblox Creators 2021-12 0:38](https://youtu.be/hD2Ive5RqtE?t=38), [Roblox Creators 2021-12 6:37](https://youtu.be/hD2Ive5RqtE?t=397), [Roblox Creators 2021-12 8:01](https://youtu.be/hD2Ive5RqtE?t=481), [Roblox Creators 2023-08 9:07](https://youtu.be/XfxbdKtSbOI?t=547), [Roblox Creators 2023-08 14:45](https://youtu.be/kNE757v3Bwg?t=885). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Front-load progression with quick early rewards** (multiple-creators): Early levels should take about a minute, with low XP thresholds. Something should unlock or a number should jump every few minutes. Avoid grind before the first reward, avoid grind walls, and give plenty of content upfront. Show visible growth (XP bars, multipliers, prestige) so minute 10 doesn't feel like minute 1. Sources: [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/game-design/onboarding), [Roblox Creators 2022-09 2:51](https://youtu.be/KD3YTfRH6fQ?t=171), [MoseyTube 2026-06 2:29](https://youtu.be/Kh_XKYw8ocU?t=149), [MoseyTube 2026-06 2:04](https://youtu.be/2rLYGu0v490?t=124), [Roblox DevForum 2026-03](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/why-your-technically-superior-game-isn%E2%80%99t-succeeding-on-roblox-and-why-the-algorithm-ignores-it/4461606). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Make rebirth and prestige meaningful** (multiple-creators): Rebirth resets progress for a permanent multiplier or unlock and serves as a long-term gate. Set first requirements low and later tiers hard. Prefer changing context over repeating the same loop with bigger numbers. Stack pet multipliers on rebirth multipliers. Make each stat multiply the previous one. Sources: [AlvinBlox 2019-01 22:25](https://youtu.be/0UhMpxxRFyc?t=1345), [Roblox DevForum 2021-03](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-make-your-game-have-replayability/1082534), [Mulch 2024-12 25:10](https://youtu.be/tdnhCXx3srA?t=1510), [SmartyRBX 2025-07 3:00](https://youtu.be/vBllyv2fnWw?t=180), [Generalist Programmer 2026-07 0:50](https://youtu.be/Oj0nbY61qsY?t=50). Newest source 2026-07-16.
- **Pace difficulty as a curve; keep it fair** (multiple-creators): Ramp up, peak at bosses, then dip. Launch slightly harder than target and ease later (easing is easier than hardening). Give breathing room between challenges, smooth spikes with hints, and keep changes within player control. Telegraph attacks, offer choices on instant-death challenges, fade platforms gradually, and use drop-off data to tune. Separate challenging from impossible. Sources: [Roblox Creators 2021-12 8:23](https://youtu.be/XTZxQvh1qkc?t=503), [Roblox Creators 2021-09 17:07](https://youtu.be/k2gbQI-ggB4?t=1027), [Roblox Creators 2021-09 1:17:07](https://youtu.be/k2gbQI-ggB4?t=4627), [Roblox Creators 2021-10 10:33](https://youtu.be/1stxDQeHhr8?t=633), [Roblox Creators 2021-10 17:44](https://youtu.be/1stxDQeHhr8?t=1064). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Give depth beyond a rising number and reveal it in layers** (multiple-creators): Static loops with only a growing number feel hollow. Add non-numeric challenges, fail states, new mechanics on upgrades rather than flat stats, and meaningful choices. Keep tools easy to learn but deep to master. Reveal systems gradually, don't explain every mechanic, use teasers, and give about 95% so players reach the last 5% themselves. Sources: [Cumin 2025-04 12:38](https://youtu.be/YmAwFbqUA0U?t=758), [Cumin 2025-04 13:19](https://youtu.be/YmAwFbqUA0U?t=799), [Cumin 2025-04 12:38](https://youtu.be/YmAwFbqUA0U?t=758), [Cumin 2025-04 13:19](https://youtu.be/YmAwFbqUA0U?t=799), [CORSO 2025-05 4:28](https://youtu.be/O2LZcQG5PPQ?t=268). Newest source 2026-09-18.
- **Don't fake progression with exponential numbers or currency walls** (multiple-creators): ByteBlox: with little content use flat difficulty increases and add content per stage; make content first, then exponential systems. Avoid currency-locked walls in favour of natural gates (equipment limits). Keep costs reachable. Stagger level locks so players don't grind one dungeon. Sources: [ByteBlox 2024-09 21:43](https://youtu.be/Nzrtg8SGOY0?t=1303), [ByteBlox 2024-09 21:43](https://youtu.be/Nzrtg8SGOY0?t=1303), [CORSO 2025-05 0:30](https://youtu.be/O2LZcQG5PPQ?t=30), [ByteBlox 2024-09 30:08](https://youtu.be/Nzrtg8SGOY0?t=1808), [ByteBlox 2024-09 30:08](https://youtu.be/Nzrtg8SGOY0?t=1808). Newest source 2026-09-13.
- **Reward desired behaviour; add incentives via taps** (multiple-creators): Reward the play style you want rather than punishing others, put loot on enemies to encourage fighting, give unique biome materials to encourage exploration, and make collectibles feed progression. Sources: [Roblox DevForum 2017-11](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/game-design-theory-planning-a-game-and-implementing-features/62838), [Roblox DevForum 2017-11](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/game-design-theory-planning-a-game-and-implementing-features/62838), [Game Maker's Toolkit 2022-04 1:54](https://youtu.be/Zrf1cou_yVo?t=114), [Roblox Creators 2022-08 1:26](https://youtu.be/lJAEKxoIUAs?t=86), [Roblox Creators 2022-05 25:22](https://youtu.be/xBlHz30PMKo?t=1522). Newest source 2022-08-18.
- **Balance the economy with sources, sinks and caps** (multiple-creators): Keep sources minus sinks near zero. Add sinks when wallets grow or a resource goes unspent. Cap resources and inventories, add drains, set a daily reward cap, scale conversion cost with level, use a separate event currency, and compute expected value per action. Fewer currencies make harder decisions; per-track currencies allow multiple progress lines. Sources: [Roblox Creators 2021-08 17:09](https://youtu.be/pSjFYDEgRxs?t=1029), [Roblox Creators 2021-08 18:32](https://youtu.be/pSjFYDEgRxs?t=1112), [Roblox Creators 2021-09 26:09](https://youtu.be/kQIyqMdh7Os?t=1569), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/economy-events), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/economy-events). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Use rarity and weighted RNG correctly** (multiple-creators): Roll from the rarest tier down; weights can be arbitrary integers or sum to 100. Keep config in one module. Show '1 in X' odds, but hide rarity until reveal (Lnor Roblox). Add luck modifiers and pity. Decide whether RNG shows before or after acquisition. Randomness multiplies content runway. Sources: [Roblox DevForum 2020-06](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-make-server-synced-daily-shops/632401), [ByteBlox 2024-05 5:07](https://youtu.be/UUgj-hmX2LM?t=307), [ByteBlox 2025-02 4:06](https://youtu.be/o-8V99horro?t=246), [PineappleDev 2025-07 0:22](https://youtu.be/QRkRk-FHN4s?t=22), [ArTheDev 2026-04 8:05](https://youtu.be/Iy9xuT1bpY4?t=485). Newest source 2026-10-12.
- **Keep skill in the loop; don't let RNG or idle replace it** (multiple-creators): Add skill checks, direct-hit bonuses, combo streaks and some required skill. Auto-roll let kids reach endgame in about 3 hours and wrecked an economy. Pure auto-clicking in one spot shouldn't be the optimal meta. Sources: [Tizzy 2026-09 10:21](https://youtu.be/CRCcsYEB_6A?t=621), [TheDankDeveloper 2026-01 10:09](https://youtu.be/BYhhm9o3H_E?t=609), [TheDankDeveloper 2026-10 0:40](https://youtu.be/eExtO_uF6Jg?t=40), [Duckable 2025-03 5:26](https://youtu.be/I9eyeEG6dpc?t=326), [Duckable 2025-03 5:26](https://youtu.be/I9eyeEG6dpc?t=326). Newest source 2026-10-06.
- **Design AFK and offline progress deliberately** (multiple-creators): AFK modes extend sessions and offline growth rewards return. Give players something to do while waiting. Make collectors roam and add capacity limits to force revisits. Welcome-back and velocity rewards shorten grind. Sources: [RoWatcher (tracker blog) 2026-00](https://rowatcher.com/news/fish-it-reels-in-270k-players-inside-roblox-s-most-addictive-casual-game-of-2026), [Mulch 2024-12 8:16](https://youtu.be/tdnhCXx3srA?t=496), [GamesBeat 2025-08](https://gamesbeat.com/?p=305182), [Roblox 2025-09 4:46](https://youtu.be/6iB5__RCQDc?t=286), [RoDev 2025-05 10:51](https://youtu.be/Gw2FwndwnOg?t=651). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Build social play into the loop, but keep solo and empty servers working** (multiple-creators): Solo-only games struggle. Add trading, parties, visible progress and clustered shared spaces, but the core loop must still work solo (AI modes, practice areas, tasks that count without guests). Remove social gates from the loop. Sources: [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/game-design/design-for-roblox), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/game-design/design-for-roblox), [VuukStudios 2026-06 8:23](https://youtu.be/catPKgFIZbQ?t=503), [rasdev 2026-10 7:28](https://youtu.be/e4o-ECkzDoI?t=448), [VuukStudios 2026-09 8:21](https://youtu.be/d5YHwCqwgYM?t=501). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Add risk/reward and real stakes** (multiple-creators): Use steal/raid loops, cash-out decisions, safe-zone loops, timers and chasers, and meaningful death. Never reset all progress on death in a non-roguelike (Cumin). Give dead players something to do. Raiding needs a reason to defend and small servers. Sources: [CaberJaber 2026-03 8:17](https://youtu.be/XEA41eXTfoA?t=497), [MonarchDev 2026-10 2:32](https://youtu.be/bbCmt98O9MA?t=152), [TheDankDeveloper 2026-05 2:45](https://youtu.be/uYuiCJrAcmI?t=165), [TheDankDeveloper 2026-05 3:26](https://youtu.be/uYuiCJrAcmI?t=206), [Tizzy 2026-09 4:42](https://youtu.be/CRCcsYEB_6A?t=282). Newest source 2026-11-01.
- **Make quests and missions serve the loop and respect time** (multiple-creators): Phrase on outcomes, avoid hard-currency tasks, keep dailies completable in a session, vary categories, chain quests, allow multi-progress, and tailor by level. Never gate gameplay behind notifications. Use quests to surface undiscovered features. Sources: [Roblox Creators 2021-10 0:05](https://youtu.be/1stxDQeHhr8?t=5), [Roblox Creators 2021-10 4:23](https://youtu.be/1stxDQeHhr8?t=263), [Roblox Creators 2021-10 5:04](https://youtu.be/1stxDQeHhr8?t=304), [Roblox Creators 2021-10 9:10](https://youtu.be/1stxDQeHhr8?t=550), [Roblox Creators 2021-10 13:29](https://youtu.be/1stxDQeHhr8?t=809). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Design season passes as a meta layer** (multiple-creators): Players earn pass XP by doing the normal game. Start around 10 tiers, release missions daily, use a non-linear XP curve, require claiming rewards, keep missions manageable, and make the pass visible. Sources: [Roblox Creators 2021-09 1:11](https://youtu.be/k2gbQI-ggB4?t=71), [Roblox Creators 2021-09 31:29](https://youtu.be/k2gbQI-ggB4?t=1889), [Roblox Creators 2021-09 37:11](https://youtu.be/k2gbQI-ggB4?t=2231), [Roblox Creators 2021-09 52:32](https://youtu.be/k2gbQI-ggB4?t=3152), [Roblox Creators 2021-09 1:59:59](https://youtu.be/k2gbQI-ggB4?t=7199). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Sustain play with evergreen, replayable content** (multiple-creators): Add replayability via leaderboards, daily challenges, modes, rounds, varied opponents, map voting, user-made content and random generation. Don't race to out-produce content. Release 2-3 playable items per update; major updates change the loop. Fix unpopular features within a week. Verify new content changes behaviour. Sources: [Roblox Creators 2021-08 3:10](https://youtu.be/pSjFYDEgRxs?t=190), [Roblox Creators 2022-01 4:02](https://youtu.be/oV8DKuAjQQs?t=242), [Roblox Creators 2021-07 2:10](https://youtu.be/Gv5OVVyWM10?t=130), [Roblox Creators 2021-07 37:10](https://youtu.be/Gv5OVVyWM10?t=2230), [AlvinBlox 2025-04 1:25](https://youtu.be/Le4QhC3dDGk?t=85). Newest source 2026-06-30.
- **Balance classes, weapons and options; protect newcomers** (multiple-creators): Give each weapon or class a distinct niche, compare purposes in a document, and avoid dominant options and solved strategies. Counter matchups aren't balance problems. Don't let new players face maxed players; guarantee a success floor. Add stagger, stamina, guard break and risky parries in combat. Avoid power creep. Sources: [Roblox Creators 2021-12 27:30](https://youtu.be/XTZxQvh1qkc?t=1650), [Roblox Creators 2022-01 58:56](https://youtu.be/oV8DKuAjQQs?t=3536), [Roblox Creators 2022-11 55:00](https://youtu.be/xXIc-p9hatU?t=3300), [Roblox Creators 2022-12 1:30](https://youtu.be/bEEd75mnBIg?t=90), [Roblox 2026-10 21:46](https://youtu.be/qLP05zvMsW0?t=1306). Newest source 2026-10-30.
- **Keep travel short, levels cohesive and readable** (multiple-creators): Keep points of interest within about 10 seconds' walk, offer fast travel after first exploration, start worlds small, plan layout around core mechanics, use colour and light to guide players, and make each area distinct. Sources: [GnomeCode 2021-06 20:52](https://youtu.be/0rMO-VHSuGs?t=1252), [GnomeCode 2021-06 23:38](https://youtu.be/0rMO-VHSuGs?t=1418), [Roblox 2026-10 23:52](https://youtu.be/qLP05zvMsW0?t=1432), [Roblox Creators 2022-03 54:58](https://youtu.be/czVxazdl0kg?t=3298), [Roblox 2024-09 12:13](https://youtu.be/vWx8UTxsi0A?t=733). Newest source 2026-10-12.
- **Test with real players and prioritise fun over polish** (multiple-creators): Watch players for 5 minutes each, play your own game for long stretches, and focus on why players leave. Verify features help (a sprint button cut D1) and A/B test by age. Fun beats graphics, clean code and promotion; kids forgive mid builds if the game is playable. Sources: [Roblox Creators 2021-11 4:32](https://youtu.be/rVr0IyfYkPg?t=272), [Roblox Creators 2021-11 4:32](https://youtu.be/rVr0IyfYkPg?t=272), [MoseyTube 2026-07 3:02](https://youtu.be/-PoyiiGhoNo?t=182), [Roblox DevForum 2024-08](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-actually-make-a-popular-game/3115791), [Roblox DevForum 2024-08](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-actually-make-a-popular-game/3115791). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Design for 10+ minute sessions, mobile-first, with accessibility** (multiple-creators): Self-reported: 10-12 min minimum at launch, under 5 minutes means boring; Active Spenders need 10 min/day (official). Mobile can be 70-80% of players: use one scaling UI and contextual HUD. Offer audio controls, rebindable keys, camera shake sliders and avoid fine dexterity demands. Sources: [VuukStudios 2026-06 4:15](https://youtu.be/catPKgFIZbQ?t=255), [VuukStudios 2026-07 8:28](https://youtu.be/Oo3sCq-TQXs?t=508), [rasdev 2026-04 2:01](https://youtu.be/G15TVsyC61g?t=121), [Roblox DevForum 2026-02](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/new-analytics-dimensions-get-insight-into-your-players%E2%80%99-spending-behaviors/4438963), [rasdev 2026-04 2:01](https://youtu.be/G15TVsyC61g?t=121). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Copy proven loops, but differentiate** (multiple-creators): Borrow proven mechanics, study top games and play several genre games first, but change key elements and add a unique feature. Most simulator hits share leave base, get a thing, return to base. Build tycoon/simulator loops as currency, multiplier, spend, repeat with a tight feedback cycle. Sources: [Tizzy 2026-06 3:46](https://youtu.be/zoz3PSMqets?t=226), [Tizzy 2026-06 8:58](https://youtu.be/4ms4cp2mrtM?t=538), [Duckable 2026-07 1:21](https://youtu.be/eRhFPgj8tc4?t=81), [Tizzy 2026-09 2:39](https://youtu.be/CRCcsYEB_6A?t=159), [TheDevKing 2020-05 16:23](https://youtu.be/AZTDrfuk9ME?t=983). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Offer a clear fantasy; weigh monetisation against engagement** (multiple-creators): Answer why do I care, what is the setting and what am I grinding at; RPGs need an objective from the start. Engagement first; pay-to-win irritates older players; give earned-currency alternatives to Robux; never require Robux for missions; place rewarded ads at natural breaks. Sources: [Ventures 2025-12 9:54](https://youtu.be/Nb50UhXcpRM?t=594), [VuukStudios 2026-09 11:34](https://youtu.be/2Q4NqQikzsY?t=694), [Cumin 2025-12 1:54](https://youtu.be/hQpC_UHxJDU?t=114), [VuukStudios 2026-10 8:05](https://youtu.be/4vuXAQt0PuQ?t=485), [VuukStudios 2026-10 8:05](https://youtu.be/4vuXAQt0PuQ?t=485). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Use timers, restocks, events and clean round structure** (multiple-creators): Time-based restocks create FOMO, scheduled events and shared global timers add urgency. Round games: intermission, prepare, match, end status, cleanup, reset; show the objective, send defeated players to a lobby, give ongoing rewards, and matchmake fast. Sources: [Tizzy 2026-09 6:04](https://youtu.be/CRCcsYEB_6A?t=364), [waveshine 2024-10 1:19](https://youtu.be/zW0VdnOqxMk?t=79), [RoWatcher (tracker blog) 2026-02](https://rowatcher.com/news/the-brainrot-invasion-how-absurd-meme-games-conquered-roblox-s-charts), [AlvinBlox 2020-06 0:22](https://youtu.be/spFZ44yqIII?t=22), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/battle-royale-series/cleanup-and-reset). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Tune movement feel; avoid friction, soft-locks and config rigidity** (multiple-creators): Use hold-to-jump, GroundController friction and acceleration, trigger actions on input Begin, cap speed, and don't gut traversal. Avoid one-way travel that strands players, soft-locks and RNG-gated build abilities. Only add durability/hunger bars if they create decisions. Keep constants in configs to tune live. Sources: [TheDankDeveloper 2025-12 9:17](https://youtu.be/D6Ej1CgiWOc?t=557), [Roblox DevForum 2026-02](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/studio-beta-avatarabilities-library-for-a-luau-based-character-controller/4403904), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/characters/character-controller-library/quick-start), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/curriculums/gameplay-scripting/implement-blasters), [ArTheDev 2025-10 22:02](https://youtu.be/KaCj7Jld35Q?t=1322). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Restrict dynamic behaviour to defined events and an allowlist of actions** (one-creator-repeated): Dynamic behaviour should trigger only on events you define, and it should be limited to a set of actions you explicitly allow. This keeps automated actors predictable and prevents them from changing game state in unplanned ways. Sources: [Roblox 2026-10 3:47](https://youtu.be/QvNAMmGqdd4?t=227). Newest source 2026-10-12.
- **Keep core game systems in your own code** (one-creator-repeated): Health, combat, inventory and rewards should stay in your own scripted logic rather than being handed to dynamic or automated behaviour. Core loop outcomes then remain deterministic and under your control. Sources: [Roblox 2026-10 3:47](https://youtu.be/QvNAMmGqdd4?t=227), [Roblox 2026-10 3:47](https://youtu.be/QvNAMmGqdd4?t=227). Newest source 2026-10-12.
- **Use dynamic behaviour as an edge-case fallback, not the default** (single-mention): Dynamic behaviour should only step in when situations become unusual. The designer keeps full control over normal play, so automation supports the loop rather than replacing the player's decisions. Sources: [Roblox 2026-10 3:47](https://youtu.be/QvNAMmGqdd4?t=227). Newest source 2026-10-12.
- **Match the AI model to the task** (single-mention): Use the human-like model for quick reactions. For longer-range planning or simple puzzles, start with behaviour-tree logic and only add human-like agents if behaviour trees are not enough. Sources: [Roblox 2026-10 21:21](https://youtu.be/QvNAMmGqdd4?t=1281). Newest source 2026-10-12.
- **Gate new zones behind speed tiers (treadmills)** (one-creator-repeated): Make each speed upgrade unlock a visibly new area, so every upgrade expands reach. Mulch's first world ran on speed thresholds (about 500M speed to reach base, 1B to reach the end of world one, 2B still insufficient). Tier costs climbed from 25B platinum to 5T and 50T. Re-check thresholds against income as it grows. Sources: [Mulch 2026-08 0:40](https://youtu.be/heoowJH9hso?t=40), [Mulch 2026-09 1:36](https://youtu.be/U2IjSOyNTB8?t=96), [Mulch 2026-08 0:00](https://youtu.be/heoowJH9hso?t=0), [Mulch 2026-08 1:21](https://youtu.be/heoowJH9hso?t=81). Newest source 2026-09-09.
- **Rebirth resets speed, keeps currency, and grants a multiplier** (one-creator-repeated): Mulch's rebirth keeps cash but resets speed, and gives a cash multiplier. Buying speed straight after rebirth makes the next progression step faster. This is a concrete way to make rebirth feel like a restart with a payoff, not a wipe. Sources: [Mulch 2026-08 4:04](https://youtu.be/heoowJH9hso?t=244), [Mulch 2026-08 3:24](https://youtu.be/heoowJH9hso?t=204). Newest source 2026-08-02.
- **Solo-test the full loop end to end before adding features** (multiple-creators): Play the loop yourself from start to finish (for example plant, grow, harvest, sell). If one player can finish the whole loop alone, fix that before adding stealing, combat, or other extras. Two indie devs reached this independently. Sources: [MonarchDev 2026-10 2:32](https://youtu.be/hPWJlFwp5Xs?t=152), [JustDucksey 2026-08 1:21](https://youtu.be/kl933X0UCC0?t=81). Newest source 2026-10-16.
- **Make sure there is always something to do** (one-creator-repeated): A loop with no missions or pending goals fails the 'is there anything to do' test. MonarchDev's top generator lost players because it had no missions. Give the loop a clear sequence (collect, process, upgrade, reward) and a next goal at every point. Sources: [MonarchDev 2026-10 12:31](https://youtu.be/3bUysm7IFxg?t=751), [MonarchDev 2026-08 1:22](https://youtu.be/jX3E9OHBeTg?t=82). Newest source 2026-10-21.
- **Scale guards and threat with each new area** (one-creator-repeated): If guards stay slow as players progress, fast players trivialise the game and the 'get all the sheep' problem appears. LovJak added guards per new area and raised difficulty to protect replayability. Sources: [LovJak 2026-10 2:46](https://youtu.be/YXcV0Ba0RJc?t=166), [LovJak 2026-10 2:46](https://youtu.be/YXcV0Ba0RJc?t=166). Newest source 2026-10-15.
- **Playtest progression speed and add gates when players blow through content** (single-mention): If playtesters rush through, add an unlock gate early. LovJak found progression felt too fast and added one before launch. Tune pacing during development, not after players have finished the content. Sources: [LovJak 2026-10 2:04](https://youtu.be/YXcV0Ba0RJc?t=124). Newest source 2026-10-15.
- **Keep rare items scarce and layer rarity modifiers on common outputs** (multiple-creators): Making every stand or item equally obtainable through an update killed Cumin's economy and collecting motivation. Add modifiers like gold or rainbow to common crops so harvests feel worth stealing and selling. Rarity only motivates if it stays hard to get. Sources: [Cumin 2026-08 16:12](https://youtu.be/xtuuL-wRFho?t=972), [JustDucksey 2026-08 4:45](https://youtu.be/kl933X0UCC0?t=285). Newest source 2026-08-10.
- **Don't let a secondary mini-game become the main income** (single-mention): Cumin's fishing mini-game became the main income and then dominated playtime, pushing out the core loop. Keep the mini-game as a side activity, and keep the main income tied to the core loop. Sources: [Cumin 2026-08 10:06](https://youtu.be/xtuuL-wRFho?t=606). Newest source 2026-08-10.
- **Check the loop has more than one optimal strategy** (single-mention): If the only optimal play is filling inventory to max and depositing, the loop has no decisions. Cumin's collect-and-deposit loop had that problem. Add trade-offs (route, risk, timing) so players must choose. Sources: [Cumin 2026-08 4:17](https://youtu.be/rjPW4GeeyFg?t=257). Newest source 2026-08-22.
- **Give optional content a reward that justifies its time cost** (single-mention): Optional puzzle rooms need payoff that matches the effort. A few seconds of timer gain for a long puzzle is not worth it. Compare reward value to expected time before shipping optional content. Sources: [Cumin 2026-08 5:23](https://youtu.be/rjPW4GeeyFg?t=323). Newest source 2026-08-22.
- **Cut padded segments and don't force full restarts on one miss** (single-mention): Shorten any shooting or challenge section that drags on. A single missed target should not restart the whole section. Cumin's feedback was that padding and full restarts kill the loop's momentum. Sources: [Cumin 2026-08 8:22](https://youtu.be/rjPW4GeeyFg?t=502). Newest source 2026-08-22.
- **Respawn players at spawn after death, not at last save** (single-mention): SimTek Game Development respawns players at spawn so they are not dropped back into the danger that killed them. Use spawn as the default respawn point. Sources: [SimTek Game Development 2026-08 14:29](https://youtu.be/9sTCUPeaJqM?t=869). Newest source 2026-08-22.
- **Add sell-all and equip-best buttons for large inventories** (single-mention): Players with big inventories need quick management. Mulch added a sell-all button and an equip-best button so players can manage large inventories without tedious manual steps. Sources: [Mulch 2026-09 12:06](https://youtu.be/U2IjSOyNTB8?t=726). Newest source 2026-09-09.
- **Prioritise long-engagement systems over cosmetics** (single-mention): Put content budget into systems with lasting engagement, such as mastery, exploration, and weapon progression. Cosmetics add no gameplay loop and are a weak use of budget. Sources: [Deconstructor of Fun 2026-10 55:31](https://youtu.be/oU3oz6Ddz7g?t=3331). Newest source 2026-10-22.
- **Plan timer-based growth systems early** (single-mention): Multi-harvest crops (cactus, pineapple) need a separate growth system. Plan for them during the core loop design rather than bolting them on later. Sources: [JustDucksey 2026-08 5:27](https://youtu.be/kl933X0UCC0?t=327). Newest source 2026-08-09.
- **Give every pressure mechanic a real cost** (multiple-creators): A mechanic that adds tension needs a drain or consequence. Sprint drains stamina and punishes at zero (death). Darkness with no danger loses pressure. Movement-based spill chance rises with speed or stepping. Raw food hurts but restores more hunger than it costs. Sources: [GnomeCode 2025-02 11:26](https://youtu.be/-oZIdFe8qHk?t=686), [SimTek Game Development 2025-09 0:40](https://youtu.be/EQqd8Xk3Wkk?t=40), [BloxxedDev 2025-03 2:04](https://youtu.be/y5aNv16bT3s?t=124), [JustDucksey 2025-07 4:11](https://youtu.be/s5nooZlK5Pc?t=251), [SimTek Game Development 2025-09 27:36](https://youtu.be/EQqd8Xk3Wkk?t=1656). Newest source 2025-09-27.
- **Tie resource drains to the loop's own activity** (one-creator-repeated): Make the drain something the core loop produces, so players must keep harvesting to keep progressing. Example: crops are fuel for a machine that drains over time. A campfire that loses fuel every second keeps the gathering loop running. Sources: [JustDucksey 2025-06 2:04](https://youtu.be/XRvcKGSLr4g?t=124), [JustDucksey 2025-07 1:23](https://youtu.be/s5nooZlK5Pc?t=83). Newest source 2025-07-27.
- **Use a capacity cap with a paid storage upgrade** (single-mention): A storage cap forces periodic sell trips, and a paid upgrade makes the cap a gold sink tied to collect-sell-upgrade. Demo value was 10 gold for +5 storage, later changed to 10→15, so treat numbers as tunable. Sources: [Suphi Kaner 2022-06 36:01](https://youtu.be/B446FyN1xtc?t=2161), [Suphi Kaner 2022-06 38:44](https://youtu.be/B446FyN1xtc?t=2324). Newest source 2022-06-30.
- **Balance threats with relief pickups and targeted pressure** (one-creator-repeated): Add pickups (speed boost, stamina, health, tokens) so players get relief between threats. Punish edge camping with threats aimed at the camper so players keep moving and the map stays dynamic. Sources: [Roox4 2023-11 4:27](https://youtu.be/kKiPL7wc8Rc?t=267), [Roox4 2023-11 6:26](https://youtu.be/kKiPL7wc8Rc?t=386). Newest source 2023-11-26.
- **Spawn threats near players to reduce luck spikes** (single-mention): Random spawns far from players created the main difficulty spike in one release. Targeting spawns near players made difficulty less luck-dependent. Use this when a spawn roll can make a round unwinnable. Sources: [Roox4 2023-11 6:42](https://youtu.be/kKiPL7wc8Rc?t=402). Newest source 2023-11-26.
- **Tie upgrades to a story motive** (one-creator-repeated): When progression is framed as helping a character, upgrades create emotional attachment to the core loop. The tycoon should resemble or matter to the character, not just be a number ladder. Sources: [ByteBlox 2025-01 16:52](https://youtu.be/mpv1SyfcpGA?t=1012), [ByteBlox 2025-01 0:30](https://youtu.be/mpv1SyfcpGA?t=30). Newest source 2025-01-31.
- **Vary rooms and keep exploration under constant threat** (one-creator-repeated): Identical rooms and fixed item spawns make exploration feel repetitive. Avoid minutes-long searching for keys. Keep threat constant during exploration instead of padding with drawer-opening. Sources: [GnomeCode 2025-02 3:57](https://youtu.be/-oZIdFe8qHk?t=237), [GnomeCode 2025-02 8:51](https://youtu.be/-oZIdFe8qHk?t=531), [GnomeCode 2025-02 10:13](https://youtu.be/-oZIdFe8qHk?t=613). Newest source 2025-02-22.
- **Add a guiding system to the next objective** (single-mention): Players stall when they don't know what to do next. A guiding system (e.g., a marker or clear next step) acts as the missing key that keeps the loop moving. Sources: [Roox4 2023-11 6:42](https://youtu.be/kKiPL7wc8Rc?t=402). Newest source 2023-11-26.
- **In horror, drive pressure with sanity or speed** (multiple-creators): Use a sanity meter as the core mechanic, with blur and colour shifts instead of monsters, to tie fear to the player's mind. Fast movement and speedrun-style timing can make horror more replayable than slow creeping. Sources: [GnomeCode 2025-02 5:27](https://youtu.be/-oZIdFe8qHk?t=327), [BloxxedDev 2025-03 4:37](https://youtu.be/CipKM4DBWxg?t=277). Newest source 2025-03-09.
- **Tick status effects at the end of the affected unit's turn** (one-creator-repeated): Start-of-turn ticking can kill or waste buffs before the player acts. Ticking at end of turn is the expected default. Example: a 3-stack buff lost a turn to start-of-turn tick and became effectively 2. Sources: [Cumin 2026-06 11:26](https://youtu.be/2IdxQNOGoqA?t=686), [Cumin 2026-06 11:26](https://youtu.be/2IdxQNOGoqA?t=686), [Cumin 2026-06 11:26](https://youtu.be/2IdxQNOGoqA?t=686). Newest source 2026-06-24.
- **Don't stack energy costs and cooldowns on one action** (one-creator-repeated): Using both an energy cost and a cooldown on the same action makes players wait without choosing. Use one time-based gate per action so turns aren't wasted. Reviewer example: one ability cost 4 turns and 5 energy. Sources: [Cumin 2026-06 12:06](https://youtu.be/2IdxQNOGoqA?t=726), [Cumin 2026-06 12:06](https://youtu.be/2IdxQNOGoqA?t=726). Newest source 2026-06-24.
- **Give combat a persistent resource or varied strategies** (one-creator-repeated): Without a persistent resource or distinct strategies, every battle opens the same way. Give players a resource they manage across fights, and make a defensive stance a real trade-off (example: +1 energy for 30% more damage taken). Sources: [Cumin 2026-06 12:47](https://youtu.be/2IdxQNOGoqA?t=767), [Cumin 2026-06 10:45](https://youtu.be/2IdxQNOGoqA?t=645). Newest source 2026-06-24.
- **Make every loss traceable to a decision** (single-mention): A death should feel like 'that attack cost me the run', not attrition. Design so the player can see which choice caused the loss. Sources: [Cumin 2026-06 13:28](https://youtu.be/2IdxQNOGoqA?t=808). Newest source 2026-06-24.
- **Make bosses change mechanics, not just HP** (single-mention): Bosses that differ only by higher HP repeat basic encounters. Give bosses new mechanics so the fight asks different decisions. Sources: [Cumin 2026-06 14:48](https://youtu.be/2IdxQNOGoqA?t=888). Newest source 2026-06-24.
- **Cut turn-based downtime with team-wide turns** (one-creator-repeated): One turn per player plus one per enemy makes long multiplayer sessions feel empty. Have all players act together, then all enemies, or let everyone queue actions visible to the team and resolve by turn order. Sources: [Cumin 2026-06 16:12](https://youtu.be/2IdxQNOGoqA?t=972), [Cumin 2026-06 17:34](https://youtu.be/2IdxQNOGoqA?t=1054), [Cumin 2026-06 18:16](https://youtu.be/2IdxQNOGoqA?t=1096), [Cumin 2026-06 16:12](https://youtu.be/2IdxQNOGoqA?t=972). Newest source 2026-06-24.
- **Let players sample content before selling a la carte unlocks** (single-mention): Let players try each content type free first, then offer separate purchases of the types they enjoy. This avoids grind walls and pay-to-win framing. Creator reports this as a monetisation approach; no revenue figures given. Sources: [GameMakers 2022-11 10:11](https://youtu.be/NaR3-VIg5-I?t=611). Newest source 2022-11-24.
- **Let community desire guide cosmetics and difficulty** (multiple-creators): Choose which cosmetics to release based on what the community asks for; items the community ignores sell noticeably worse (GameMakers, no figure). Similarly, retune difficulty from player feedback: janisjanis01 buffed rods after players said the game was too hard. Sources: [GameMakers 2024-08 13:13](https://youtu.be/gp_hDijjC4U?t=793), [janisjanis01 2025-11 15:13](https://youtu.be/iEvgIId-8Uw?t=913). Newest source 2025-11-23.
- **Replace passive walk-and-collect with active interaction** (one-creator-repeated): Walking around collecting drops was boring. janisjanis01 replaced it with a click-to-damage-then-collect step and called it a huge upgrade (self-reported, 2025-01). Keep the player's input inside the loop rather than letting collection run passively. Sources: [janisjanis01 2025-01 9:15](https://youtu.be/EpPU3Am8lgE?t=555), [janisjanis01 2025-01 9:15](https://youtu.be/EpPU3Am8lgE?t=555). Newest source 2025-01-05.
- **Give each paid upgrade tier two benefits** (single-mention): Tie progression to purchasable bags that raise both strength and a Robux multiplier, giving a clear upgrade path. janisjanis01 added 3 bags to the original 5 (8 total) to balance progression. Pairs well with a capacity cap plus paid storage upgrade. Sources: [janisjanis01 2025-01 9:15](https://youtu.be/EpPU3Am8lgE?t=555), [janisjanis01 2025-01 14:53](https://youtu.be/EpPU3Am8lgE?t=893). Newest source 2025-01-05.
- **Expect every new power to be overpowered; schedule a nerf pass** (one-creator-repeated): janisjanis01 found each new tower or power was overpowered on first pass. Plan a nerf pass before wider release rather than treating balance as one-off. Playtest the power immediately after adding it. Sources: [janisjanis01 2026-10 6:52](https://youtu.be/A2QD-eT3IG0?t=412), [janisjanis01 2026-10 7:34](https://youtu.be/A2QD-eT3IG0?t=454). Newest source 2026-10-25.
- **Test stats that scale off other entities when those entities are absent** (single-mention): A mortar scaled off base DPS but dealt zero damage when no turrets were present. Any bonus that references another object needs a check in the zero-object state, or it silently fails. Sources: [janisjanis01 2026-10 6:52](https://youtu.be/A2QD-eT3IG0?t=412). Newest source 2026-10-25.
- **Keep older content relevant through scaling** (single-mention): Late-game units such as swords and basic blocks became pointless after certain waves. Add upgrade or scaling paths so earlier content stays useful as threats grow. Sources: [janisjanis01 2026-10 8:15](https://youtu.be/A2QD-eT3IG0?t=495). Newest source 2026-10-25.
- **Ship cheap QoL shortcuts early** (single-mention): A back-to-plot button had an outsized impact on the experience, according to janisjanis01. Small navigation shortcuts can beat new content for perceived quality; add them during early playtests. Sources: [janisjanis01 2026-10 7:34](https://youtu.be/A2QD-eT3IG0?t=454). Newest source 2026-10-25.
- **Verify drop and egg odds in play after every edit** (single-mention): A config mistake showed Common at 3% and Legendary at 55%. After editing weights, sample the drops in a test session and compare the observed rates to the intended table before shipping. Sources: [janisjanis01 2024-04 5:10](https://youtu.be/7z8XlhAtGpQ?t=310), [janisjanis01 2025-06 4:17](https://youtu.be/W9JYImC0FBg?t=257). Newest source 2025-06-15.
- **Balance resource drain against how easily the resource is found** (single-mention): A hunger system drained faster than players could find food, causing early deaths. Sprinting doubled the drain. Compare time-to-empty with time-to-find for each drained resource, and make sure the loop's own activity restores it. Sources: [janisjanis01 2025-10 15:56](https://youtu.be/MML_z-jpmF4?t=956), [janisjanis01 2025-10 3:36](https://youtu.be/MML_z-jpmF4?t=216). Newest source 2025-10-11.
- **Remove loading screens and waits to keep players in flow** (single-mention): Pulling players out of the loop with loading screens breaks flow. Use instant matchmaking and seamless loops so the next action starts immediately. Sources: [rasdev 2026-04 2:41](https://youtu.be/yFKY2FlwHjE?t=161). Newest source 2026-04-30.
- **Pick a core mechanic that is explainable in one line and fun to watch** (multiple-creators): Choose a mechanic that is simple to explain and enjoyable to watch, such as blending items, and build the loop around it. Validate it before adding any hooks. Sources: [janisjanis01 2025-06 0:50](https://youtu.be/W9JYImC0FBg?t=50), [rasdev 2026-04 3:23](https://youtu.be/yFKY2FlwHjE?t=203), [rasdev 2026-04 3:23](https://youtu.be/yFKY2FlwHjE?t=203). Newest source 2026-04-30.
- **Layer simulator depth with exploration, codes and rare collectibles** (single-mention): The sequel kept players engaged with map exploration, secret codes, rare pets and a currency that buys gamepasses. Add these layers on top of the basic rising number. Sources: [janisjanis01 2024-10 5:07](https://youtu.be/ZyQ73kfh_b4?t=307). Newest source 2024-10-27.
- **Check multiplicative stacking at extreme counts** (single-mention): A rebirth multiplier that looked fine early was too strong at 400,000 rebirths, where small changes compound. The creator trimmed it by 0.01. Test the multiplier at maximum expected counts before release. Sources: [janisjanis01 2024-06 0:43](https://youtu.be/JeyYzEjPjc8?t=43), [janisjanis01 2024-06 0:43](https://youtu.be/JeyYzEjPjc8?t=43), [janisjanis01 2024-06 0:43](https://youtu.be/JeyYzEjPjc8?t=43). Newest source 2024-06-30.
- **Spawn timed items by cloning templates in a loop** (single-mention): Run a while loop with a 2-second wait, pick a random item from a template list, and clone it into a dedicated spawned folder. Eggs dropped from 20 studs above random spawn points worked as a tutorial loop. Keeping spawns in one folder makes cleanup and counting easy. Sources: [AlvinBlox 2020-04 19:00](https://youtu.be/DhS5oexaE3w?t=1140), [AlvinBlox 2020-04 14:13](https://youtu.be/DhS5oexaE3w?t=853), [AlvinBlox 2020-04 20:22](https://youtu.be/DhS5oexaE3w?t=1222). Newest source 2020-04-05.
- **Give endless wave loops a terminal game-over condition** (single-mention): A wave or spawn loop with no end state keeps spawning forever. Fire a game-over event when base health reaches 0 or less, and break the loop so the round actually ends. Pair this with the existing round-structure rule. Sources: [GnomeCode 2022-01 22:47](https://youtu.be/de5-o6euwJE?t=1367). Newest source 2022-01-04.
- **Use build-to-earn loops for very small maps** (single-mention): Placing buildings to earn money and attract citizens gave a clear core loop even on a tiny map. Small spaces can carry a full loop if the loop is about placement and growth rather than map size. Sources: [GnomeCode 2020-06 40:21](https://youtu.be/Z74HM6xcsBE?t=2421). Newest source 2020-06-26.
- **Use a key hunt to gate areas in small spaces** (single-mention): A structure where players find the right key to unlock areas gives a clear progression goal in a small map. It is an alternative to distance-based zone gating when space is limited. Sources: [GnomeCode 2020-06 36:14](https://youtu.be/Z74HM6xcsBE?t=2174). Newest source 2020-06-26.
- **Store room and content metadata in a keyed data table** (single-mention): Keep room direction, stairs and weight in a RoomInfo ModuleScript table keyed by room name. Rules become data-driven, so adding or tuning a room means editing data, not logic. Sources: [GnomeCode 2022-10 8:07](https://youtu.be/QLoF9Er8Oo8?t=487). Newest source 2022-10-08.
- **Pick weighted items with a cumulative-sum roll and give every entry a weight** (one-creator-repeated): Sum all integer weights, roll math.random between 1 and the total, then walk the table accumulating weight until the roll is met. Every entry needs a weight value, even a 0 for the start room, or the total breaks. Sources: [GnomeCode 2022-10 16:15](https://youtu.be/QLoF9Er8Oo8?t=975), [GnomeCode 2022-10 17:37](https://youtu.be/QLoF9Er8Oo8?t=1057), [GnomeCode 2022-10 16:55](https://youtu.be/QLoF9Er8Oo8?t=1015). Newest source 2022-10-08.
- **Test generated layouts at scale for known loop bugs** (single-mention): Generate many procedural layouts before shipping. One GnomeCode run tested 100 generated rooms for a corner-loop bug. Random generation can produce rare failure shapes that a few manual builds will not reveal. Sources: [GnomeCode 2022-10 0:00](https://youtu.be/QLoF9Er8Oo8?t=0). Newest source 2022-10-08.

## Checklist
- [ ] Write the core loop as action, reward, repeat plus a progression engine; confirm it is explainable in one sentence/30 seconds.
- [ ] Prototype with greybox parts and playtest the loop before building other systems.
- [ ] Check each proposed system answers 'how does this feed the loop?'; cut it otherwise.
- [ ] Add instant feedback (sound, VFX, number pop-ups, orbs) on every core action.
- [ ] Define short, mid and long-term goals and show the long-term goal early.
- [ ] Make the first session rewarding: low early XP thresholds, an unlock every few minutes.
- [ ] Log unlock timestamps on a fresh alt account to find walls.
- [ ] Add visible progression (XP bar, multipliers, rebirth/prestige).
- [ ] Plan sources and sinks; add caps, drains and event currency.
- [ ] Keep rarity weights and prices in a config module; roll from rarest first.
- [ ] Add a social element and a solo/empty-server path.
- [ ] Add risk/reward (steal, cash-out, safe zone) where it fits the loop.
- [ ] Release 2-3 playable items per update; check new content changes behaviour.
- [ ] Design the UI mobile-first with a single scaling UI.
- [ ] Offer accessibility options (volume, keybinds, shake slider).
- [ ] Tune quests/dailies to fit within a session; no Robux-gated tasks.
- [ ] Playtest to measure average session length (aim above 10 minutes).
- [ ] Check drop-off per checkpoint/funnel step and fix the largest.
- [ ] Watch real players for 5 minutes to find boring or frustrating moments.
- [ ] Verify abilities, objectives and puzzles work and cannot be exploited.
- [ ] Define the exact events that can trigger any dynamic or automated behaviour.
- [ ] Create an allowlist of actions that automated actors may perform and block everything else.
- [ ] Keep health, combat, inventory and reward logic in your own deterministic game code.
- [ ] Reserve dynamic behaviour for unusual or edge-case situations; keep normal play under designer control.
- [ ] For quick reactions (e.g. dodging, responding to the player), use the human-like agent model.
- [ ] For longer-range planning or simple puzzles, start with behaviour-tree logic before adding human-like agents.
- [ ] Test automated actors against the allowlist to confirm they cannot change core systems.
- [ ] Solo-test the loop end to end; if one player can finish it alone, fix that before adding features.
- [ ] Confirm the loop always has a next goal or pending mission.
- [ ] Gate each new zone with a speed or progression tier that the player can see.
- [ ] Check thresholds against current income, not launch-day income.
- [ ] Confirm rebirth keeps currency, resets speed, and grants a multiplier.
- [ ] Scale guard count and threat with each new area.
- [ ] Playtest progression speed; add an unlock gate if players finish content too quickly.
- [ ] Keep rare items hard to obtain; add rarity modifiers to common outputs.
- [ ] Keep mini-games secondary; main income stays in the core loop.
- [ ] Confirm the loop has more than one optimal strategy.
- [ ] Match optional reward value to the time cost of the optional content.
- [ ] Shorten padded segments; don't restart the whole section after one miss.
- [ ] Use spawn as the default respawn point after death.
- [ ] Add sell-all and equip-best buttons for large inventories.
- [ ] Put budget into long-engagement systems before cosmetics.
- [ ] Plan growth-timer systems (multi-harvest crops) during core design.
- [ ] When taking camera control for a custom or cinematic view, set Camera.CameraType to Scriptable, and set it back to Custom when done (SimTek, T17883).
- [ ] Describe how each key button should feel before building it; a single press with no feedback is boring (MonarchDev, Q1832).
- [ ] Before shipping any pressure mechanic, write down its cost and what happens at zero.
- [ ] Pair fuel or hunger drains with the harvest loop so gathering keeps progression moving.
- [ ] Add a storage cap and a paid upgrade so players must sell, creating a gold sink.
- [ ] Add relief pickups between threat waves.
- [ ] Target threats at players who camp edges so the map stays dynamic.
- [ ] If spawn luck causes a difficulty spike, spawn threats near players.
- [ ] Give upgrades a story reason so progression feels like helping a character.
- [ ] Vary room layouts and item spawns; cut key-hunting padding.
- [ ] Show a guiding marker or clear next step when players stall.
- [ ] Turn-based: tick statuses and buffs at the end of the affected unit's turn.
- [ ] Turn-based: use one time-based gate per action; don't stack energy and cooldown.
- [ ] Turn-based: give players a persistent resource or distinct strategies.
- [ ] Turn-based: make bosses change mechanics, not just HP.
- [ ] Multiplayer turn-based: resolve team turns together, then enemies, with visible queued actions.
- [ ] Use a volume the player stands inside for trigger zones; standing-on-platform detection proved unreliable.
- [ ] Held items lose collision with the player; design stacking or standing on them explicitly.
- [ ] Offer a free sample of each content type before any a la carte paywall.
- [ ] Survey the community for cosmetic requests before release; deprioritise items they ignore.
- [ ] Replace walk-to-collect pickups with a click or interaction step inside the loop.
- [ ] Give each paid upgrade tier two benefits (for example strength and earnings multiplier) for a clear upgrade path.
- [ ] Assume every new power is overpowered; schedule a nerf pass before wider release.
- [ ] For any stat that scales off another object, test it with zero of that object present.
- [ ] Add upgrade or scaling paths so older units keep value in later waves.
- [ ] Add a return-to-base or equivalent shortcut during early playtests.
- [ ] Adjust difficulty from playtester feedback, not assumption.
- [ ] After editing any drop table or egg weight, sample real drops and compare them to the configured percentages.
- [ ] For every drained resource, measure time-to-empty against time-to-find and fix the gap if players run out first.
- [ ] Audit the loop for loading screens and waits between actions, and remove or shorten them.
- [ ] Confirm matchmaking and respawn are instant so the session stays in flow.
- [ ] Write a one-line pitch for the core mechanic and confirm it is fun to watch before adding systems.
- [ ] Test each multiplier at the highest expected count, such as 400,000 rebirths, to check it does not compound out of control.
- [ ] For simulator-style games, plan secret codes, exploration and a rare collectible tier beyond the main rising number.
- [ ] Spawn timed items with a loop and a fixed interval; start at 2 seconds and tune from playtests.
- [ ] Clone spawned items from a template list into one folder so cleanup and counting stay simple.
- [ ] Add a game-over trigger at health 0 or below and break the spawn or wave loop when it fires.
- [ ] Store room or content metadata in a ModuleScript table keyed by name, not hard-coded in logic.
- [ ] Give every weighted table entry a weight value, using 0 for entries that should never be random (e.g. start room).
- [ ] Generate at least 100 procedural layouts and check them for loops, dead ends and corner bugs before release.
- [ ] For small maps, consider a build-to-earn or key-hunt loop when distance-based zoning is not possible.

## Where creators agree
- Core loop must be fun first; monetisation, polish and visuals come after (Roblox Creators, Roblox, SmartyRBX, AlvinBlox, Last Level Studios, rasdev, Tizzy, bubDEV, Cumin).
- Layered short/mid/long-term goals with early quick rewards (Roblox Creators, Roblox DevForum, Stewiepfing, MoseyTube, VuukStudios, Roblox).
- Instant feedback and juice drive retention (SmartyRBX, Stewiepfing, AlvinBlox, Tizzy, Leif, ByteBlox, Roblox Creator Docs).
- Social play and player interaction raise retention; solo-only games struggle (Roblox Creator Docs, VuukStudios, rasdev, RoWatcher, Roblox).
- Simple core with hidden depth (Tizzy, MoseyTube, Roblox Creators, Stewiepfing, Daiorg).
- Rebirth/prestige as long-term progression (AlvinBlox, SmartyRBX, Mulch, Generalist Programmer, rasdev, 1_Bruce).
- Economy needs sinks and caps (Roblox Creators, Roblox Creator Docs, Game Maker's Toolkit).
- Don't gate on hard currency or Robux for missions (Roblox Creators, Roblox Creator Docs, PineappleDev).
- Several indie devs independently say to build and test the core loop end to end, solo, before extras (JustDucksey, MonarchDev, LovJak, Duckable).
- Mulch and LovJak both use gating (speed tiers, unlock gates) to control pacing and keep fast players from blowing through content.
- Cumin and JustDucksey both say rarity must stay meaningful, or the collecting motivation and economy break.
- Pressure mechanics need a real consequence or cost (GnomeCode, SimTek Game Development, BloxxedDev, JustDucksey).
- Constant threat or no dead time keeps play engaging (GnomeCode, Roox4, Cumin).
- Player and community feedback should steer cosmetics and difficulty tuning (GameMakers T18532; janisjanis01 T18602).
- Build and validate the core mechanic before adding psychology hooks or polish (rasdev, janisjanis01 on choosing a simple, fun core).
- Keep the core loop simple and enjoyable before layering systems on top (T18894 and T18939 / Q1968).

## Where creators disagree
- **Exponential/fast number progression versus honest progression**
  - MoseyTube and rasdev: keep progression fast, with huge numbers going up
  - ByteBlox: don't fake progression with x2/x3 costs when content is thin
  - CORSO: exponential costs that take months feel unwinnable
  - Roblox Creators: a non-linear XP curve is good, with early tiers rewarding quickly
- **Idle/auto mechanics**
  - Mulch, Roblox Creator Docs and RoWatcher: AFK/auto raises speed and session time
  - Duckable and Cumin: idle clickers and RNG feel unearned and hollow
  - Tizzy: auto-roll wrecked the economy
  - Leif: pure auto-clicking shouldn't be the optimal meta
- **How much to worry about difficulty**
  - GnomeCode: don't over-worry; keep next milestones
  - Roblox Creators and RoyalDev: keep it fair, never impossible
- **Hiding or showing rarity and goals**
  - Roblox: show far stronger gear and distant goals early
  - Lnor Roblox: hide rarity until reveal
  - RoWatcher: show '1 in X' odds
- **Convenience features such as sprint**
  - Roblox Creators: a sprint button cut D1 retention
  - ArTheDev and Roblox Creator Docs: sprint and dash tuning improve feel
  - Cumin: don't gut traversal in movement games
- **Update cadence**
  - Roblox Creators: weekly updates sustain metrics, major ones change the loop
  - Roblox: Piggy weekly chapters left little replay; sustainability matters more than frequency
- **Integer versus fractional weights**
  - GnomeCode (T19019) says use integer weights per item and sum them.
  - GnomeCode (T19019, N8192) also uses 0.1 weights to make a rare room very unlikely.
  - Fractional weights conflict with the integer roll in T19020; no source resolves this.

## Date context (what may be outdated)
- Older advice (2017-2018 DevForum and Roblox talks) covers combat and design-question basics; later 2021-2022 Roblox Creators talks formalise the core loop, tumors and goal tiers.
- Early 2020-2021 sources predate genre trends (Grow a Garden, Steal a Brainrot); 2025-2026 sources reflect those and show base-raiding, offline growth and restock loops. Visit counts and hit claims are self-reported and unverified.
- Roblox Creator Rewards (2025-06) counts only the first 10 minutes; 2026 official statements say the path is fun, repeatable loops and evergreen retention.
- Controller and Ability system APIs (2026) postdate older Humanoid-based movement tweaking.
- Indie drop rates, CCU and income claims are unverified.
- Many Creator Docs numbers are tutorial example values, not benchmarks.
- Official Roblox guidance on dynamic behaviour and AI agent choice is dated 2026-10, the most recent item in this batch (today is 2026-11-01). Treat it as current, and check for later Roblox documentation updates.
- No older advice in the existing file conflicts with these items, so no existing rule is superseded.
- All items are dated 2026-08 to 2026-10. Current date is 2026-11-01, so this advice is recent.
- Numbers are game-specific, self-reported, and tied to each creator's economy. They go stale fast and should be treated as examples, not benchmarks.
- No [OFFICIAL] items in this batch. The camera tip (CameraType Scriptable) is a Roblox API fact from a creator; verify against current official docs before relying on it.
- No new advice conflicts with the existing rules.
- Newest source is Cumin (2026-06); its end-of-turn status ticking and team-wide turn advice supersede default start-of-turn ticking for turn-based combat.
- Stamina and sprint values come from a 2025-09 comedic demo; tune rather than copy.
- Suphi Kaner's 2022-06 backpack values were changed in a later build (10→15 storage); treat as demo numbers.
- Roox4 (2023-11) and ByteBlox (2025-01) findings predate later engine and design changes; verify before relying on them.
- Newest guidance is janisjanis01's 2026-10 tower/power balance advice (T18726, T18727, T18729); prefer it when older 2025-01 items conflict.
- GameMakers items are 2022-11 and 2024-08; monetisation advice may predate current Roblox paid-content policies. Verify before applying.
- Highlight limit of 31 (N7956, 2025-11) is a platform constraint that can change; re-check before relying on it.
- Bag and Robux-multiplier design (T18556, 2025-01) and coin-walking replacement (T18555, 2025-01) predate the 2026 tower-defense items.
- janisjanis01 items date from 2024-03 to 2025-10 and include numbers tied to specific configs (egg odds, 0.01 rebirth nerf, 2x hunger drain). These may be outdated; treat them as examples, not defaults.
- rasdev items from 2026-04 and 2026-06 are the most recent. Where they conflict with older advice, prefer rasdev's flow-state and 10+ minute beginner guidance.
- Items tagged [low-advice] (T18758, T18765, T18927, N8049, N8051, N8052, N8060, N8137, Q1943) are weaker and should be checked against other sources.
- All new items date from 2020-04 to 2022-10, which is older than the current date (2026-11). Treat their API and tooling advice as needing verification against current Roblox and Luau practice.
- The examples use wait(2) (T18989). The legacy wait() call is generally superseded by task.wait in current Luau; this is outside-source knowledge, not from the creators.
- The integer-weight advice (T19019) and the 0.1 weights (N8192) conflict within the same creator's material; if you build on it, scale weights to integers (for example, multiply by 10) so the integer roll stays valid. This is inference, not a creator claim.
- No new items are marked [OFFICIAL]; all figures are self-reported indie examples.

## Instructions for an agent using this file
- If asked to design a game, first write the core loop as action, reward, upgrade, repeat, then prototype it with greybox before adding systems.
- If a proposed feature does not feed the core loop, recommend cutting it or reshaping it; if it creates a parallel loop or opt-out path, flag it as a tumor.
- If progression feels slow, suggest front-loading early rewards (unlock every few minutes, low early XP) and a goal within 10-15 minutes.
- If designing a simulator, tycoon or clicker, include currency, multiplier upgrades, rebirth, pets and rarity, and also add depth, skill or non-numeric challenge.
- If adding RNG, roll from the rarest tier, store weights in a config, and decide whether rarity is hidden until reveal.
- If the economy inflates, add sinks, caps or drains; use a separate event currency for events.
- If asked about quests or season passes, tie tasks to the core loop, avoid Robux-gated tasks, keep dailies inside one session, and release missions daily.
- If a game is solo or prone to empty servers, add AI/practice modes and social hooks (trading, visible progress, parties).
- If retention is low, fix core gameplay before monetisation and check funnel drop-offs; prefer official statements over indie self-reported metrics.
- If tuning movement or combat, adjust friction/acceleration, trigger on input Begin, telegraph attacks, and avoid solved strategies.
- If a design involves automated or AI-driven actors, then restrict them to defined events and an explicit allowlist of actions.
- If a core system (health, combat, inventory, rewards) is involved, then implement it in deterministic game code, not in dynamic behaviour.
- If an AI needs quick reactions, then use the human-like agent model.
- If an AI needs long-range planning or simple puzzle logic, then start with behaviour trees and add human-like agents only if needed.
- If a design relies on automation, then keep the designer in control of normal play and let automation act only in unusual situations.
- Cite only the official Roblox sources for these points; no indie creator evidence exists yet for this topic.
- If designing zones or areas, then gate each one behind a visible speed or progression tier, and check thresholds against current income.
- If a playtest shows players rushing content, then add an unlock gate before launch.
- If one solo tester can finish the whole loop alone, then fix that before adding any new system.
- If a loop has no pending missions or goals, then add one before shipping.
- If an optional activity has a reward that is small relative to its time cost, then raise the reward or cut the activity.
- If a secondary mini-game is taking over playtime or income, then move income back to the core loop.
- If the loop has only one optimal action (for example fill-to-max), then add a trade-off that creates real choice.
- If a player dies, then respawn at spawn by default unless the design needs otherwise.
- If inventories grow large, then add sell-all and equip-best buttons.
- If camera control is needed for a custom view, then set Camera.CameraType to Scriptable and restore Custom afterward.
- If a mechanic is meant to create pressure, verify it has a drain, cost, or consequence; if none, add one.
- If designing a sell loop, add a storage cap plus a paid upgrade to create a sink.
- If designing turn-based combat, tick statuses at end of the unit's turn and use one gate per action.
- If designing multiplayer turn-based combat, default to team-wide turns to cut downtime.
- If asked for numbers, present demo values as starting points and explain they are self-reported.
- If horror is the genre, prefer sanity or speed mechanics over monsters for pressure.
- If spawns cause unwinnable rounds, target spawns near players before tuning difficulty.
- If a design proposes a paywall, first check whether players can sample that content type for free.
- If you add a new power or stat, schedule a balance and nerf review in the next playtest cycle, not as optional follow-up.
- If a bonus references another object, test the feature with that object absent.
- If a mechanic is passive walk-and-collect, consider an active interaction step before adding more rewards.
- When tuning difficulty, prioritise playtester feedback over assumptions.
- Treat the indie figures (18.5k, 8 bags, 31 highlight limit) as examples, not benchmarks; they are self-reported.
- If the user edits a drop table or egg weight, remind them to verify observed rates in play before shipping.
- If a resource drains over time, compute time-to-empty versus time-to-find and flag any case where players run out first.
- If a multiplier compounds with a count such as rebirths, test it at the maximum expected count before recommending it.
- If the core loop is not yet fun, advise against adding psychology hooks, retention mechanics or polish.
- If the game shows loading screens between loop actions, recommend removing or shortening them to keep flow.
- Treat all indie figures as self-reported and avoid presenting them as benchmarks without that caveat.
- If a spawn or wave loop has no end state, add a game-over or termination trigger before adding other features.
- If you build a weighted random table, give every entry a numeric weight (use 0 for never-random entries) and use the cumulative-sum roll.
- If weights include fractions, convert them to integers before using an integer random roll; do not assume fractional totals work with math.random as written in the source.
- If the map is very small, prefer a build-to-earn or key-hunt progression before adding distance-based zoning.
- If you generate layouts procedurally, test at least 100 samples for loops and dead ends before accepting the generator.
- If you use legacy examples (wait(2), 2020-era tutorials), verify them against current API docs before copying.

## Not covered here (research elsewhere)
- No concrete official D1/D7/D30 retention benchmarks beyond examples.
- Little coverage of A/B testing methodology and analytics setup.
- Minimal depth on monetisation pricing.
- Sparse genre-specific loop guidance for roleplay, horror and fighting beyond a few examples.
- Verified drop-rate, pity and pricing numbers from top games are missing; indie figures are unverified.
- Limited guidance on onboarding/FTUE specifics beyond just-in-time tutorials.
- No coverage of anti-exploit design for loops (duping, alt farming) beyond a couple of tips.
- How dynamic behaviour is configured in Roblox Studio, including APIs, setup steps and naming.
- Performance cost of dynamic behaviour and human-like agents at scale, especially on mobile.
- Multiplayer and security implications of automated actors, including server authority.
- How to test and debug AI agents, and what counts as a passing result.
- Indie creator experiences with automation or AI agents and their effect on fun, retention or monetisation.
- Concrete examples of how the core loop rules (for instance, skill in the loop) apply when automated actors are involved.
- Behaviour-tree design patterns and where they break down compared with human-like agents.
- No [OFFICIAL] Roblox docs in this batch; verify API claims (Camera, DataStore for rebirth state) against current developer docs.
- No retention, session-length, or conversion metrics beyond one 5-minute timer; cannot validate pacing numbers.
- No coverage of monetisation tuning, or how paid speed or rebirth boosts affect the loop.
- No mobile or accessibility guidance in this batch.
- No concrete tuning formulas for treadmill cost curves, guard scaling, or reward-to-time ratios.
- Creator figures are self-reported and game-specific; sample sizes are small and survivor-biased.
- No coverage of checkpoint or save design beyond the respawn-at-spawn tip.
- No guidance on how many treadmill tiers or rebirth cycles are healthy over a long lifetime.
- Inventory and credit function code patterns (T18318, T18319) are implementation-level and not covered in depth here.
- Multiplayer networking and synchronisation for simultaneous turns is not covered.
- Turn-based combat advice comes from one creator only; cross-check with other sources.
- No retention, playtest or session-length metrics beyond the single 10+ minute guidance already in the file.
- Monetisation of storage or stamina upgrades is not covered.
- Official platform guidance on trigger volumes, held-item collision and physics is not in these sources.
- Horror design beyond the two tips here (sanity, speed) is not covered.
- No retention, conversion, or session-length numbers tied to these changes.
- No data comparing a la carte pricing against bundles for revenue or player satisfaction.
- No official Roblox guidance on paid upgrade policy or current highlight limits.
- No guidance on how many playtesters to recruit or how to run feedback sessions.
- Tower-defense specifics such as damage formulas and wave scaling values are not covered.
- Mobile and non-tap input handling for click-to-interact mechanics is not addressed.
- No threshold figures for acceptable loading time or matchmaking latency.
- No guidance on how to set numeric drain rates beyond the single 2x sprint example.
- No data on pricing or monetisation of gamepass-buying currencies.
- Benchmarks such as the 10+ minute beginner session are self-reported and not independently validated.
- Mutation and variant weights are not quantified.
- No measured retention effect for secret codes or exploration layers.
- No source gives the effect of spawn interval or weight values on player retention, income or pacing; playtest these numbers yourself.
- No official Roblox documentation items are included, so the API advice (wait, math.random, ModuleScripts) is unverified against current docs.
- No guidance on game-over UX (restart flow, respawn, stats screen).
- No data on how many weights or rooms make a procedural generator feel varied to players.
- No evidence on whether the build-to-earn or key-hunt structures sustain play beyond the first session.
- No benchmarks for spawn cleanup performance or how many clones a folder can hold before lag.

## Quotes (near-verbatim)
- "Try not punishing the player but reward them for the behavior you want." ([Roblox DevForum 2017-11 ](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/game-design-theory-planning-a-game-and-implementing-features/62838))
- "You don't want to be the tic-tac-toe of a melee combat game because everyone knows how to not lose a tic-tac-toe and it's just not fun." ([Roblox 2018-07 16:49](https://youtu.be/f8cSSzVnh9s?t=1009))
- "Stagger is such an important feedback tool for saying hey I messed up I should stop doing whatever I just did." ([Roblox 2018-07 12:33](https://youtu.be/f8cSSzVnh9s?t=753))
- "It's never about telling the player where you want to go; it's about using your environment, your assets and all those choices to drive them there." ([Roblox 2018-07 30:44](https://youtu.be/u0SxwDxFQB8?t=1844))
- "Focus on the core game loop first... as long as the core concept is fun to play then you're onto a winner." ([Last Level Studios 2019-09 43:22](https://youtu.be/h4W8yHxmxns?t=2602))
- "Your game should be easy to learn, but fun to play." ([Roblox DevForum 2020-07 ](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-make-a-successful-roblox-game/690805))
- "Replayability comes from the idea that the value of the second playthrough won't be the same as the previous playthrough." ([Roblox DevForum 2021-03 ](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-make-your-game-have-replayability/1082534))
- "Allow the players to improve if they do something different." ([Roblox DevForum 2021-03 ](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-make-your-game-have-replayability/1082534))
- "At least 75% of that game's revenue is generated by only 1-5% of its player base." ([Roblox DevForum 2021-05 ](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/roblox-game-monetization-guide/1235207))
- "Tumor is a system that directly conflicts with your core loop or competes for player attention with your core loop." ([Roblox Creators 2021-07 15:54](https://youtu.be/UL4QaSbfyC4?t=954))
- "A season pass is a feature that runs for a set duration of time... it facilitates that core loop." ([Roblox Creators 2021-09 1:11](https://youtu.be/k2gbQI-ggB4?t=71))
- "If your players can't find it, if they don't know where it lives in the UI, they're not going to engage with it. So surfacing is really important." ([Roblox Creators 2021-09 13:39](https://youtu.be/k2gbQI-ggB4?t=819))
- "It's always easier to make a feature easier over the span of it actually being live, than more difficult." ([Roblox Creators 2021-09 1:17:07](https://youtu.be/k2gbQI-ggB4?t=4627))
- "We walled off a lot of the roleplay experience to their houses and kind of like dropping them into this empty world." ([Roblox Creators 2021-09 19:13](https://youtu.be/UlKAoiFLgUo?t=1153))
- "They need short term goals they can achieve, not just long term grind." ([Roblox Creators 2021-10 16:11](https://youtu.be/1stxDQeHhr8?t=971))
- "If I'm just asking the player to throw a party, it doesn't mean that anyone else is required to show up." ([Roblox Creators 2021-11 26:08](https://youtu.be/iWSZJSE2NEc?t=1568))
- "If you're not enjoying it then chances are absolutely no one else is gonna enjoy it." ([Roblox Creators 2021-11 4:32](https://youtu.be/rVr0IyfYkPg?t=272))
- "core gameplay gets less fun the more you play it" ([Roblox Creators 2021-12 3:11](https://youtu.be/hD2Ive5RqtE?t=191))
- "If you add content without any progression, your superfans are going to blow through the content right away, and they're just going to want more." ([Roblox Creators 2021-12 14:35](https://youtu.be/XTZxQvh1qkc?t=875))
- "Will some classes be stronger than others? Sure. However, it is important to note that each class should hold an almost equal advantage." ([Roblox DevForum 2021-12 ](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/a-guide-to-balance-the-pillars-of-balance/1585238))

## Verified additions

_Hand-added, labelled and sourced rows kept in `youtube-learn/supplements/topics/TOPIC.md`; they survive rebuilds. Chunk 9 (2026-11-01): 2026 case-study evidence._

- **Add a consequence layer to a collection loop** (CORROBORATED, StudioKrew + eGamers + API): Steal An Egg adds player theft and a 7-player server cap to hatch-and-upgrade; Grow a Garden 2 adds night raids and defences; Steal A Pet And Ride It also caps servers at 7 (API). Sources: [StudioKrew Top Roblox games August 2026 (GGAID data)](https://studiokrew.com/blog/top-roblox-games-august-2026/), [eGamers](https://egamers.io/robloxs-no-1-game-steal-an-egg-was-pulled-hours-after-reaching-the-top/), [Roblox public API snapshot 2026-11-01](https://games.roblox.com/v1/games).
- **Sequels without collection history decay** (SINGLE-SOURCE interpretation of CORROBORATED numbers): Grow a Garden 2 fell from 505K (July) to 171.8K (2026-08-20) to 1,239 (2026-11-01, API); StudioKrew argues a new event with no history cannot revalue an old collection, whereas Murder Mystery 2 (created 2014, 548K now) revalues 12 years of tradeable items each season. Sources: [StudioKrew Top Roblox games August 2026 (GGAID data)](https://studiokrew.com/blog/top-roblox-games-august-2026/), [Roblox public API snapshot 2026-11-01](https://games.roblox.com/v1/games).
- **Luck-based, AFK-friendly versions of a proven genre can beat the skill version** (SINGLE-SOURCE, RoWatcher 2026): Fish It reported 77-minute average sessions and 500K+ peak vs Fisch ~100K; passes seen via API: Advanced Luck 545, Extra Luck 245, VIP 445 Robux. Sources: [RoWatcher Fish It](https://rowatcher.com/news/fish-it-reels-in-270k-players-inside-roblox-s-most-addictive-casual-game-of-2026), [Roblox public API snapshot 2026-11-01](https://games.roblox.com/v1/games).
- **Obby-runner with tool upgrades reached #1 trending in 15 days** (OFFICIAL API): DONT SPLASH (created 2026-10-17) sells 12 cheap passes (30 to 150 Robux: upgrade maxes, tool slots, 2x Cash 99); no creator commentary found, so why it works is not established. Source: [Roblox public API snapshot 2026-11-01](https://games.roblox.com/v1/games).
- **Offline progress plus short sessions** (SINGLE-SOURCE, StudioKrew/GamesBeat): "gardens grow while you are offline" and 5-minute sessions are cited for Grow a Garden; the 2026 chart evidence shows this alone does not hold a sequel. Sources: [StudioKrew Top Roblox games August 2026 (GGAID data)](https://studiokrew.com/blog/top-roblox-games-august-2026/), [GamesBeat](https://gamesbeat.com/?p=305182).
