- **Impulse-priced cosmetics and treadmill passes instead of progression gates** (OFFICIAL API listing): +1 Speed Keyboard Escape lists 46 passes (trails 19 to 1,989 Robux, ADMIN Treadmill 1,199, auras to 1,849+), Monkey Escape 40 passes (Starter Pack 9 Robux up to 2,399). Source: [Roblox public API snapshot 2026-11-01](https://games.roblox.com/v1/games).

## Appendix: every extracted tip on this topic

- 2017-11-29 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/game-design-theory-planning-a-game-and-implementing-features/62838): Before building, answer: target audience, desired learning curve, what differentiates your game, player goals and rewards, and the main anchoring feature.
- 2017-11-29 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/game-design-theory-planning-a-game-and-implementing-features/62838): If players don't engage, diagnose the cause: a steep learning curve may need a tutorial, and too many stats may need trimming.
- 2017-11-29 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/game-design-theory-planning-a-game-and-implementing-features/62838): Reward the behaviour you want rather than punishing other play styles, e.g. reward stealth rather than penalising aggression.
- 2018-07-24 Roblox [14:42](https://youtu.be/4rvzuYMtSlA?t=882): Consider social buffs: small XP, health or item bonuses for inviting or playing with friends, to reward bringing friends and staying longer.
- 2018-07-24 Roblox [19:04](https://youtu.be/4rvzuYMtSlA?t=1144): In competitive shooters, separate competitive zones from adjacent social or safe zones where players can chat instead of fighting.
- 2018-07-24 Roblox [23:51](https://youtu.be/4rvzuYMtSlA?t=1431): For two-player mechanics, require dexterity, speed or timing so one person cannot do both roles, which discourages alt-account farming.
- 2018-07-24 Roblox [21:17](https://youtu.be/DWUfL8azgIk?t=1277): Build rewarding content for non-paying players too; they make the game's paying players' spending possible and visible.
- 2018-07-24 Roblox [9:40](https://youtu.be/JTV8apW2oBk?t=580): Design gameplay with constant action and a clear hook so it is easy for YouTubers to make exciting videos.
- 2018-07-24 Roblox [5:23](https://youtu.be/NmyzbBbrBS4?t=323): Pick ideas for what other people will want to play, not just what you find fun; this correlates with success level.
- 2018-07-24 Roblox [8:14](https://youtu.be/f8cSSzVnh9s?t=494): Build stagger into every hit so the hit player gets feedback to stop spamming; stagger communicates mistakes better than a health bar.
- 2018-07-24 Roblox [12:33](https://youtu.be/f8cSSzVnh9s?t=753): Give parries a risky, mastery-rewarding payoff; players who parry consistently become feared yet learnable, adding depth over time.
- 2018-07-24 Roblox [11:54](https://youtu.be/f8cSSzVnh9s?t=714): Balance offense and defense so neither extreme dominates; design feedback that discourages spamming, passive blocking, and rewards calculated risk.
- 2018-07-24 Roblox [15:27](https://youtu.be/f8cSSzVnh9s?t=927): Add a stamina system to prevent attack spam, and use directional attacks and blocks so defense is an active choice, not a passive hold.
- 2018-07-24 Roblox [21:18](https://youtu.be/f8cSSzVnh9s?t=1278): Make parry a timed window at the end of an attack that costs your block for that period; this creates a read-and-counter risk.
- 2018-07-24 Roblox [33:18](https://youtu.be/f8cSSzVnh9s?t=1998): Add a guard break mechanic so blocking cannot be spammed; avoid having two options with identical results and weaknesses.
- 2018-07-24 Roblox [33:59](https://youtu.be/f8cSSzVnh9s?t=2039): Avoid solved, tic-tac-toe-like combat where one strategy always wins; add layers of depth so mastery remains possible.
- 2018-07-24 Roblox [40:09](https://youtu.be/f8cSSzVnh9s?t=2409): Use combos to gate powerful attacks behind setup steps, making high-reward moves predictable and fair to defend.
- 2018-07-24 Roblox [2:57](https://youtu.be/u0SxwDxFQB8?t=177): Answer global questions first (game type, tone, player camera perspective) before placing any art; they change UI, environment, and camera together.
- 2018-07-24 Roblox [8:29](https://youtu.be/u0SxwDxFQB8?t=509): Camera perspective changes layout: first-person needs close, narrowed focus; third-person needs multiple points of interest around the player; isometric needs clear foreground, midground, background.
- 2018-07-24 Roblox [17:54](https://youtu.be/u0SxwDxFQB8?t=1074): Use color to tell players what enemies are and how dangerous they are, so threats are readable without text or tutorial.
- 2018-07-24 Roblox [30:03](https://youtu.be/u0SxwDxFQB8?t=1803): Dim the space right around the player and make the key landmark the brightest object, so the player naturally heads toward it.
- 2018-07-24 Roblox [6:40](https://youtu.be/u0SxwDxFQB8?t=400): In open worlds, reuse assets heavily and use obstructions and partial blockers to steer players; Roblox instancing will reduce load for reused objects.
- 2018-07-24 Roblox [7:22](https://youtu.be/u0SxwDxFQB8?t=442): In obstacle courses, keep the play area clear: subdue and desaturate set pieces, and make collision match what players see, or they stop trusting the game.
- 2018-07-24 Roblox [39:34](https://youtu.be/u0SxwDxFQB8?t=2374): Limit play areas with deterrence: hills, dense trees, dim lighting and distant landmarks discourage going elsewhere; use invisible colliders as backup.
- 2018-07-24 Roblox [41:38](https://youtu.be/u0SxwDxFQB8?t=2498): For endless-looking worlds, use hills that rise and fall, fog, and far-off simplified objects; for edges, use cliffs or insurmountable water with visible limits.
- 2018-09-07 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/modeling-a-projectiles-motion/176677): Set Velocity on a cloned physics part for collision-accurate projectiles; use CFrame stepping on RenderStepped if collisions don't matter.
- 2018-09-09 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/game-development-resources-mega-thread/177780): Read 'Psychology of Feedback Loops and How to Make Them' and 'Creating Unique Game Ideas' to design core loops.
- 2019-01-26 AlvinBlox [22:25](https://youtu.be/0UhMpxxRFyc?t=1345): Use IntValue for rebirths so counts are whole numbers and NumberValue for strength; rebirth resets stats but grants a permanent counter.
- 2019-09-13 Last Level Studios [43:22](https://youtu.be/h4W8yHxmxns?t=2602): Nail the core gameplay loop first, make it fun, then layer monetisation and extra features on top.
- 2019-10-11 Last Level Studios [12:13](https://youtu.be/vY0om5O-Oh8?t=733): Keep art style consistent; mixing realistic and cartoony assets confuses players and loses the style-based player base.
- 2019-10-11 Last Level Studios [14:17](https://youtu.be/vY0om5O-Oh8?t=857): Define a clear target audience (age and demographic) before building; it drives style, marketing channels and platform choices.
- 2019-10-11 Last Level Studios [21:28](https://youtu.be/vY0om5O-Oh8?t=1288): Make campaign modes fully linear or fully open-world; mixing both with hidden required steps frustrates players.
- 2020-03-30 GameMakers [12:18](https://youtu.be/1q9Q6mRkciA?t=738): Prioritise engagement first; monetisation is an augmentation of a solid base game, not a substitute for it.
- 2020-03-30 GameMakers [33:45](https://youtu.be/1q9Q6mRkciA?t=2025): Fast, affordable launch and iteration plus premium payouts make Roblox attractive to new developers targeting older audiences.
- 2020-04-05 AlvinBlox [19:00](https://youtu.be/DhS5oexaE3w?t=1140): Use a while loop with wait(2) to spawn a random item from a template list every two seconds; clone it into a spawned folder.
- 2020-05-07 TheDevKing [1:46](https://youtu.be/AZTDrfuk9ME?t=106): Read Roblox's 'Designing for Engagement' article; it lists five engagement drivers: social, variety, convenience, achievement and investment.
- 2020-05-07 TheDevKing [4:31](https://youtu.be/AZTDrfuk9ME?t=271): Offer both casual and enthusiast paths and multiple ways to win; add progression so players feel they are advancing.
- 2020-05-07 TheDevKing [16:23](https://youtu.be/AZTDrfuk9ME?t=983): Don't copy a game exactly; change key elements and add live events to differentiate from the original.
- 2020-05-08 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/procedural-animation-what-it-is-and-how-to-do-it/564782): Procedural animation blends transitions naturally because code only targets the end pose, not the prior pose, enabling custom easing for blends.
- 2020-05-12 TheDevKing [4:02](https://youtu.be/Zqz_gclwM0M?t=242): Study top-earning games' game pass and product presentation (shop boards vs GUI, prompt timing) and copy what works for your own game.
- 2020-06-01 AlvinBlox [0:22](https://youtu.be/spFZ44yqIII?t=22): Schedule live events by comparing os.time() (Unix seconds) against a start timestamp in a while loop, firing the event once the start time is reached.
- 2020-06-18 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-make-server-synced-daily-shops/632401): Set the reset time by computing an hour offset from 00:00 UTC in your local timezone.
- 2020-06-18 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-make-server-synced-daily-shops/632401): Generate fewer shop items than the total item pool, otherwise duplicates cannot be avoided.
- 2020-06-18 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-make-server-synced-daily-shops/632401): Implement rarity with ordered weight intervals (rarest first) and NextNumber for decimal-precise weights.
- 2020-06-18 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-make-server-synced-daily-shops/632401): Prevent duplicates by respinning only from a copy of the rarity pool with already-chosen items removed.
- 2020-06-18 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-make-server-synced-daily-shops/632401): Enable HTTP Requests in Game Settings > Options before using the Google time fetch module.
- 2020-06-26 GnomeCode [40:21](https://youtu.be/Z74HM6xcsBE?t=2421): A place-buildings-to-earn-money-and-citizens loop gives a clear core loop even in a tiny map.
- 2020-06-26 GnomeCode [36:14](https://youtu.be/Z74HM6xcsBE?t=2174): A key-hunt structure (find the right key to unlock areas) gives a clear progression goal in a small space.
- 2020-06-27 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/creating-a-simple-realistic-camera-with-view-bobbing-dynamic-blur-sprinting/646943): Best suited to horror, adventure and exploration games where immersion matters; less suited to third-person.
- 2020-07-26 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-make-a-successful-roblox-game/690805): Ask whether you'd keep playing after about four rounds; if parts feel boring or unengaging, restart the core mechanic design from scratch.
- 2020-07-26 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-make-a-successful-roblox-game/690805): Build a core mechanic that is engaging, diverse, stimulating, and easy to update; an obby works by adding harder new sections as players progress.
- 2020-07-26 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-make-a-successful-roblox-game/690805): Ensure enough depth so players don't get bored after five minutes; easy to learn, but fun to play.
- 2020-07-26 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-make-a-successful-roblox-game/690805): Give players rewards such as scoreboard ranks, in-game economies, cosmetics, or login rewards, since players are less likely to act without a reward.
- 2020-08-14 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-make-a-raycasting-gun/723716): Destroy visual bullet parts after about 0.25 seconds so they do not clutter Workspace.
- 2020-08-24 Last Level Studios [17:06](https://youtu.be/uYIE3IVmKY8?t=1026): Compute signal state with a priority order: hard danger from occupancy overrides manual requests, then advanced signals ahead, then manual state with optional roll-back.
- 2020-12-11 SimTek Game Development [7:48](https://youtu.be/8QUJ_Qc6JTs?t=468): Store per-player item counts in a table keyed by player and item name, incrementing the old value on pickup.
- 2020-12-21 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/understanding-easing-styles/937281): Pick easing by feel: Back overshoots then settles, Bounce drops and rebounds lower, Elastic overshoots several times. Use these for juicy UI and object motion.
- 2021-01-02 AlvinBlox [7:00](https://youtu.be/lW3BgRRUEvI?t=420): A game with one random outcome and little to do leads players to quit after a few rounds. Add depth and reasons to keep playing.
- 2021-01-04 B Ricey [16:10](https://youtu.be/xwaCg7SWUhc?t=970): Lock placed parts from being dragged so players cannot grab and topple others' towers, forcing skillful throws.
- 2021-01-04 B Ricey [18:12](https://youtu.be/xwaCg7SWUhc?t=1092): Make thrown objects knock players over so players can defend their tower, adding dynamic interaction.
- 2021-01-07 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/pid-control-pt-1-proportional-control/968881): Use a P controller (CO = kP * error) to drive physics velocity smoothly instead of setting velocity directly; force falls to 0 as you approach target speed.
- 2021-01-17 B Ricey [10:21](https://youtu.be/7BSYIEdo6E4?t=621): Use script attributes as designer-facing config (e.g. debug flag, wait times) so non-coders can tune values in the Properties window.
- 2021-03-03 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-make-your-game-have-replayability/1082534): Avoid rigid outcomes; let players change results by choices so a second playthrough differs noticeably from the first.
- 2021-03-03 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-make-your-game-have-replayability/1082534): Mutate a simple click loop into varied outcomes (e.g. mining with unknown finds, clicks that trigger combat) instead of pure clicking.
- 2021-03-03 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-make-your-game-have-replayability/1082534): In tycoons, let independent player decisions pay off later so players are motivated to experiment.
- 2021-03-03 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-make-your-game-have-replayability/1082534): Give tools that change playstyle rather than straight stat upgrades; avoid flat +health per level.
- 2021-03-03 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-make-your-game-have-replayability/1082534): Make rebirth/prestige change the context (new scale or setting), not repeat the same loop with bigger numbers.
- 2021-03-03 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-make-your-game-have-replayability/1082534): Give players a clear game philosophy about how to play (e.g. safe play vs risk) and keep the game consistent with it.
- 2021-03-03 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-make-your-game-have-replayability/1082534): Place map mechanisms and hidden areas that unlock new activities so the loop is not just clicking and jumping.
- 2021-03-25 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/fps-using-viewmodels-the-improved-version-parts-2-out-of-3/1129877): Set tool parts CanCollide off and Massless on, otherwise they can fling the player.
- 2021-04-22 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/camera-animations-for-cutscenes-and-gameplay/1181691): During cutscenes set Humanoid.AutoRotate to false and Camera.CameraType to Scriptable, then restore Custom and the saved CFrame when finished.
- 2021-05-17 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/roblox-game-monetization-guide/1235207): Treat players as First Purchasers, Repeat Purchasers and Whales; the first purchase is the hardest, so offer a cheap entry item first.
- 2021-06-14 GnomeCode [16:21](https://youtu.be/0rMO-VHSuGs?t=981): Add checkpoints using Team objects set to auto-assignable with unique team colors, and make spawn locations non-neutral with matching team color and Team Change On Touch.
- 2021-06-14 GnomeCode [20:52](https://youtu.be/0rMO-VHSuGs?t=1252): Keep distances between points of interest to about 10 seconds of walking. Long empty walks feel boring and make players quit.
- 2021-06-14 GnomeCode [23:38](https://youtu.be/0rMO-VHSuGs?t=1418): Place NPCs along long routes to give quests and dialogue, so travel doubles as content rather than dead space.
- 2021-07-16 Roblox Creators [2:10](https://youtu.be/Gv5OVVyWM10?t=130): Major updates should change the core loop or game dynamics, not just add content; weekly updates sustain metrics while major ones improve retention and reach new audiences.
- 2021-07-16 Roblox Creators [37:10](https://youtu.be/Gv5OVVyWM10?t=2230): Fix unbalanced or unpopular features within the first week; players shouldn't have to warm up to a feature that is not fun.
- 2021-07-22 Roblox Creators [8:51](https://youtu.be/UL4QaSbfyC4?t=531): Ask first how any new feature enhances the core loop; integrate content into the loop rather than alongside it.
- 2021-07-22 Roblox Creators [9:41](https://youtu.be/UL4QaSbfyC4?t=581): A core loop is the minute-to-minute action repeated every session and the engine for all progression; if it isn't fun, nothing else will save the game.
- 2021-07-22 Roblox Creators [15:54](https://youtu.be/UL4QaSbfyC4?t=954): Avoid tumors: systems that compete with the core loop for attention. They split the player base and create update debt for a small subset of players.
- 2021-07-22 Roblox Creators [16:38](https://youtu.be/UL4QaSbfyC4?t=998): Don't offer an opt-out alternative to the core activity (e.g. fight club vs class); a 50/50 split typically tilts to 10/90, leaving one side empty.
- 2021-07-22 Roblox Creators [35:55](https://youtu.be/UL4QaSbfyC4?t=2155): If the core loop isn't fun, fixing it is the top priority, even if it means treating a major expansion as a redo of the core experience.
- 2021-07-22 Roblox Creators [39:19](https://youtu.be/UL4QaSbfyC4?t=2359): Keep the core loop simple and easy to enter; the systems around it create the memorable moments like boss fights with dodge and heal choices.
- 2021-07-22 Roblox Creators [40:43](https://youtu.be/UL4QaSbfyC4?t=2443): Focus on a single core loop; parallel loops create an unintentional rift where players effectively play different games.
- 2021-07-22 Roblox Creators [41:24](https://youtu.be/UL4QaSbfyC4?t=2484): Let players create their own side experiences (e.g. trading for profit) but don't build dedicated content for emergent behaviours of a minority.
- 2021-07-26 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/weighted-chance-system/1373953): Weighted chance system: store prize chances in a table (0-1), sum them into Weight, roll math.random over that total, then iterate again to find the matching prize.
- 2021-08-05 Roblox Creators [9:09](https://youtu.be/pSjFYDEgRxs?t=549): Before balancing, define how often players engage with a feature (daily, per session, weekly) and how long it should last.
- 2021-08-05 Roblox Creators [17:09](https://youtu.be/pSjFYDEgRxs?t=1029): Add currency or resource sinks (repeatable spend items) to pull inflated economies back toward balance over time.
- 2021-08-05 Roblox Creators [18:32](https://youtu.be/pSjFYDEgRxs?t=1112): Cap currencies and resources (e.g. 99 wood) so players spend rather than hoard and you can require more in future content.
- 2021-08-05 Roblox Creators [4:21](https://youtu.be/pSjFYDEgRxs?t=261): Consider how a feature changes the economy: what resources it creates or sinks and which existing systems it affects.
- 2021-08-05 Roblox Creators [3:10](https://youtu.be/pSjFYDEgRxs?t=190): Build sustainable, evergreen content; racing players to create content faster than they consume it is a losing battle.
- 2021-08-05 Roblox Creators [2:29](https://youtu.be/pSjFYDEgRxs?t=149): Make rewards effortful; exclusive items that take hours to obtain validate effort and advertise the feature to others.
- 2021-08-26 Roblox Creators [7:24](https://youtu.be/dBlP8NdJQM0?t=444): Plan the beginning and ending of a story before writing the middle; MiniToon says this avoids struggling to wrap up later chapters.
- 2021-08-26 Roblox Creators [14:35](https://youtu.be/dBlP8NdJQM0?t=875): Put essential story in main progression; hidden notes should only be extra lore for superfans, not required to understand the plot.
- 2021-08-26 Roblox Creators [32:11](https://youtu.be/dBlP8NdJQM0?t=1931): Give each room a distinct color or props; repeated identical rooms confuse players and make them get lost.
- 2021-08-27 Last Level Studios [18:33](https://youtu.be/3djSOjdGaE8?t=1113): Build on a niche you're good at: he focused on comedy chaos games with silly absurdist tone, which kept the game family-friendly.
- 2021-09-02 Roblox Creators [18:55](https://youtu.be/kQIyqMdh7Os?t=1135): Choose cadence content that supports your core loop (quests, combat, exploration, customization) and identify gaps from it.
- 2021-09-02 Roblox Creators [26:09](https://youtu.be/kQIyqMdh7Os?t=1569): Release sink items to pull inflated resources out of inventories; make sure new items differ enough to avoid cannibalising old sales.
- 2021-09-07 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/open-source-lock-on-combat-in-3d/1458506): Lock-on enables slow, methodical combat that shiftlock combat makes hard to design, since shiftlock tends to reduce games to jump-position-attack.
- 2021-09-16 Roblox Creators [1:11](https://youtu.be/k2gbQI-ggB4?t=71): Build season passes as a meta layer over existing systems; players do the normal game to earn pass XP, so little new content is needed.
- 2021-09-16 Roblox Creators [17:07](https://youtu.be/k2gbQI-ggB4?t=1027): Launch difficulty slightly harder than target and ease it later; players dislike a sudden spike in difficulty after a fixed pace.
- 2021-09-16 Roblox Creators [31:29](https://youtu.be/k2gbQI-ggB4?t=1889): Plan for missions to be locked and released per day so players cannot burn through the whole season on day one, which protects retention.
- 2021-09-16 Roblox Creators [37:11](https://youtu.be/k2gbQI-ggB4?t=2231): Use a non-linear XP curve so early tiers feel rewarding quickly and later tiers feel more earned, instead of monotonous linear grind.
- 2021-09-16 Roblox Creators [52:32](https://youtu.be/k2gbQI-ggB4?t=3152): Budget only a share of daily playtime (e.g. 25-75%) for the season pass so it does not replace socialising and other systems.
- 2021-09-16 Roblox Creators [1:59:59](https://youtu.be/k2gbQI-ggB4?t=7199): Weight mission rewards by their frequency using SUMPRODUCT to get a realistic average XP per daily.
- 2021-09-30 Roblox Creators [19:13](https://youtu.be/UlKAoiFLgUo?t=1153): Give players lots of content straight out of the gate; don't wall off roleplay behind grinding. Players come to social roleplay games to experience things immediately.
- 2021-09-30 Roblox Creators [15:43](https://youtu.be/UlKAoiFLgUo?t=943): Build interactive props (lockers, makeup vanity, library books) so players can tell their own stories in the space.
- 2021-09-30 Roblox Creators [30:31](https://youtu.be/UlKAoiFLgUo?t=1831): Add human-like cues (bridges, stairs) so players understand paths; avoid over-fantasy layouts that feel unnatural.
- 2021-09-30 Roblox Creators [38:08](https://youtu.be/UlKAoiFLgUo?t=2288): Treat the avatar customizer as a core system; Myzta redeveloped it about seven times and spent roughly 3 months polishing it.
- 2021-09-30 Roblox Creators [48:15](https://youtu.be/UlKAoiFLgUo?t=2895): Keep the HUD minimal: put UI in 3D space where possible, and only show essentials like emotes, map, camera, customizer and backpack.
- 2021-10-21 Roblox Creators [28:36](https://youtu.be/d4g30UupgKw?t=1716): Keep effort proportional to reward; if content is too hard to collect, players give up.
- 2021-10-28 Roblox Creators [0:05](https://youtu.be/1stxDQeHhr8?t=5): Build quest tasks by listing every system and content item, then asking 'what can the player blank' with both generic and specific terms, so all systems get engagement.
- 2021-10-28 Roblox Creators [4:23](https://youtu.be/1stxDQeHhr8?t=263): Group systems into categories and pick one task per category so no quest set touches the same system twice, giving variety.
- 2021-10-28 Roblox Creators [5:04](https://youtu.be/1stxDQeHhr8?t=304): Respect player time: long tasks suit achievements, not dailies; keep most time on the fun part rather than travel or timers.
- 2021-10-28 Roblox Creators [9:10](https://youtu.be/1stxDQeHhr8?t=550): Tune difficulty via modifiers: quantity, level gates, per-session caps, timers, time limits and cooldowns; generic tasks are easier than specific ones.
- 2021-10-28 Roblox Creators [10:33](https://youtu.be/1stxDQeHhr8?t=633): Pace quests with breathing room between high-challenge periods; overwhelmed players burn out, leave, and take friends with them.
- 2021-10-28 Roblox Creators [12:05](https://youtu.be/1stxDQeHhr8?t=725): Keep difficulty fair: changes should be within player control (level up, gear, strategy) and rewards commensurate with effort.
- 2021-10-28 Roblox Creators [13:29](https://youtu.be/1stxDQeHhr8?t=809): Compare average quest completion time with your average session length; dailies should not consume a player's whole session.
- 2021-10-28 Roblox Creators [14:09](https://youtu.be/1stxDQeHhr8?t=849): Avoid quests needing literal hours; for collection quests, hide more items than required so the last few aren't tedious.
- 2021-10-28 Roblox Creators [14:49](https://youtu.be/1stxDQeHhr8?t=889): Handle one-time tasks carefully: let prior accomplishments count or allow repeats so players are not stuck on a quest they can't redo.
- 2021-10-28 Roblox Creators [16:11](https://youtu.be/1stxDQeHhr8?t=971): Reserve very difficult or long tasks for achievements (Shark Bite example: unlock expensive high-level boats) and give short-term goals early for a sense of progress.
- 2021-10-28 Roblox Creators [17:44](https://youtu.be/1stxDQeHhr8?t=1064): Shape quest chains like a story arc: gradually increase effort to peaks, vary peak frequency and intensity, and end each chain on a satisfying break.
- 2021-10-28 Roblox Creators [19:07](https://youtu.be/1stxDQeHhr8?t=1147): Allow min-maxing: let players progress multiple quests at once with one action (e.g. planting wheat seeds that count for both a plant and a harvest quest).
- 2021-10-28 Roblox Creators [19:48](https://youtu.be/1stxDQeHhr8?t=1188): Chain quests so one sets up the next (gather ingredients, then bake a cake), so earlier effort pays off.
- 2021-10-28 Roblox Creators [21:31](https://youtu.be/1stxDQeHhr8?t=1291): Tailor quests to player cohorts by player level so players meet quests when they have the skill and knowledge for them.
- 2021-11-11 Roblox Creators [4:57](https://youtu.be/iWSZJSE2NEc?t=297): Be precise in task wording (e.g. 'drink freshwater' not 'drink water'); specificity also creates variant tasks with different difficulty.
- 2021-11-11 Roblox Creators [6:20](https://youtu.be/iWSZJSE2NEc?t=380): Phrase requirements on the outcome (5 tomatoes) rather than attempts (harvest 5 times) when drops are random, to avoid unfair grind.
- 2021-11-11 Roblox Creators [8:32](https://youtu.be/iWSZJSE2NEc?t=512): Avoid quest tasks that require hard currency or Robux spend; prefer soft currency and check per-player wallet analytics before setting amounts.
- 2021-11-11 Roblox Creators [9:53](https://youtu.be/iWSZJSE2NEc?t=593): Rate task difficulty by inherent action difficulty and social dependency; trade tasks are harder because they need other players online.
- 2021-11-11 Roblox Creators [10:34](https://youtu.be/iWSZJSE2NEc?t=634): Use 'have' or 'get' task wording to credit items players already own, rewarding work already done.
- 2021-11-11 Roblox Creators [13:00](https://youtu.be/iWSZJSE2NEc?t=780): Define a consistent game voice (excitable vs dry, wacky vs serious) and apply it to all text including UI.
- 2021-11-11 Roblox Creators [16:31](https://youtu.be/iWSZJSE2NEc?t=991): Deliver story via NPC dialogue pop-ups that bookend a quest line: introduce the story at start and wrap it up on completion.
- 2021-11-11 Roblox Creators [13:47](https://youtu.be/iWSZJSE2NEc?t=827): Have quest-giver NPCs deliver quests in their own voice and perspective to make otherwise dull tasks more personal.
- 2021-11-11 Roblox Creators [23:33](https://youtu.be/iWSZJSE2NEc?t=1413): Build dailies from easy generic tasks (plant fruit, harvest any crop, throw a party) and medium tasks, avoiding hard ones so players finish within a session.
- 2021-11-11 Roblox Creators [26:08](https://youtu.be/iWSZJSE2NEc?t=1568): Choose social tasks like throwing a party that count even on empty servers so solo players are not blocked.
- 2021-11-11 Roblox Creators [36:08](https://youtu.be/iWSZJSE2NEc?t=2168): Know how long achievements take players to complete, e.g. currency needed for a big purchase divided by earn rate, before setting rewards.
- 2021-11-18 Roblox Creators [4:32](https://youtu.be/rVr0IyfYkPg?t=272): Test your own game for long stretches; if you keep getting lost in testing, it's a good sign it's fun. If you're bored, players will be too.
- 2021-11-18 Roblox Creators [6:13](https://youtu.be/rVr0IyfYkPg?t=373): Start with small core concepts and test them; if one mechanic gets players engaged, pivot the game toward it and invest more time only then.
- 2021-11-18 Roblox Creators [10:14](https://youtu.be/rVr0IyfYkPg?t=614): Make player activity visible to others (Wacky Wizards moved from private rooms to an open world), enabling sharing potions and discovering recipes.
- 2021-12-01 Roblox Creators [8:19](https://youtu.be/cY1B5_x4RoU?t=499): Tune micro-animations: too fast goes unnoticed, too slow and players leave before it finishes; well-timed animations add polish.
- 2021-12-01 Roblox Creators [10:23](https://youtu.be/wrf6DRlfoO8?t=623): Use teasers and mysteries (e.g. Piggy's hidden true ending hunt) to make players theorize and retain curiosity.
- 2021-12-03 Roblox Creators [0:38](https://youtu.be/hD2Ive5RqtE?t=38): Map goals in three tiers: long-term (weeks/months), mid-term (days to a week), short-term (achievable in one session, usually the core loop). Each tier should feed the next.
- 2021-12-03 Roblox Creators [5:16](https://youtu.be/hD2Ive5RqtE?t=316): Put something at stake in core gameplay. Goals that tie each session to a larger outcome make the core loop more interesting than repeated actions with no consequence.
- 2021-12-03 Roblox Creators [6:37](https://youtu.be/hD2Ive5RqtE?t=397): Start goal design from the long-term goal, since it is the main driving force. Then derive mid and short-term goals from it.
- 2021-12-03 Roblox Creators [8:01](https://youtu.be/hD2Ive5RqtE?t=481): If short-term goals are not your core loop, either the core loop needs work or the short-term goals need reframing.
- 2021-12-09 Roblox Creators [1:52](https://youtu.be/XTZxQvh1qkc?t=112): Pick progression type to fit core loop and audience; XP is most flexible, economy suits loops that earn coins to buy items.
- 2021-12-09 Roblox Creators [7:00](https://youtu.be/XTZxQvh1qkc?t=420): Build progression on top of the core loop; a progression system competing with core gameplay splits player attention.
- 2021-12-09 Roblox Creators [8:23](https://youtu.be/XTZxQvh1qkc?t=503): Shape difficulty as a curve: ramp up, peak at boss fights, then dip so players recover and feel powerful.
- 2021-12-09 Roblox Creators [12:30](https://youtu.be/XTZxQvh1qkc?t=750): Gate lower-tier items behind progression so they gain prestige and players learn their mechanics instead of skipping them.
- 2021-12-09 Roblox Creators [19:16](https://youtu.be/XTZxQvh1qkc?t=1156): Offer meaningful choices in progression (e.g., skill tree picks) so players develop strategies and talk about them.
- 2021-12-09 Roblox Creators [27:30](https://youtu.be/XTZxQvh1qkc?t=1650): Avoid power creep by rebalancing overpowered items and taking player feedback; seasonal resets and trading also help.
- 2021-12-10 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-make-a-terrifying-horror-game/1583917): If flashlight is the main light, make its use cost something (battery drain, noise, creature approaches); scale battery systems to difficulty.
- 2021-12-10 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-make-a-terrifying-horror-game/1583917): Place batteries in drawers, cabinets, or interactable devices, not scattered on floors and tables for no reason.
- 2021-12-11 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/a-guide-to-balance-the-pillars-of-balance/1585238): Balance on six axes: mechanical, progressive, skill progression, comparative, conceptual, and executional. Stats alone don't cover balance.
- 2021-12-11 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/a-guide-to-balance-the-pillars-of-balance/1585238): List each class's or weapon's strengths and weaknesses. Check whether strengths overpower weaknesses by a severe amount, not just whether there are more strengths.
- 2021-12-11 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/a-guide-to-balance-the-pillars-of-balance/1585238): A major weakness can offset many strengths. Example: high HP and damage fail if long cooldowns and low base mobility leave the class unable to catch opponents.
- 2021-12-11 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/a-guide-to-balance-the-pillars-of-balance/1585238): Don't let level-ups make new players fight maxed players in PvP. Gate stat progression so starting players can compete, or the system breaks balance.
- 2021-12-11 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/a-guide-to-balance-the-pillars-of-balance/1585238): When comparing classes or weapons, break each into attributes, weight attributes by importance, and mark each green (stronger), red (weaker), or grey (neutral).
- 2021-12-11 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/a-guide-to-balance-the-pillars-of-balance/1585238): Treat a class as too strong or weak only if the issue persists against all other classes. If it is only a counter matchup, it is not a balance problem.
- 2021-12-11 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/a-guide-to-balance-the-pillars-of-balance/1585238): Check whether a class's central idea fits the game's design. Example: an always-airborne class is near-invincible if most attacks can't aim vertically.
- 2021-12-11 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/a-guide-to-balance-the-pillars-of-balance/1585238): Give a concept-heavy class secondary options so it is not reliant on its core gimmick. Air moves that are supplementary, not required, make the class viable.
- 2021-12-11 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/a-guide-to-balance-the-pillars-of-balance/1585238): Test executional balance: bugs and unintuitive behaviour, such as a projectile firing off-center, make an ability feel weak and players avoid it.
- 2022-01-02 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/full-beginners-guide-on-scripting-animefighting-vfxvisual-effects-part-1/1610853): Use UserInputService and check GameProcessedEvent so inputs typed in chat or textboxes are ignored.
- 2022-01-04 GnomeCode [22:47](https://youtu.be/de5-o6euwJE?t=1367): Trigger a game-over event when base health <= 0 and break the wave loop, so the game actually ends instead of spawning forever.
- 2022-01-13 Roblox Creators [4:02](https://youtu.be/oV8DKuAjQQs?t=242): Release 2-3 playable content items per update (weapons, maps, grind levels, features) so players always have something new to play or buy to skip grind.
- 2022-01-13 Roblox Creators [58:56](https://youtu.be/oV8DKuAjQQs?t=3536): Treat power creep as manageable: design weapons into distinct niches, use the existing content as a framework, and keep feature-complete systems untouched.
- 2022-01-13 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-animate-2-characters-and-play-it-in-game-like-a-takedown-animation/1625809): Position both characters relative to each other (about 5 studs apart, facing) before playing a paired animation so the two rigs line up.
- 2022-01-27 Roblox Creators [0:18](https://youtu.be/bROouMt1b3g?t=18): Define your core loop first: the most-repeated minute-to-minute action players do, since every session relies on it. Without it, the game lacks cohesion.
- 2022-01-27 Roblox Creators [5:33](https://youtu.be/bROouMt1b3g?t=333): For every new system you add, ask how it interacts with the core loop. If it doesn't feed or augment the loop, don't build it.
- 2022-01-27 Roblox Creators [3:10](https://youtu.be/bROouMt1b3g?t=190): Give different player roles (e.g. cop vs robber) distinct but meaningful interactions with the same loop, rather than separate loops.
- 2022-01-27 Roblox Creators [6:43](https://youtu.be/bROouMt1b3g?t=403): Use reward systems like season passes or attendance bonuses to reward players for engaging with the core loop.
- 2022-02-01 GDC Festival of Gaming [37:50](https://youtu.be/WGRpglQ87XE?t=2270): When missions feel repetitive or stall, add varied ways to play such as daily goals and rotating limited-time events, so players always have something meaningful to do.
- 2022-02-01 GDC Festival of Gaming [55:25](https://youtu.be/WGRpglQ87XE?t=3325): If a feature is confusing, give it a real gameplay effect. Cooking Dash's pets were cosmetic, so they were changed to auto-serve food.
- 2022-02-03 Roblox Creators [9:03](https://youtu.be/iI2t7Yd7Byc?t=543): Prototype the core loop first and confirm it is fun before building anything else.
- 2022-02-17 Roblox Creators [18:04](https://youtu.be/BkQL2_DY9Mc?t=1084): Build multi-step, collaborative systems (e.g. pizza production line) where players depend on each other for rewards.
- 2022-02-17 Roblox Creators [28:57](https://youtu.be/BkQL2_DY9Mc?t=1737): Split new and experienced players into separate worlds or protect them, or newcomers get griefed and quit.
- 2022-03-17 Roblox Creators [9:56](https://youtu.be/czVxazdl0kg?t=596): Plan level layout around core mechanics first (collection, combat, progression), then add visuals; reposition interactables before decorating.
- 2022-03-17 Roblox Creators [12:52](https://youtu.be/czVxazdl0kg?t=772): For fast-paced FPS, prioritise gameplay playability over realism; good level design matters more than detail.
- 2022-03-17 Roblox Creators [40:52](https://youtu.be/czVxazdl0kg?t=2452): Design for multiple play styles (stealth, roamer, sniper) by giving each a route through the same space.
- 2022-03-17 Roblox Creators [50:51](https://youtu.be/czVxazdl0kg?t=3051): Use linear corridors for forced routes and open visual cues (inviting space, obstacles) when players should explore freely.
- 2022-03-17 Roblox Creators [52:13](https://youtu.be/czVxazdl0kg?t=3133): Use visual cues like particle effects, billboard GUIs and beams to mark interactables for players of all ages and skill levels.
- 2022-03-17 Roblox Creators [54:58](https://youtu.be/czVxazdl0kg?t=3298): Start open worlds smaller than needed (a 2k x 2k world had players not reaching key locations); expand only after core interactables work.
- 2022-03-17 Roblox Creators [1:07:04](https://youtu.be/czVxazdl0kg?t=4024): In horror, make each unlocked area look visually distinct so progression feels rewarding rather than endless grey corridors.
- 2022-03-17 Roblox Creators [1:21:13](https://youtu.be/czVxazdl0kg?t=4873): Keep open-world exploration concise for mobile; sprinting and navigation are hard on mobile, so keep regions explorable but not taxing.
- 2022-04-16 B Ricey [6:02](https://youtu.be/TmiU5CY6BW0?t=362): Store the gamepass ID as a number attribute on each button and tag buttons with a shared tag, so one reusable script can wire up any number of passes.
- 2022-04-25 Game Maker's Toolkit [0:44](https://youtu.be/Zrf1cou_yVo?t=44): Design economy entities deliberately: taps create resources, inventories store them, converters spend them, drains remove them, traders trade them. Map your loop against these.
- 2022-04-25 Game Maker's Toolkit [1:54](https://youtu.be/Zrf1cou_yVo?t=114): Use taps to incentivise behaviour: put loot on enemies to encourage fighting, unique materials per biome to encourage exploration.
- 2022-04-25 Game Maker's Toolkit [3:04](https://youtu.be/Zrf1cou_yVo?t=184): Set inventory upper limits to force meaningful choices and stop players hoarding resources they never use.
- 2022-04-25 Game Maker's Toolkit [4:29](https://youtu.be/Zrf1cou_yVo?t=269): Control progression pace by tuning converter costs or tap output; XP cost vs. XP per kill tells you roughly how many kills before a power-up.
- 2022-04-25 Game Maker's Toolkit [5:13](https://youtu.be/Zrf1cou_yVo?t=313): Fewer currencies or shared crafting materials create harder decisions; separate per-upgrade materials remove decisions.
- 2022-04-25 Game Maker's Toolkit [6:45](https://youtu.be/Zrf1cou_yVo?t=405): Positive power-creep loops are addictive but become grind; add a negative feedback loop or puzzle depth to avoid tedium.
- 2022-04-25 Game Maker's Toolkit [8:58](https://youtu.be/Zrf1cou_yVo?t=538): Scale conversion cost with level (Elden Ring rune curve) so farming trivial enemies is ineffective and players explore harder areas.
- 2022-04-25 Game Maker's Toolkit [9:49](https://youtu.be/Zrf1cou_yVo?t=589): Add drains (breakage, upkeep, spoilage, ammo use) to slow power growth and force players to re-engage with the economy.
- 2022-04-25 Game Maker's Toolkit [12:09](https://youtu.be/Zrf1cou_yVo?t=729): Add trader NPCs with their own stock and prices by location so players profit from arbitrage, creating trade-route puzzles.
- 2022-04-25 Game Maker's Toolkit [12:53](https://youtu.be/Zrf1cou_yVo?t=773): Model supply and demand (Moonlighter: flooding a market drops price) so repeated sales cannot be farmed infinitely.
- 2022-04-25 Game Maker's Toolkit [13:37](https://youtu.be/Zrf1cou_yVo?t=817): Add price volatility with risk and reward (Animal Crossing turnips) to fix exploits and add decisions about how much to risk.
- 2022-04-28 Roblox Creators [0:47](https://youtu.be/5N84f1ZuwRc?t=47): Don't assume a useful-seeming feature helps; a sprint button cut day-one retention, possibly by reducing social interaction or speeding exploration.
- 2022-05-05 Roblox Creators [25:22](https://youtu.be/xBlHz30PMKo?t=1522): Give hostile players upgradeable abilities and give all players health (three strikes) so a single mistake isn't instant elimination.
- 2022-05-12 Roblox Creators [38:46](https://youtu.be/14RMDByrG1U?t=2326): Establish the core loop fast and make it tight; persistent brand games need a loop that gives players a reason to come back.
- 2022-05-12 Roblox Creators [36:42](https://youtu.be/14RMDByrG1U?t=2202): Pick the game style that naturally fits the IP's fantasy (e.g., roleplay for a superhero show) and validate it with community playtests before showing the brand.
- 2022-05-12 Roblox Creators [1:09:32](https://youtu.be/14RMDByrG1U?t=4172): Immerse in the IP (read source material, watch the show) because fans notice inauthentic content and it can turn off the core evangelists.
- 2022-06-11 Roblox Creators [0:23](https://youtu.be/Mep4_w8qxeE?t=23): Define one core loop: the most repeated minute-to-minute action. If it isn't fun, the rest of the game won't be either.
- 2022-06-11 Roblox Creators [13:28](https://youtu.be/Mep4_w8qxeE?t=808): Before adding any system, state which part of the core loop it improves. Systems that don't tie back to the loop feel random and jumbled.
- 2022-06-11 Roblox Creators [38:48](https://youtu.be/Mep4_w8qxeE?t=2328): Aim for one core loop per experience. Multiple loops usually mean the loop is overcomplicated or a tumor is present.
- 2022-06-11 Roblox Creators [39:42](https://youtu.be/Mep4_w8qxeE?t=2382): Remove social gates from the core loop. Adopt Me's solo pet feature boosted engagement after parents needed another player to earn currency.
- 2022-06-11 Roblox Creators [42:13](https://youtu.be/Mep4_w8qxeE?t=2533): The core loop should work solo or multiplayer. Give single-player players meaningful loop actions, as Jailbreak did with armored vehicle heists.
- 2022-06-11 Roblox Creators [44:21](https://youtu.be/Mep4_w8qxeE?t=2661): Separate core loop (repeated actions) from progression (levels, unlocks). Give max-level players an endgame, a season, or a periodic reset.
- 2022-06-11 Roblox Creators [50:12](https://youtu.be/Mep4_w8qxeE?t=3012): Design the whole experience around the core loop, especially early on. Unanchored cool ideas create a confusing mess and a short novelty period.
- 2022-06-16 Roblox Creators [27:19](https://youtu.be/hCT9zCJ1M5c?t=1639): Let players conclude the lesson: give about 95% and let them reach the last 5% themselves. This aids retention and satisfaction.
- 2022-06-16 Roblox Creators [28:45](https://youtu.be/hCT9zCJ1M5c?t=1725): Make learning interactive rather than read-only; interaction is where knowledge sticks for players.
- 2022-06-16 Roblox Creators [36:27](https://youtu.be/hCT9zCJ1M5c?t=2187): Simplify building tools for mobile while keeping enough possibility space for players to be expressive.
- 2022-06-16 Roblox Creators [46:49](https://youtu.be/hCT9zCJ1M5c?t=2809): Offer optional hints and encouragement for struggling players, plus glossaries or visual analogies, rather than rewriting lessons (rewrites multiply dev time).
- 2022-06-16 Roblox Creators [49:33](https://youtu.be/hCT9zCJ1M5c?t=2973): Use a guide character who asks questions to model reasoning, so the player still makes the decision.
- 2022-06-16 Roblox Creators [59:47](https://youtu.be/hCT9zCJ1M5c?t=3587): After high failure rates, reword the concept with real-world metaphors; failure rates dropped afterward.
- 2022-06-30 Suphi Kaner [36:01](https://youtu.be/B446FyN1xtc?t=2161): Add a capacity cap plus a paid storage upgrade to force a sell loop; this creates a gold sink tied to the collect-sell-upgrade cycle.
- 2022-08-18 Roblox Creators [0:45](https://youtu.be/lJAEKxoIUAs?t=45): Layer short-term progression (getting faster) on top of the core loop, then add midterm goals like gated worlds (e.g., level 50) so players have a reason to keep leveling.
- 2022-08-18 Roblox Creators [1:26](https://youtu.be/lJAEKxoIUAs?t=86): Make collectibles feed progression: rings unlock pets that speed progress, so players keep collecting and feel forward momentum.
- 2022-08-18 Roblox Creators [2:48](https://youtu.be/lJAEKxoIUAs?t=168): Use stacking multiplier boosts (e.g., +5, +10, +15 on top of 1:1) that grow exponentially, as simulators do, to sustain the desire to keep progressing.
- 2022-09-01 Roblox Creators [4:12](https://youtu.be/KD3YTfRH6fQ?t=252): Show the final long-term goal early in the tutorial so players know where progression leads, even if it takes weeks or months to reach.
- 2022-09-01 Roblox Creators [4:53](https://youtu.be/KD3YTfRH6fQ?t=293): Wrap the long-term goal in story/lore that gives purpose, then let players choose their own path to it.
- 2022-09-01 Roblox Creators [5:34](https://youtu.be/KD3YTfRH6fQ?t=334): Give players several valid progression paths (combat, gathering, trading, mounts) so each can pursue what they find fun and meaningful.
- 2022-09-01 Roblox Creators [5:34](https://youtu.be/KD3YTfRH6fQ?t=334): Don't explicitly explain every system; let players discover mechanics like skill difficulty scaling, which makes them feel smart.
- 2022-09-01 Roblox Creators [2:51](https://youtu.be/KD3YTfRH6fQ?t=171): Balance skill leveling so early levels take about a minute and cost grows exponentially, pushing players to spread across skills.
- 2022-09-01 Roblox Creators [3:32](https://youtu.be/KD3YTfRH6fQ?t=212): Add meaningful choices that vary between sessions so the game doesn't go stale.
- 2022-09-01 Roblox Creators [1:27](https://youtu.be/KD3YTfRH6fQ?t=87): Gate content behind upgrades: settlement levels unlock bigger land plots and new crafting recipes, giving a clear gating structure.
- 2022-09-01 Roblox Creators [1:27](https://youtu.be/KD3YTfRH6fQ?t=87): Layer goals by horizon: short-term repetitive income, mid-term chosen recipe purchases, long-term final objective.
- 2022-09-01 Roblox Creators [0:47](https://youtu.be/KD3YTfRH6fQ?t=47): Use visible social status (other players' armor and settlements) as a motivator to aspire to the conqueror role.
- 2022-09-01 Roblox Creators [0:05](https://youtu.be/KD3YTfRH6fQ?t=5): Start players vulnerable with limited map visibility (fog) so early progression feels like conquering the unknown.
- 2022-10-08 GnomeCode [8:07](https://youtu.be/QLoF9Er8Oo8?t=487): Store room metadata (direction, stairs, weight) in a RoomInfo ModuleScript table keyed by room name so rules can be data-driven.
- 2022-10-08 GnomeCode [16:15](https://youtu.be/QLoF9Er8Oo8?t=975): Use integer weights per item and sum them; a weight of 0.1 makes a rare room very unlikely while common pieces get higher numbers.
- 2022-10-08 GnomeCode [17:37](https://youtu.be/QLoF9Er8Oo8?t=1057): Weighted random pick: sum all weights, roll math.random between 1 and total, then walk the table accumulating weight until the roll is met.
- 2022-10-08 GnomeCode [16:55](https://youtu.be/QLoF9Er8Oo8?t=1015): Every entry needs a weight value (even the start room set to 0) or the total-weight sum will break.
- 2022-10-22 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/nextpath-next-gen-script-for-nextbots/2030360): Avoid relying solely on Humanoid:MoveTo() for NPC chasing; it makes NPCs get stuck on walls. Use pathfinding for long range and switch to MoveTo only when close.
- 2022-11-17 Roblox Creators [2:05](https://youtu.be/2kUFhwfuZ-w?t=125): For older audiences, offer simple tools that are easy to learn but hide deep mechanics players can discover and master over time.
- 2022-11-17 Roblox Creators [2:05](https://youtu.be/2kUFhwfuZ-w?t=125): Younger players see pay-to-win items as shortcuts to skill; older players resent them. Design monetisation with the target age group in mind.
- 2022-11-22 Roblox Creators [13:06](https://youtu.be/xXIc-p9hatU?t=786): Build direct skill-based competition (aim, precise movement) for older audiences rather than click-to-level mechanics.
- 2022-11-22 Roblox Creators [18:06](https://youtu.be/xXIc-p9hatU?t=1086): Layer a rich meta system with resources to manage on top of simple inputs, so players can optimise reward pacing.
- 2022-11-22 Roblox Creators [18:48](https://youtu.be/xXIc-p9hatU?t=1128): Keep the mastery curve high; if it's too low, players feel they've mastered the game quickly and leave.
- 2022-11-22 Roblox Creators [21:14](https://youtu.be/xXIc-p9hatU?t=1274): Balance high skill ceiling against new players; veteran dominance can drive newcomers away, so plan matchmaking or skill separation.
- 2022-11-22 Roblox Creators [25:16](https://youtu.be/xXIc-p9hatU?t=1516): Support emergent, silly play (trick shots, unintended mechanics) and treat them as features if fun, bugs if not.
- 2022-11-22 Roblox Creators [40:17](https://youtu.be/xXIc-p9hatU?t=2417): Provide simple tools that have hidden depth to discover and master; easy to play, difficult to master.
- 2022-11-22 Roblox Creators [50:54](https://youtu.be/xXIc-p9hatU?t=3054): Ensure most players get at least some success; guarantee a floor such as a kill-streak reward to avoid one-sided matches.
- 2022-11-22 Roblox Creators [52:15](https://youtu.be/xXIc-p9hatU?t=3135): Give everyone the same random weapon order in RNG modes so bad pulls feel fair; older players value consistency.
- 2022-11-22 Roblox Creators [55:00](https://youtu.be/xXIc-p9hatU?t=3300): Tweak weapons to fit playstyles rather than raw power; baseline weapons should compete with later unlocks to prevent power creep.
- 2022-11-24 GameMakers [10:11](https://youtu.be/NaR3-VIg5-I?t=611): Avoid grind walls and pay-to-win. Let players try each content type, then offer a la carte purchases of the types they enjoy.
- 2022-12-01 Roblox Creators [27:38](https://youtu.be/0PEBFsguCdw?t=1658): Ensure content is replayable and the core gameplay loop is fun on its own; sustainability matters more than update frequency.
- 2022-12-15 GnomeCode [7:38](https://youtu.be/R-xurNJtx7M?t=458): Lerp lets you change the target mid-movement, which TweenService cannot do; use it for dynamic, on-the-fly motion.
- 2022-12-15 Roblox Creators [1:30](https://youtu.be/bEEd75mnBIg?t=90): Design each weapon to feel unique and fill a specific niche; pair with attachments that push guns further in those distinct directions.
- 2023-01-12 Roblox Creators [0:00](https://youtu.be/okAygI1sxsA?t=0): Use in-game currencies to reward players for actions (e.g. cop paychecks, criminal robbery payouts), since you cannot give Robux rewards.
- 2023-01-12 Roblox Creators [0:40](https://youtu.be/okAygI1sxsA?t=40): Split progression into separate currencies per track (e.g. XP for one team, cash for another) so players can progress on multiple lines.
- 2023-01-29 SmartyRBX [1:16](https://youtu.be/DOMuVNlHv8Y?t=76): Make the game fun first; mid-quality builds can still be popular with strong gameplay. Polish visuals afterwards.
- 2023-02-08 SmartyRBX [0:16](https://youtu.be/rdWyuDEpGyk?t=16): Make the game bug-free, good-looking, easy to understand, with clear objectives and always something for players to do, so they have fun and invite friends.
- 2023-02-23 Roblox Creators [1:30](https://youtu.be/BsyU-SJ9L-c?t=90): Build one universal UI that scales to all devices instead of separate PC and mobile UIs; separate UIs are hard to maintain. Some devs use up to seven scaled UIs; avoid that.
- 2023-02-23 Roblox Creators [6:47](https://youtu.be/BsyU-SJ9L-c?t=407): Show as little UI as possible; move shops, quests and destinations into the physical world, but make them discoverable.
- 2023-02-23 Roblox Creators [39:35](https://youtu.be/BsyU-SJ9L-c?t=2375): Keep design consistent: buttons should look the same everywhere so players learn the interface once.
- 2023-02-23 Roblox Creators [27:02](https://youtu.be/BsyU-SJ9L-c?t=1622): Design mobile first since it is the lowest spec; if it works on mobile it will work on other devices.
- 2023-02-23 Roblox Creators [6:04](https://youtu.be/BsyU-SJ9L-c?t=364): Mobile is often 70-80% of players if optimized; treat UI/UX as more important than first-time user experience.
- 2023-03-02 Roblox Creators [4:28](https://youtu.be/PjwJb_nSePY?t=268): Long-press to reveal extra info is hidden; only put non-critical details behind it, and show critical info directly.
- 2023-03-02 Roblox Creators [9:48](https://youtu.be/PjwJb_nSePY?t=588): Keep PC features on mobile by merging menus rather than cutting them; only cut movement controls that don't apply to touch (e.g. slide).
- 2023-03-09 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/guide-to-making-better-horror-experiences/2212571): Let gameplay carry the game over story; learn pacing, plot and characters before writing one.
- 2023-03-16 Roblox Creators [0:05](https://youtu.be/CmXyiJw5N_Y?t=5): Design spawn and lobby space for user-to-user interaction; players should naturally meet, trade and talk. Tasty Farms cut 25 scattered plots into one clustered town, which felt far more social.
- 2023-03-16 Roblox Creators [0:46](https://youtu.be/CmXyiJw5N_Y?t=46): Keep the level cohesive with your core mechanics. A strong movement system wasted on a level that doesn't complement it is a common flaw, especially in FPS games.
- 2023-03-31 AlexDev [1:24](https://youtu.be/bYyD6tyz6Dg?t=84): Skip manual stat allocation if you want low-friction progression; grant stat boosts via items and buffs so players never feel they picked a wrong build.
- 2023-03-31 AlexDev [2:22](https://youtu.be/bYyD6tyz6Dg?t=142): Use one shared stat system for both players and enemies so the same numbers balance both sides.
- 2023-04-02 Itz_FloppyFish [2:26](https://youtu.be/H6LsbS_SA2w?t=146): Tune the respawn delay per coin (e.g. 5 seconds, or 120 for two minutes) to control how often the same collectible reappears.
- 2023-04-02 Itz_FloppyFish [3:04](https://youtu.be/H6LsbS_SA2w?t=184): Vary the reward value by area (e.g. 1 currency in area one, 10 in area two) to scale rewards as players progress.
- 2023-04-12 Mulch [9:38](https://youtu.be/6perjdMg3T4?t=578): Leaderboard scores grew too large and crashed players' scores; cap or rescale currency rewards early.
- 2023-04-15 Roox4 [7:28](https://youtu.be/Pbrvfdn9UN8?t=448): Tune enemy difficulty by iterating numbers in playtests; the creator raised minigun fire rate until it was borderline unbeatable.
- 2023-05-07 1Coal [2:01](https://youtu.be/JzB8fpyofnw?t=121): Make boost items clearly labeled as part of the core system so players know they can use them to improve their run.
- 2023-05-18 Roblox Creators [36:37](https://youtu.be/K1pEI6LVJJA?t=2197): Offer options to lower or disable background music and sound effects; many players with cognitive disabilities find overlapping sounds overwhelming.
- 2023-05-18 Roblox Creators [41:43](https://youtu.be/K1pEI6LVJJA?t=2503): Make keybinds fully customizable rather than one fixed scheme, since disability needs vary and abled players also have preferences.
- 2023-05-18 Roblox Creators [43:45](https://youtu.be/K1pEI6LVJJA?t=2625): Support both WASD and arrow keys for movement, and avoid requiring simultaneous key presses or hard-to-reach keys like F-keys.
- 2023-05-18 Roblox Creators [39:39](https://youtu.be/K1pEI6LVJJA?t=2379): Avoid designs requiring simultaneous move and shoot or fast precise inputs; test with abled and disabled players because a build system can work for one and fail for the other.
- 2023-05-18 Roblox Creators [44:26](https://youtu.be/K1pEI6LVJJA?t=2666): Enable keyboard UI navigation and test it; ensure every modal has a clear close action and menu actions have keybinds.
- 2023-05-18 Roblox Creators [40:21](https://youtu.be/K1pEI6LVJJA?t=2421): Make buttons larger and controls simple so players with motor or precision difficulties can play at their own pace without feeling rushed.
- 2023-05-18 Roblox Creators [52:23](https://youtu.be/K1pEI6LVJJA?t=3143): Limit camera shake and provide a disable option or intensity slider, since strong shake makes the game hard to see for everyone.
- 2023-05-25 Roblox Creators [19:32](https://youtu.be/5Ex0tW-n9_w?t=1172): Minimize text in gameplay: use arrows, pointers, and images to guide players through tasks, and show actions rather than explaining them.
- 2023-06-08 Roblox Creators [2:50](https://youtu.be/pWF06iX9_Gg?t=170): Explain hard concepts with real-world metaphors; this lowered failure rates after rewording.
- 2023-06-09 SmartyRBX [0:00](https://youtu.be/gc7K98RlOik?t=0): Plan your game around a core loop: an endless cycle of actions and rewards that keeps players hooked. Most successful games repeat the same core actions throughout.
- 2023-06-09 SmartyRBX [2:02](https://youtu.be/gc7K98RlOik?t=122): Build a core loop in three steps: identify the main action players do most often, define the reward for it, then create a reason to repeat it.
- 2023-06-09 SmartyRBX [2:02](https://youtu.be/gc7K98RlOik?t=122): Extend a basic loop with branches and variations, but keep the underlying action-reward-repeat cycle the same.
- 2023-07-31 Amora [8:29](https://youtu.be/KPK86YQ6t-Y?t=509): Check music.IsPlaying before calling Play to avoid restarting the track every frame.
- 2023-08-02 Roblox Creators [4:56](https://youtu.be/XfxbdKtSbOI?t=296): Teach the core loop: the repeated actions players do to progress, and why they should spend time doing them.
- 2023-08-02 Roblox Creators [6:48](https://youtu.be/XfxbdKtSbOI?t=408): Increase difficulty and time required for progression over time, paired with commensurate rewards, to avoid boredom.
- 2023-08-02 Roblox Creators [9:07](https://youtu.be/XfxbdKtSbOI?t=547): Give goals achievable in the next few sessions plus long-term goals taking weeks or months, and make them clear.
- 2023-08-07 Roblox Creators [0:08](https://youtu.be/gkFKF9A-snY?t=8): Define your core loop as three parts: minute-to-minute interaction, the most repeated action, and the progression engine that drives the player forward.
- 2023-08-07 Roblox Creators [0:48](https://youtu.be/gkFKF9A-snY?t=48): Make sure the core loop works for every role or team in the game; Jailbreak's police and criminals share the same progression engine (buying weapons and vehicles).
- 2023-08-18 Roblox Creators [3:16](https://youtu.be/kNE757v3Bwg?t=196): Design every update around the core loop. If new content doesn't fit into a part of the core loop, cut it.
- 2023-08-18 Roblox Creators [14:06](https://youtu.be/kNE757v3Bwg?t=846): Add competitive systems such as PvP, leaderboards, and tournaments to give players a reason to compete.
- 2023-08-18 Roblox Creators [14:45](https://youtu.be/kNE757v3Bwg?t=885): Create short, mid, and long-term player goals through collections and achievements, so players always know what to do next.
- 2023-08-18 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-successfully-grow-your-game-ft-senseideveloper-vikinglaw-promoting-and-advertising-your-game/2535279): Build a satisfying, simple core loop first; Sensei_Developer says graphics don't make a good game, core gameplay does.
- 2023-08-18 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-successfully-grow-your-game-ft-senseideveloper-vikinglaw-promoting-and-advertising-your-game/2535279): Keep players always doing something; constant objectives drive engagement and return play.
- 2023-08-25 1Coal [1:04](https://youtu.be/YBSsksqfxo0?t=64): Add real stakes such as losing loot or dying, since games without stakes or failure states feel boring to watch and play.
- 2023-08-25 Roblox Creators [13:18](https://youtu.be/lOOvOIJoMi4?t=798): For unfamiliar mechanics, pick a metaphor players already know (e.g. a deck of cards for spell casting in Spellbound) and prototype it with players.
- 2023-08-25 Roblox Creators [15:20](https://youtu.be/lOOvOIJoMi4?t=920): Hunt friction: allow multi-quantity purchases of consumables and cut lengthy animations or extra navigation between inventory and shop.
- 2023-08-29 Suphi Kaner [42:29](https://youtu.be/OAZvYqbzOWI?t=2549): Apply TweenService:GetValue to a normalised alpha to add easing (bounce, exponential) to any looping path movement.
- 2023-08-29 janisjanis01 [2:02](https://youtu.be/fRBNHaMtvFs?t=122): Add replayability via rebirths, rounds or leaderboards so players return to earn more items after finishing.
- 2023-09-06 Roblox Creators [1:21](https://youtu.be/N2l0BDgSNtQ?t=81): Find the bottleneck where players get stuck in the core loop (e.g. resource gathering too hard) and fix it by lowering the cost or increasing the resource supply.
- 2023-09-08 Roblox Creators [2:35](https://youtu.be/LpAU6TheAZ4?t=155): Scale challenges to match player progression (e.g. enemies grow with upgraded gear) so long-term play has a reason to continue.
- 2023-09-19 Roblox [5:09](https://youtu.be/9eaev-ypgWo?t=309): Track anti-objectives (e.g. getting caught) as a risk signal, but check whether players return later before cutting them.
- 2023-09-19 Roblox [8:22](https://youtu.be/9eaev-ypgWo?t=502): Story-driven games need constant big updates; Piggy found weekly chapter drops left players replaying little, so add replay hooks.
- 2023-09-19 Roblox [9:02](https://youtu.be/9eaev-ypgWo?t=542): Serve two player types: story enjoyers and gameplay gamers. Piggy added collectibles, multiple endings and unlockable abilities for both.
- 2023-09-19 Roblox [31:38](https://youtu.be/JjkAaXVULEk?t=1898): Add hard, aspirational achievements (e.g., Doors' 1000 secret rooms) that creators will grind and film for content.
- 2023-09-19 Roblox [6:36](https://youtu.be/UVq0045HNQ4?t=396): Build high fidelity around four pillars: immersive world, responsive character feel, real-world-like details, and a sense of always something new.
- 2023-10-20 TheDankDeveloper [2:44](https://youtu.be/DJsRZ2FfoMk?t=164): Make the game enjoyable: an obby alone is not enough; add a currency (e.g. wins) to buy fun things in-game, since enjoyment drives players and Robux.
- 2023-10-25 TheDankDeveloper [2:53](https://youtu.be/OR7xEm4iaO8?t=173): Scale enemy waves so players must add towers over rounds; the speaker found towers were overwhelmed by round nine with no added defenses.
- 2023-11-26 Roox4 [4:27](https://youtu.be/kKiPL7wc8Rc?t=267): Add pickups (speed boost, stamina, health, tokens) to balance difficulty and give players relief between threats.
- 2023-11-26 Roox4 [6:26](https://youtu.be/kKiPL7wc8Rc?t=386): Punish edge camping with targeted threats so players must keep moving and the map stays dynamic.
- 2023-11-26 Roox4 [6:42](https://youtu.be/kKiPL7wc8Rc?t=402): Make random spawns less luck-dependent by targeting spawns near players, which created the main difficulty spike.
- 2023-12-12 TheDankDeveloper [2:46](https://youtu.be/1jRaSYhrEcU?t=166): Balance pet drop rates and damage values early; the speaker noted the lack of a rarity system and that the game needs balancing.
- 2023-12-17 Last Level Studios [5:26](https://youtu.be/kQ6JMjjGvow?t=326): Aim for a multiplayer experience that satisfies both enthusiasts and casual players; SCR's broad appeal was a deliberate design goal.
- 2023-12-31 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/understand-the-roblox-algorithm/2772921): Give players goals at three horizons: short-term (next game/kill), mid-term (new weapon/skin/challenge via quests), long-term (season pass, best item).
- 2023-12-31 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/understand-the-roblox-algorithm/2772921): Make the game feel reactive with sound effects and UI animations; without feedback (enemy damage, building progress) players may leave confused.
- 2024-02-16 Stewiepfing [3:27](https://youtu.be/v8tFfBTr6M8?t=207): Keep the core game simple for young audiences, but leave deeper mechanics for players to discover and research, which signals investment.
- 2024-02-16 Stewiepfing [4:50](https://youtu.be/v8tFfBTr6M8?t=290): Pair short, frequent small goals (next egg, currency threshold) with big optional goals (huge pet, leaderboard rank, completing the game).
- 2024-02-16 Stewiepfing [6:28](https://youtu.be/v8tFfBTr6M8?t=388): Give strong feedback on every achievement (sound effects, checkmarks, particle fireworks) to create dopamine hits that keep players playing.
- 2024-02-16 Stewiepfing [7:44](https://youtu.be/v8tFfBTr6M8?t=464): Build replayability through multiple worlds, many pets/currencies to unlock, and varied content such as new opponents or multiple endings.
- 2024-02-17 B Ricey [9:23](https://youtu.be/FaEMgVDlaNQ?t=563): Have at least four more plot spawn points than max players, since Roblox can reallocate players and spawn extras, otherwise players get errors or default spawns.
- 2024-02-18 SmartyRBX [8:23](https://youtu.be/kUNFgrvUkj4?t=503): Build dopamine loops: timed rewards (e.g. pet at 3 min, random prize at 10 min) keep players chasing goals.
- 2024-02-18 SmartyRBX [14:17](https://youtu.be/kUNFgrvUkj4?t=857): Add VFX, particles and sounds on every action as feedback; they act as rewards and confirm the player's action was good.
- 2024-02-18 SmartyRBX [3:49](https://youtu.be/kUNFgrvUkj4?t=229): Lean into destruction gameplay (things breaking/falling apart) with more buildings; a gap he sees on Roblox today.
- 2024-02-28 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/discovery-on-roblox-past-present-and-future-vision/2859111): Prioritise craftsmanship, quality, performance, device compatibility, localisation and good game design, since Discovery optimises for engagement and retention.
- 2024-03-01 MonzterDEV [5:30](https://youtu.be/em15yK5RjBk?t=330): Roblox says quality correlates with investment in updates and events, and rewards player love measured by time and money spent.
- 2024-03-12 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/howto-physics-based-tweenservice/2873727): Plain TweenService on moving platforms makes players slide off; drive the movement per frame and feed velocity to touching parts instead.
- 2024-03-14 1Coal [0:52](https://youtu.be/gWNOANEPYLY?t=52): Get a working prototype out fast, then judge whether it's fun by playtesting; a base plate test felt off and the project was nearly shelved.
- 2024-03-14 1Coal [3:44](https://youtu.be/gWNOANEPYLY?t=224): Give the hunted side a head start (3 seconds) so the asymmetric round feels fair.
- 2024-03-30 B Ricey [0:26](https://youtu.be/1XJRZ1x2x4g?t=26): Validate placement with a bounding box: transform the object's four corner points into plot local space and check abs(x) and abs(z) against half plot size, instead of full mesh collision.
- 2024-03-30 ByteBlox [5:11](https://youtu.be/lJ_ZwfNHkQU?t=311): Create a Folder named exactly 'leaderstats' under each Player to show values in the default leaderboard; add IntValue children for each stat.
- 2024-03-30 ByteBlox [8:11](https://youtu.be/lJ_ZwfNHkQU?t=491): Use math.random(1,10) equal to 1 for a 10% chance; reset parts to collidable and opaque each cycle before re-rolling.
- 2024-04-14 janisjanis01 [5:10](https://youtu.be/7z8XlhAtGpQ?t=310): Verify egg drop chances after editing; a mistake showed Common at 3% and Legendary at 55%, so check the odds in play.
- 2024-04-14 ByteBlox [12:01](https://youtu.be/RWl6zQYanOA?t=721): Store upgrade state as a multiplier value on the player and scale both reward and next upgrade price by it (price = base x multiplier) for an escalating grind.
- 2024-04-14 ByteBlox [0:00](https://youtu.be/RWl6zQYanOA?t=0): Simulator core loop: give the player a currency, multiply its earn rate via purchases, and keep the grind endless; the speaker says this is why these games are popular.
- 2024-04-23 Stewiepfing [9:24](https://youtu.be/qMdRT1xf9GM?t=564): Stagger orb spawns with a short task.wait (~0.15s) so currency pops out one at a time instead of all at once, which looks better.
- 2024-04-23 Stewiepfing [8:42](https://youtu.be/qMdRT1xf9GM?t=522): Make collected-feedback orbs non-collidable and anchored after they settle so players can't get stuck touching them.
- 2024-05-11 ByteBlox [5:07](https://youtu.be/UUgj-hmX2LM?t=307): Define rarity as weights summing to 100 (e.g. legendary 1, epic 19, rare 30, common 50) so the odds are easy to read and tune.
- 2024-05-11 ByteBlox [9:13](https://youtu.be/UUgj-hmX2LM?t=553): Removing the spawn delay produced several legendaries quickly in testing; a spawn-rate change visibly alters how often rare items appear.
- 2024-06-04 AlvinBlox [12:34](https://youtu.be/5YNFRCD0pd0?t=754): Use task.delay to revert temporary effects like walkspeed after a set duration without yielding the purchase function.
- 2024-06-10 ByteBlox [0:41](https://youtu.be/_rksdF5JoHo?t=41): Make death meaningful: harsh-but-fair death makes the risk-taking before it interesting, as in Minecraft. Trivial respawns remove the tension that gives earlier play its stakes.
- 2024-06-10 ByteBlox [11:16](https://youtu.be/_rksdF5JoHo?t=676): Give dead players something to do: ghost states that unlock abilities or new interactions keep them playing 24/7 instead of waiting for respawn. Roblox games should always have an activity available.
- 2024-06-10 ByteBlox [19:31](https://youtu.be/_rksdF5JoHo?t=1171): Consider a death-loop structure: death resets the world but keeps the player's knowledge, so later runs go further. Outer Wilds and Undertale show this, and Doors teaches cues through death.
- 2024-06-10 ByteBlox [22:54](https://youtu.be/_rksdF5JoHo?t=1374): Store player knowledge in a persistent ship-log-style UI so information gained across loops stays visible and players can solve clues without re-reading everything.
- 2024-06-16 janisjanis01 [0:40](https://youtu.be/NWyK_PbFdxE?t=40): Make the odds improve as the player plays, as Murder Mystery 2 does with murderer chance, so players keep playing to reach a better chance.
- 2024-06-16 janisjanis01 [2:48](https://youtu.be/NWyK_PbFdxE?t=168): Keep the game uncompletable. Trading in Pet Simulator 99 lets players keep chasing better items after maxing out.
- 2024-06-23 janisjanis01 [9:14](https://youtu.be/e725ByFD8EI?t=554): Gate areas behind a unique entry, such as a water slide into the ocean zone, to give each world a distinct arrival moment.
- 2024-06-24 ByteBlox [4:19](https://youtu.be/ekM2z9FMhZc?t=259): Store per-item data (cost, cash per second, owned amount) as Attributes on a template frame and clone it, so each item shares structure but has different values.
- 2024-06-24 ByteBlox [11:53](https://youtu.be/ekM2z9FMhZc?t=713): Run a task.wait(1) loop that iterates owned items and adds amount x cash-per-second to the player's total each tick to create passive income.
- 2024-06-30 janisjanis01 [0:43](https://youtu.be/JeyYzEjPjc8?t=43): Nerf overpowered progression early; the creator slightly reduced the rebirth multiplier by 0.01 because it compounds at 400,000 rebirths.
- 2024-07-06 TheDevBacon [2:51](https://youtu.be/_XJVDRr2ZuA?t=171): Build a game that deserves players; promotion will not retain players if the gameplay is weak.
- 2024-07-19 Mulch [0:40](https://youtu.be/y9Ii0VEFaEU?t=40): In race obbies, make all paths start from the same front line so the race is fair.
- 2024-08-12 GameMakers [13:13](https://youtu.be/gp_hDijjC4U?t=793): Let community desire guide which cosmetics you release; items the community ignores sell noticeably worse.
- 2024-08-13 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-actually-make-a-popular-game/3115791): Focus on why players leave and what feels boring; the author says this is the best way to raise playtime.
- 2024-08-17 Daiorg [3:33](https://youtu.be/Yku_51UE_GM?t=213): Generate rooms randomly from many variants with several routes each, so runs feel unpredictable and replayable.
- 2024-08-17 Daiorg [6:27](https://youtu.be/Yku_51UE_GM?t=387): Give each threat a distinct mechanic and counter (e.g. stare-based entity, light-triggered squid, stalker), rather than repeating one scare type.
- 2024-08-17 Daiorg [7:50](https://youtu.be/Yku_51UE_GM?t=470): Reveal lore through death: each death unlocks info about what killed you, rewarding failure and giving reasons to retry.
- 2024-09-20 GnomeCode [6:49](https://youtu.be/SUZPuQrKJuU?t=409): Add a dedicated voting stage between intermission and gameplay with its own countdown (example: 5 seconds).
- 2024-09-20 GnomeCode [19:07](https://youtu.be/SUZPuQrKJuU?t=1147): Reset player health to max when teleporting between rounds so damage does not carry over into the next map, especially for fighting games.
- 2024-09-23 Roblox [11:38](https://youtu.be/DeWXxpnoe4U?t=698): Design console gameplay for longer sessions: console users spend 50% more time per session than mobile, so reward depth rather than quick re-engagement.
- 2024-09-23 Roblox [12:19](https://youtu.be/DeWXxpnoe4U?t=739): Add deeper content and secondary activities (e.g. player customization) so players stay in the game during breaks from the main loop.
- 2024-09-23 Roblox [14:23](https://youtu.be/DeWXxpnoe4U?t=863): Mix short and long progression goals: short goals (~10 min) suit mobile, long goals (1-2+ hours) give console players a strong sense of accomplishment.
- 2024-09-23 Roblox [27:11](https://youtu.be/DeWXxpnoe4U?t=1631): Copy familiar control schemes from existing games for the genre rather than reinventing them; players want to use what they already know.
- 2024-09-23 Roblox [24:28](https://youtu.be/DeWXxpnoe4U?t=1468): Bridge skill gaps across platforms: gunfight Arena uses a weaker assist on console and stronger on mobile, and matches console players in console lobbies.
- 2024-09-23 Roblox [30:05](https://youtu.be/LMUQpm0HpEQ?t=1805): Use parties as an intentional discovery mechanic, e.g. teleport options or group-only secret areas that require collaboration.
- 2024-09-23 Roblox [11:08](https://youtu.be/QC6XWgJRJEM?t=668): Make the spawn route guide players to core loop mechanics and make minigame entry obvious; this drove ~110% session time increase.
- 2024-09-23 Roblox [13:17](https://youtu.be/QC6XWgJRJEM?t=797): Let players collect rewards inside the progression area; Swordburst 3 floor-reward staircase drop-off smoothed after rewards became collectable in-tower.
- 2024-09-23 Roblox [8:50](https://youtu.be/T7w3Q54JOPE?t=530): For Japan, localize heavily; English is less prevalent, and local content and culture shape top charts. Consider train/train-simulation and arcade sports like volleyball and soccer.
- 2024-09-23 Roblox [12:13](https://youtu.be/vWx8UTxsi0A?t=733): Identify main gameplay areas (landmarks) and check travel time between them so players don't lose interest or get lost.
- 2024-09-23 Roblox [15:58](https://youtu.be/vWx8UTxsi0A?t=958): Treat each location as a movie scene with its own lighting mood so the player's journey progresses from beginning to end.
- 2024-09-23 Roblox [26:50](https://youtu.be/vWx8UTxsi0A?t=1610): Avoid saturated colors that conflict with game signals; players read red as lava and green as safe, so test colors in playtests.
- 2024-09-24 ByteBlox [5:16](https://youtu.be/Issrodr_wK8?t=316): Add small juice to even a trivial game (bounce, color change, progress, aura); the speaker says it takes about 20-30 minutes for a beginner.
- 2024-09-29 ByteBlox [16:48](https://youtu.be/Nzrtg8SGOY0?t=1008): Only add a progress bar (durability, hunger, oxygen) if it ties into other systems and creates meaningful decisions; a bar that only punishes with no reward is just annoying.
- 2024-09-29 ByteBlox [21:43](https://youtu.be/Nzrtg8SGOY0?t=1303): Don't fake progression with exponentially growing numbers (x2/x3 costs) when you have little content; use flat difficulty increases and add new content for each stage.
- 2024-09-29 ByteBlox [30:08](https://youtu.be/Nzrtg8SGOY0?t=1808): Replace currency-locked walls with natural gates such as vehicle or equipment limits (Subnautica crush depth, jetpack fuel) so the world feels open and unrestricted.
- 2024-09-29 ByteBlox [38:35](https://youtu.be/Nzrtg8SGOY0?t=2315): Avoid soft-locks from nonrenewable items; provide an in-game way to restart or access all content (Grounded-style restart keeping progress) instead of requiring a new save.
- 2024-09-29 ByteBlox [41:43](https://youtu.be/Nzrtg8SGOY0?t=2503): Give every upgradeable feature real upgrade tiers and playtest them at the relevant progression level so players don't hit a capped, tedious baseline.
- 2024-09-29 ByteBlox [47:10](https://youtu.be/Nzrtg8SGOY0?t=2830): In tycoons, don't attach cash rewards to manual minigames like obbies; it forces players to stay engaged when the point is automation.
- 2024-10-12 GnomeCode [1:31](https://youtu.be/H9CbgCe47Mo?t=91): Don't over-worry about difficulty; keep players engaged with next milestones instead, as in Pet Simulator, since too-hard moments make players leave.
- 2024-10-12 GnomeCode [11:01](https://youtu.be/H9CbgCe47Mo?t=661): Ensure real gameplay exists early and is fun; a basic obby with more gameplay beats a flashy game with little to do.
- 2024-10-12 waveshine [1:19](https://youtu.be/zW0VdnOqxMk?t=79): Use a shared global timer that players can shorten by waiting, spending Robux, or completing activities, creating a reason to keep playing.
- 2024-10-13 Suphi Kaner [0:00](https://youtu.be/AkF-4x7qfzM?t=0): Keep each keyframe rotation delta under 180°; animations always take the shortest path, so a 360° jump between keys does not move at all.
- 2024-10-13 Suphi Kaner [41:31](https://youtu.be/AkF-4x7qfzM?t=2491): Check animation marker times (stored as strings) with tostring or tonumber before comparing; string vs number mismatches break logic silently.
- 2024-10-27 janisjanis01 [5:07](https://youtu.be/ZyQ73kfh_b4?t=307): Give simulators depth: exploration of maps, secret codes, rare pets, and a currency that buys gamepasses kept players engaged in the sequel.
