- 2024-10-28 waveshine [1:25](https://youtu.be/3BHo5a_OF5U?t=85): Raise session time: obbies need plenty of stages; simulators/incrementals must not have slow progression.
- 2024-11-01 Roox4 [4:31](https://youtu.be/LC8AV8Yy6Z4?t=271): Design each map around the core chaos goal, but test concepts early; hanging-rope platforms and similar ideas were discarded after play testing.
- 2024-11-01 Roox4 [6:37](https://youtu.be/LC8AV8Yy6Z4?t=397): Add movement options (sprint, dash, launch pads) when players die too often on foot; the game became more forgiving and traversal less frustrating.
- 2024-11-01 Roox4 [7:18](https://youtu.be/LC8AV8Yy6Z4?t=438): Check the mood of a theme in playtests; a testing-facility theme felt eerie and unfun, so the setting was replaced with a simpler observation room.
- 2024-11-02 SimTek Game Development [0:00](https://youtu.be/zimfV0QYV2w?t=0): Gate a mechanic on player speed (linear velocity magnitude > ~20 studs/s) so knockback only triggers when running fast, not during normal movement.
- 2024-11-02 SimTek Game Development [4:09](https://youtu.be/zimfV0QYV2w?t=249): Make a speed pellet using a neon, anchored, non-collidable part with CanTouch and CanQuery on, then destroy it on touch after setting WalkSpeed (e.g. 16 to 50).
- 2024-12-01 Mulch [8:16](https://youtu.be/tdnhCXx3srA?t=496): Auto-farm and auto-hatch tools shift the loop from clicking to waiting; the creator says idle play made progression feel much faster, a pattern to design around.
- 2024-12-01 Mulch [25:10](https://youtu.be/tdnhCXx3srA?t=1510): Rebirth resets progress but grants permanent multipliers and unlocks; the creator frames rebirths as the main long-term progression gate.
- 2025-01-05 janisjanis01 [9:15](https://youtu.be/EpPU3Am8lgE?t=555): Replace passive coin-walking with active interaction (click drops to damage them, then collect); creator calls it a huge upgrade.
- 2025-01-05 janisjanis01 [9:15](https://youtu.be/EpPU3Am8lgE?t=555): Tie progression to purchasable bags that raise both strength and Robux multiplier, giving a clear upgrade path.
- 2025-01-15 CallMeDogey [4:37](https://youtu.be/USJC5l_wpuY?t=277): Clicker core loop: click for money, buy pets, unlock new zones. Keep it simple and playable before polishing.
- 2025-01-15 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/i-made-a-planetaryspherical-gravity-system/3383865): Mimic the default Roblox humanoid controller's feel when building custom character controllers; the author says this made the movement satisfying.
- 2025-01-17 PineappleDev [1:54](https://youtu.be/OzqyuEoN7t8?t=114): Add a moving hazard (sweeping kill bricks) to a simple climb so the repeated task stays a little interesting.
- 2025-01-17 PineappleDev [2:35](https://youtu.be/OzqyuEoN7t8?t=155): Put a rewarding payoff at the end of a long climb (platform, carpet, gold pile) so the effort feels worth it.
- 2025-01-24 ByteBlox [20:12](https://youtu.be/SCtYVCRVzM8?t=1212): Store platform/coin count in one variable and compare leaderstats coin value to it to detect the win condition; makes the game easy to resize.
- 2025-01-27 ByteBlox [10:48](https://youtu.be/RkgxOf3iZfA?t=648): Use ProximityPrompt with a hold duration (e.g. 0.8s) for collection; tune hold time to control how fast the loop feels.
- 2025-01-27 ByteBlox [15:14](https://youtu.be/RkgxOf3iZfA?t=914): Detect completion by listening to ChildRemoved on the collectibles folder and checking remaining children count.
- 2025-01-29 JustDucksey [1:29](https://youtu.be/jKyzG9HPrRM?t=89): Prototype pickup systems with temporary parts first; the dev found scripting all-in-one pickup logic caused items to constantly respawn, so test separate approaches.
- 2025-01-29 JustDucksey [3:33](https://youtu.be/jKyzG9HPrRM?t=213): Simplify mechanics where possible, e.g. replant the crop itself instead of adding a separate seed item, to keep the game easy to understand.
- 2025-01-31 ByteBlox [16:52](https://youtu.be/mpv1SyfcpGA?t=1012): Tie tycoon progress to the story so upgrading the base feels like helping the character, creating emotional attachment to the core loop.
- 2025-02-01 ByteBlox [4:06](https://youtu.be/o-8V99horro?t=246): Roll rarity from the rarest tier down: check legendary first, then epic, rare, common, using one random number from 1 to chance. Checking common first breaks the odds.
- 2025-02-07 Duckable [4:46](https://youtu.be/OS-k2iOEfhM?t=286): Build a simple core loop: action, sell for currency, buy upgrades, unlock expansion. Make the loop satisfying enough to keep players hooked.
- 2025-02-07 Duckable [5:28](https://youtu.be/OS-k2iOEfhM?t=328): Give players repeatable actions, rewards for completing set tasks, and unlockable expansion or new content to sustain play.
- 2025-02-09 Daiorg [8:26](https://youtu.be/nutv8RT5UrQ?t=506): In two-player games, balance each player's role and difficulty so neither carries more weight, otherwise players get frustrated and cooperation drops.
- 2025-02-14 Duckable [4:51](https://youtu.be/6g8ZYtDKPBw?t=291): Give XP for placing items and costs for items to create a basic progression loop.
- 2025-02-22 GnomeCode [3:57](https://youtu.be/-oZIdFe8qHk?t=237): Vary room types; repeated identical rooms and fixed item spawns make exploration feel repetitive.
- 2025-02-22 GnomeCode [5:27](https://youtu.be/-oZIdFe8qHk?t=327): Skip slow elevator intros and drop players straight into the action when the genre allows it.
- 2025-02-22 GnomeCode [8:51](https://youtu.be/-oZIdFe8qHk?t=531): Avoid pacing where players spend minutes searching drawers for keys; keep the game moving with constant threat.
- 2025-02-22 GnomeCode [11:26](https://youtu.be/-oZIdFe8qHk?t=686): Add real danger to darkness mechanics; darkness that carries no consequence loses its pressure.
- 2025-02-22 GnomeCode [5:27](https://youtu.be/-oZIdFe8qHk?t=327): Fast movement and speedrun-style timing can make a horror game more replayable than slow creeping.
- 2025-03-04 PineappleDev [2:44](https://youtu.be/IEAr0I_sUV4?t=164): Replace paid respawn with milestone checkpoints: finish a stage section to unlock a checkpoint to the next, reducing frustration from forced Robux spending.
- 2025-03-04 PineappleDev [4:05](https://youtu.be/IEAr0I_sUV4?t=245): Add a earned in-game currency (e.g. +10 coins per 10 seconds played) as an alternative to Robux for respawns and trolls, letting free players progress.
- 2025-03-08 GnomeCode [7:00](https://youtu.be/u_8p65Bsm2Y?t=420): Smooth a sharp difficulty spike by adding an on-screen hint before the hard section so players are not instantly killed.
- 2025-03-08 GnomeCode [2:02](https://youtu.be/u_8p65Bsm2Y?t=122): Use per-checkpoint drop-off data to tune difficulty of later rooms, e.g. adjusting after seeing only ~26% finish.
- 2025-03-09 BloxxedDev [4:37](https://youtu.be/CipKM4DBWxg?t=277): Tie the horror to the player's mind by using the sanity meter as the core mechanic, with blur and colour shifts instead of monsters.
- 2025-03-15 ByteBlox [21:14](https://youtu.be/3rxZj5-Qx30?t=1274): Look into lag compensation / rollback techniques if building precise combat, since the server must validate hits against where the target was in the past.
- 2025-03-15 Duckable [1:38](https://youtu.be/I9eyeEG6dpc?t=98): For round-based games, use matchmaking that drops players straight into the next match with one click to avoid lobby waiting and keep retention high.
- 2025-03-15 Duckable [2:34](https://youtu.be/I9eyeEG6dpc?t=154): Add progression systems (per-weapon levels, unlockable cosmetics, paid upgrade currency) so players always have a next goal even after losses.
- 2025-03-15 Duckable [5:26](https://youtu.be/I9eyeEG6dpc?t=326): Require some player skill (learning curve, movement, aiming) rather than idle clicking or RNG rolling so achievements feel earned.
- 2025-03-18 ArTheDev [8:16](https://youtu.be/V7PdX03D_hM?t=496): On XP gain, carry overflow XP into the next level and raise the level's XP requirement each time so levels scale.
- 2025-03-21 PineappleDev [2:25](https://youtu.be/E6Bev4VCWeA?t=145): Held items lose collision with the player character, so players can walk through them; account for this if you want stacking or standing on objects.
- 2025-03-22 CallMeDogey [1:05](https://youtu.be/rHyiN2GV-J0?t=65): Use simple, cheap settings like low gravity and floating platforms to quickly give a space theme distinct feel.
- 2025-03-23 SmartyRBX [2:53](https://youtu.be/_wqZyaTEsHQ?t=173): Give players quick rewards and progression goals to work toward; simple tycoons, obbies and simulators perform well for this reason.
- 2025-03-29 BloxxedDev [2:04](https://youtu.be/y5aNv16bT3s?t=124): Make a physical mechanic depend on player stability, such as spill chance rising with stepping, distance or fast movement, so it stays tense.
- 2025-03-30 SmartyRBX [2:45](https://youtu.be/GPP0DzJGpXc?t=165): Add one proven feature (status, skins, upgrades, community, or simplicity) to your current game and measure how players react before building more.
- 2025-03-30 SmartyRBX [2:04](https://youtu.be/GPP0DzJGpXc?t=124): Make sure someone wants a system before investing heavily in it, e.g. before spending 200 hours on it.
- 2025-03-30 janisjanis01 [2:38](https://youtu.be/Pruto1wqxAU?t=158): Add a unique progression feature (e.g. plant-and-wait garden giving stat multipliers) to differentiate from other simulators.
- 2025-04-06 SmartyRBX [1:27](https://youtu.be/N2FND74tgGE?t=87): Show visible growth constantly (XP bars, multipliers, prestige) so every action feels like forward movement; the speaker says games feel dead by minute 10 otherwise.
- 2025-04-06 SmartyRBX [2:07](https://youtu.be/N2FND74tgGE?t=127): Give players rare, showable status items (skins, pets, achievements, leaderboard ranks, stats like total kills) to chase and flex.
- 2025-04-06 SmartyRBX [3:19](https://youtu.be/N2FND74tgGE?t=199): Include learnable systems with depth to master, such as crafting combos or hidden strategies, so players feel smart when they figure them out.
- 2025-04-06 SmartyRBX [4:59](https://youtu.be/N2FND74tgGE?t=299): Map each dopamine loop as trigger, action, reward; if a game lacks a clear one, it is missing its core design.
- 2025-04-06 Cumin [12:38](https://youtu.be/YmAwFbqUA0U?t=758): Give simulator-style loops real mechanical depth; reviewer argues a static loop with only a rising number feels hollow, unlike RPGs that add interacting systems and non-numeric challenges.
- 2025-04-06 Cumin [13:19](https://youtu.be/YmAwFbqUA0U?t=799): Add a fail state or challenges that can't be beaten by raw numerical strength; without them, players only have patience as the challenge.
- 2025-04-10 JustDucksey [2:49](https://youtu.be/BbNlDGBROdg?t=169): Give fuel a visible drain mechanic tied to driving so resource pressure is part of the core loop.
- 2025-04-10 BloxxedDev [1:23](https://youtu.be/RVZBjAUzf7o?t=83): Replace click-to-enter prompts with walk-in trigger zones; standing-on-platform detection proved unreliable, while a volume the player stands inside worked consistently.
- 2025-04-19 JustDucksey [1:28](https://youtu.be/qxE7JhikvwY?t=88): Start with a simple core loop like mine-and-sell with leaderstats; a basic first finished game still teaches core systems.
- 2025-04-19 JustDucksey [5:34](https://youtu.be/qxE7JhikvwY?t=334): Use a rarity system for collectible parts so some items are harder to find, giving the loop a reason to keep going.
- 2025-04-28 AlvinBlox [0:42](https://youtu.be/Le4QhC3dDGk?t=42): Build a core loop: a repeated action players find satisfying each time and that gives a reason to return tomorrow.
- 2025-04-28 AlvinBlox [1:25](https://youtu.be/Le4QhC3dDGk?t=85): Avoid games completable in one session; add replayability via leaderboards, daily challenges, modes, or varied opponents.
- 2025-04-28 AlvinBlox [0:42](https://youtu.be/Le4QhC3dDGk?t=42): Build a satisfying core loop: the repeated action players do over and over, which gives them a reason to return tomorrow.
- 2025-04-28 AlvinBlox [1:25](https://youtu.be/Le4QhC3dDGk?t=85): Avoid games completable in one session; add replayability via leaderboards, daily challenges, modes, or varied opponents so each join feels different.
- 2025-05-07 JustDucksey [6:10](https://youtu.be/o-8KX6XgC7s?t=370): Give regrowing crops (bushes) a separate production system that yields repeated harvests instead of one-time pickup.
- 2025-05-11 Cumin [9:38](https://youtu.be/2auVezIcNAE?t=578): Telegraph enemy attacks clearly (e.g. slow approach then pounce) so players can learn dodge timing quickly, per the BlockTales critique.
- 2025-05-11 Cumin [18:55](https://youtu.be/2auVezIcNAE?t=1135): Let players roam the world regardless of level and add collectibles that boost progression; the speaker praised Shadowvis for this.
- 2025-05-17 SimTek Game Development [1:02](https://youtu.be/10uZXhWd_Aw?t=62): Build a generic pickup script driven by part name so new resources (wood, grass, stone) are added by duplicating a part and renaming it.
- 2025-05-21 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/math-for-roblox-developers-1-vectors/3659072): Use .Unit for direction-only vectors (e.g. velocity or raycast direction) so magnitude does not vary with a long source vector.
- 2025-05-23 RoDev [9:30](https://youtu.be/Gw2FwndwnOg?t=570): Dev 5 found emerald was reachable in about 5 minutes; tune ore value and time-to-unlock against the progression curve before adding content.
- 2025-05-23 RoDev [10:51](https://youtu.be/Gw2FwndwnOg?t=651): Add something to do while waiting for timers to finish, as Grow a Garden does with fast permanent fruit, so idle time isn't dead time.
- 2025-05-29 CORSO [0:30](https://youtu.be/O2LZcQG5PPQ?t=30): Make progression costs reachable: exponential upgrade prices that take months to max can make a progression system feel unwinnable for most players.
- 2025-05-29 CORSO [4:28](https://youtu.be/O2LZcQG5PPQ?t=268): Make upgrades add new mechanics rather than only flat stat boosts, as BTD6's monkey knowledge tree does, to keep depth.
- 2025-06-07 BloxxedDev [1:15](https://youtu.be/Gk-iTN8Ru_Q?t=75): Expose growth speed, max height and base height as top-of-script constants so you can tune the growth feel without rewriting logic.
- 2025-06-07 AlvinBlox [1:57](https://youtu.be/Y93DRfB-Deo?t=117): Make the core loop obvious and engaging from the first second of gameplay; keep depth for later updates.
- 2025-06-07 AlvinBlox [3:21](https://youtu.be/Y93DRfB-Deo?t=201): Make every interaction give immediate feedback or reward (explosion, dopamine hit); don't gate fun behind grinding or pay-to-win.
- 2025-06-08 Cumin [10:04](https://youtu.be/Z1tj1OPOEZg?t=604): Make the core movement fun from the start; don't gut traversal (slow walk, stamina sprint) in a movement-based game.
- 2025-06-08 Cumin [11:53](https://youtu.be/Z1tj1OPOEZg?t=713): Give players a default impulse/boost move; locking core movement behind skill points makes early play feel broken.
- 2025-06-08 Cumin [14:14](https://youtu.be/Z1tj1OPOEZg?t=854): Simplify durability tracking; a single durability bar (as in Titan Warfare) keeps combat momentum better than hidden counters.
- 2025-06-14 JustDucksey [2:04](https://youtu.be/XRvcKGSLr4g?t=124): Tie a resource drain (fuel) to a harvest loop (crops as fuel) so players must keep gathering to keep progressing.
- 2025-06-15 janisjanis01 [0:50](https://youtu.be/W9JYImC0FBg?t=50): Pick a core mechanic that is simple to explain (blend items) and fun to watch, then build the loop around it.
- 2025-06-24 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/introducing-creator-rewards-earn-more-by-growing-the-community/3777628): Optimise for fun, repeatable loops that bring users back every day; Roblox says this is the path to maximising Creator Rewards earnings.
- 2025-06-24 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/introducing-creator-rewards-earn-more-by-growing-the-community/3777628): Build a deep in-experience economy; Creator Rewards only counts the first 10 minutes, and Roblox says top earners will still earn mostly from well-designed loops and microtransactions.
- 2025-06-24 SmartyRBX [29:54](https://youtu.be/skcp4P68i84?t=1794): Make the game fun first (core loop, mechanics) and add monetization as an extra layer, not the main feature.
- 2025-06-26 Mulch [6:10](https://youtu.be/B2vC4-QArWg?t=370): Test whether a block/item needs rotation or directional placement before building many variants; check mechanics in-game early.
- 2025-06-28 SmartyRBX [7:14](https://youtu.be/Lplz7ZFcKlQ?t=434): Prioritise owning monetised games over commissions: a game earns passively 24/7 while commissions require your time.
- 2025-06-29 janisjanis01 [0:21](https://youtu.be/5LMGVfslAYs?t=21): Make Shorts loop seamlessly so viewers rewatch; creator credits loops for replays.
- 2025-07-04 SmartyRBX [7:01](https://youtu.be/ddfqOQ7H_h8?t=421): Prioritise fun over graphical complexity or map size; a fun, simple game that players enjoy will succeed.
- 2025-07-06 SmartyRBX [12:42](https://youtu.be/Gml4to_jXQ0?t=762): Successful games tap into player desire rather than skill; study what players want to play, e.g. cash-grab trends.
- 2025-07-07 SimTek Game Development [44:45](https://youtu.be/DcLba0yVLHs?t=2685): Decouple the attack loop from the AI decision tick: run firing in a task.spawn while loop gated by state, checking target and self health each shot, with about 0.2 s between shots.
- 2025-07-07 SimTek Game Development [48:14](https://youtu.be/DcLba0yVLHs?t=2894): Use a debounce flag in cooldown so the speech bubble and state transition run only once, then reset the flag after a short task.delay (about 2 s).
- 2025-07-09 SmartyRBX [1:01](https://youtu.be/vBllyv2fnWw?t=61): Build a simple primary loop of one repeated action (buy, steal) that players can repeat endlessly.
- 2025-07-09 SmartyRBX [1:36](https://youtu.be/vBllyv2fnWw?t=96): Add a secondary loop where players sell lower-tier items to buy better ones, creating an upgrade cycle.
- 2025-07-09 SmartyRBX [2:20](https://youtu.be/vBllyv2fnWw?t=140): Vary the loop with many item tiers and visual differences so players feel progression, not endless repetition.
- 2025-07-09 SmartyRBX [3:00](https://youtu.be/vBllyv2fnWw?t=180): Add a rebirth system as a long-term goal that multiplies cash and upgrades core actions, giving players a reason to keep playing.
- 2025-07-11 PineappleDev [0:41](https://youtu.be/ggWeHThD_3Q?t=41): Build a simple obby first to learn Studio basics: UI, scripting, DataStores for checkpoints, and map building.
- 2025-07-11 SmartyRBX [1:24](https://youtu.be/zXzXOw5605E?t=84): Map your game to Maslow needs (survival, safety, belonging, self-esteem, self-actualization) to explain and design what keeps players engaged.
- 2025-07-11 SmartyRBX [3:24](https://youtu.be/zXzXOw5605E?t=204): Competitive games like Bedwars and Arsenal work by feeding self-esteem: players want to feel skilled and better than opponents.
- 2025-07-13 janisjanis01 [2:28](https://youtu.be/pD8fKkzF8O8?t=148): Make growth run offline; the creator says it is the main reason grow-a-garden style games are popular.
- 2025-07-13 janisjanis01 [5:26](https://youtu.be/pD8fKkzF8O8?t=326): Add random mutations that multiply item value to create reasons to keep collecting.
- 2025-07-15 PineappleDev [0:22](https://youtu.be/QRkRk-FHN4s?t=22): Store rarity weights as arbitrary integers (e.g. 800/150/45/5) rather than percentages; no need to sum to 100 or 1000.
- 2025-07-21 SmartyRBX [8:38](https://youtu.be/exOJsFcer1Q?t=518): Prioritise fun over visuals; a realistic-looking but boring game loses players within 20-30 minutes.
- 2025-07-27 JustDucksey [4:11](https://youtu.be/s5nooZlK5Pc?t=251): Make food risky-but-rewarding: raw food restores hunger but damages the player, cooked food restores double and does no damage, encouraging campfire use.
- 2025-07-31 Cumin [9:39](https://youtu.be/jXogdTgtlXE?t=579): Never reset all of a player's progress on death in a non-roguelike; it makes the progression loop feel pointless.
- 2025-07-31 Cumin [13:17](https://youtu.be/jXogdTgtlXE?t=797): Check that the repeated tasks players do for the whole session lead to a satisfying experience before building around them.
- 2025-07-31 Cumin [3:36](https://youtu.be/jXogdTgtlXE?t=216): Only use a campaign structure if it has real progression, story presentation and changing content; otherwise drop it.
- 2025-08-00 GamesBeat [page](https://gamesbeat.com/?p=305182): Build a persistent world that progresses while players are offline; the garden growing while you're away was a novel concept on Roblox.
- 2025-08-04 waveshine [3:03](https://youtu.be/xnlaYLnCtFc?t=183): Prioritise core retention over code polish: add meaningful progression, RNG, leaderboards, enemies and something to grind for. Players care about fun, not clean code.
- 2025-08-10 Stewiepfing [0:00](https://youtu.be/THpSS73tveg?t=0): Simulator players expect familiar feedback effects (pop-ups, orbs, animated gradients); copying these genre-standard systems boosts perceived game quality.
- 2025-08-24 Erthlord [7:03](https://youtu.be/dB6Us5PaWFw?t=423): Bubix's checklist: the game must be fun, keep players playing a long time, bring them back, be clickable, and be able to make money.
- 2025-08-29 Roblox Creators [16:25](https://youtu.be/7AtvvUxvCaU?t=985): Place rewarded prompts in high-traffic areas (lobbies, menus) and at natural gameplay breaks, never mid-chase or mid-action.
- 2025-08-29 Roblox Creators [40:27](https://youtu.be/7AtvvUxvCaU?t=2427): Make the reward something players value within the game loop (skins, abilities, area access); Basketball Zero saw 8x impressions from this.
- 2025-08-29 Roblox Creators [8:15](https://youtu.be/7AtvvUxvCaU?t=495): Rewarded ads can be simple; reference code in docs. Fit the reward to the game's look and feel, but a heavy custom build is optional.
- 2025-08-31 Daiorg [1:53](https://youtu.be/GkKUU9mCgwc?t=113): Keep the core design simple and fun; NDS and Work at a Pizza Place stayed popular for years with minimal objectives.
- 2025-08-31 Daiorg [20:02](https://youtu.be/GkKUU9mCgwc?t=1202): Build a short, repeatable loop that keeps players hooked (Grow a Garden: plant, wait ~5 minutes, harvest, buy seeds, repeat).
- 2025-08-31 Daiorg [8:30](https://youtu.be/GkKUU9mCgwc?t=510): Adding a trading system to a game can greatly increase engagement, as seen with Murder Mystery 2.
- 2025-08-31 Cumin [5:08](https://youtu.be/wdf6cfH3rnA?t=308): Give players a reason to defend their base; an always-open base that anyone can steal from makes the loop feel pointless.
- 2025-09-05 Roblox [4:46](https://youtu.be/6iB5__RCQDc?t=286): Let progress accrue while players are offline (e.g., plants grow while away) so returning after hours or days feels rewarding.
- 2025-09-05 Roblox [5:27](https://youtu.be/6iB5__RCQDc?t=327): Keep chase goals reachable for most players so missing a week doesn't leave someone behind; they can rejoin the next update.
- 2025-09-06 Cumin [1:42](https://youtu.be/Jf2RvsWMPes?t=102): Speaker argues designing for long-term play (not just quick monetisation) is what keeps a game alive, though he says Roblox's front page currently rewards the opposite.
- 2025-09-26 Roblox [5:21](https://youtu.be/R1gecdxghwE?t=321): Design for independent input and form-factor dimensions rather than targeting PC, console or mobile separately, so new devices work automatically.
- 2025-09-26 Roblox [6:23](https://youtu.be/V7MKrGggOOA?t=383): In round-based games, use a signal that prioritises servers where a round starts soon so late joiners don't wait in lobbies (one puzzle game saw +7% session time).
- 2025-09-26 Roblox [7:33](https://youtu.be/V7MKrGggOOA?t=453): For skill-matched games, store ELO as a player attribute or in a data store and use a proximity signal to match similar-skill players.
- 2025-09-26 Roblox [5:17](https://youtu.be/YgyfcMfxQcI?t=317): Make the core loop fun first; doubling retention of an unfun game still yields zero. Test the core loop with friends early.
- 2025-09-26 Roblox [7:18](https://youtu.be/YgyfcMfxQcI?t=438): Leave room for player feedback in the roadmap; plan a predicted roadmap but be ready to pivot when players ask for something else.
- 2025-09-26 Roblox [16:08](https://youtu.be/cUxgt4asjFU?t=968): Build a genuinely fun game first; Roblox says the top reason players return is that the game is fun, and discovery amplifies fun rather than replacing it.
- 2025-09-26 Erthlord [6:21](https://youtu.be/fdDscQ6CWIs?t=381): Simple loops with a clear pet-like buy, feed, sell, upgrade cycle were rated best; keep the core loop simple and polished.
- 2025-09-26 Roblox [21:42](https://youtu.be/j6OPA95-1O0?t=1302): Simplify matchmaking tickets: TDS removing difficulty from the menu gave +14% play time and +4% D1 but +40% disconnects, so they kept control.
- 2025-09-26 Roblox [17:42](https://youtu.be/j6OPA95-1O0?t=1062): Add a short trade delay for new accounts to cut scam reports; Adopt Me saw scam reports down 5.5%, playtime up 1%, revenue up ~2%.
- 2025-09-27 SimTek Game Development [0:40](https://youtu.be/EQqd8Xk3Wkk?t=40): Give a sprint mechanic a real cost: drain stamina while sprinting, recover slowly when not, and apply a punishment (here, death) at zero.
- 2025-10-03 ArTheDev [22:02](https://youtu.be/KaCj7Jld35Q?t=1322): Give the double jump its own jump power value so the second jump can be tuned separately from the base jump.
- 2025-10-03 ArTheDev [20:41](https://youtu.be/KaCj7Jld35Q?t=1241): Let jumping cancel a slide; this gives players a skill-based way to chain movement.
- 2025-10-03 ArTheDev [19:59](https://youtu.be/KaCj7Jld35Q?t=1199): Reduce slide speed over time or keep it constant; choose deliberately, since it sets the feel of the movement.
- 2025-10-09 JustDucksey [4:09](https://youtu.be/XZKHQPObHgw?t=249): If the core loop feels too simple, add a timer, breakable fences, and trapping mechanics to create more decisions.
- 2025-10-11 janisjanis01 [15:56](https://youtu.be/MML_z-jpmF4?t=956): Check that core resource drain is balanced against resource availability; the hunger system drained too fast for players to find food, causing early deaths.
- 2025-10-17 Duckable [5:55](https://youtu.be/x7VmLQ9dBAI?t=355): Strong jam entries hook players within the first minute with a clear, immediate goal and a funny, readable hook.
- 2025-10-18 Creators on the Blox [10:12](https://youtu.be/WODeLtB2YH4?t=612): Pick a unique format nobody else does; he says no one else makes Roblox story films with music, so differentiation drives attention.
- 2025-10-18 Creators on the Blox [1:31:34](https://youtu.be/WODeLtB2YH4?t=5494): Hide lore easter eggs in videos; he says Roblox players deeply connect to lore and reward viewers who look closer.
- 2025-10-25 Creators on the Blox [38:04](https://youtu.be/XtWTnz56ZpM?t=2284): Build each new area as a self-contained floor with its own style and mini-game; contained themes keep mixed styles cohesive and make updates feel fresh.
- 2025-10-25 Creators on the Blox [40:50](https://youtu.be/XtWTnz56ZpM?t=2450): Start from the build or aesthetic, then derive the mini-game from it; reverse-engineering a mini-game from a set piece works for him.
- 2025-11-07 DitchyDevelopments [2:30](https://youtu.be/3Pa8QmFB_TE?t=150): Add replayability beyond the core mechanic: round events (low gravity, infected mode), map voting, and collectible power-ups, so players don't just repeat hide and seek.
- 2025-11-10 Creators on the Blox [22:19](https://youtu.be/Qn0uVUSxG8I?t=1339): Pick setting by logic and stakes: an underwater facility makes a containment story where threats cannot escape, which shapes the design.
- 2025-11-15 Creators on the Blox [33:38](https://youtu.be/W8ghgtipQgI?t=2018): Audience reacted to the big-name guest lineup more than to the core gameplay, so guest-driven hype drove views when plain collab videos stopped performing.
- 2025-11-15 Creators on the Blox [45:23](https://youtu.be/W8ghgtipQgI?t=2723): Let viewers compete via custom challenges in participating games, e.g., hidden swords across games, to turn audience requests into engagement.
- 2025-11-21 Duckable [14:41](https://youtu.be/F9sfnpkzcPw?t=881): Layer in a simple loop first (buy, place, hatch, sell), then add upgrades, mutations and rarity tiers as progression.
- 2025-11-23 janisjanis01 [15:13](https://youtu.be/iEvgIId-8Uw?t=913): Tune difficulty based on player feedback; the creator buffed rods after hearing the game was too hard.
- 2025-12-02 TheDankDeveloper [7:55](https://youtu.be/D6Ej1CgiWOc?t=475): Use invisible hitboxes for hazards so the player dies on contact; spacing obstacles apart matters for playability.
- 2025-12-02 TheDankDeveloper [9:17](https://youtu.be/D6Ej1CgiWOc?t=557): Switch from tap-to-jump to hold-to-jump; the creator says this made the game feel much better.
- 2025-12-03 Roblox [3:59](https://youtu.be/oDIpSDOYfY0?t=239): In chaotic party platformers, keep level design loose rather than restrictive to allow organic moments; accept that coordination gets harder.
- 2025-12-11 bubDEV [0:40](https://youtu.be/7z8rfg_jBuk?t=40): Game quality still does most of the work; a good game by Roblox's standards with a strong loop is what the algorithm rewards.
- 2025-12-15 SimTek Game Development [35:47](https://youtu.be/023CG-y2xPY?t=2147): Tie inventory to the loop: destroying a block adds +1 and placing removes -1 through one shared credit function taking a delta value.
- 2025-12-15 SimTek Game Development [36:29](https://youtu.be/023CG-y2xPY?t=2189): Remove a tool from the character when its stack count hits zero by passing an update flag into the tool-tip update function.
- 2025-12-18 Ventures [9:54](https://youtu.be/Nb50UhXcpRM?t=594): Answer three questions for players: why do I care (fantasy/scenario), what is the story/setting, and what am I grinding at. All three are needed to retain players.
- 2025-12-18 Ventures [8:31](https://youtu.be/Nb50UhXcpRM?t=511): Build a clear progression loop with visible upgrades, like Home Run Simulator's bat upgrades or Fish It's rods and rarer fish. Players must feel each upgrade motivates the next.
- 2025-12-21 Daiorg [30:23](https://youtu.be/JCMIb008Y6E?t=1823): Keep visuals simple so gameplay is clear; flashy visual noise distracts players and reduces attention for good game design.
- 2025-12-21 Daiorg [33:52](https://youtu.be/JCMIb008Y6E?t=2032): Use optional difficulty: safe zones for kids, optional side bosses and crafted gear for experienced players, so one game serves ages 4 to adults.
- 2025-12-21 Daiorg [26:35](https://youtu.be/JCMIb008Y6E?t=1595): Make the game engaging rather than idle; 99 Nights runs take one to two hours, showing active play can reach number one.
- 2025-12-21 Daiorg [54:22](https://youtu.be/JCMIb008Y6E?t=3262): Saving progress (base saves) is requested often but is the hardest technical feature; weigh the cost before promising it.
- 2025-12-21 Daiorg [1:07:59](https://youtu.be/JCMIb008Y6E?t=4079): Present information clearly and explain thoroughly, making it understandable to a broad audience, including non-players.
- 2025-12-23 Cumin [1:54](https://youtu.be/hQpC_UHxJDU?t=114): Give RPGs a clear goal from the start: a main quest or objective marker. Without one, players quit; the speaker quit Pilgrim and Critical Fantasy for this reason.
- 2025-12-23 Cumin [19:23](https://youtu.be/hQpC_UHxJDU?t=1163): Use randomized per-run perks on level-up in dungeon games; Pixel Blade's three-choice perks felt meaningful and roguelike.
- 2025-12-23 Cumin [29:52](https://youtu.be/hQpC_UHxJDU?t=1792): Don't let players travel to zones they cannot return from. A one-way fare that strands under-levelled players is punishing.
- 2026-00-00 RoWatcher (tracker blog) [page](https://rowatcher.com/news/fish-it-reels-in-270k-players-inside-roblox-s-most-addictive-casual-game-of-2026): Offer an AFK/auto mode once unlocked; reported average session reached 77.25 minutes, far above typical Roblox sessions.
- 2026-00-00 RoWatcher (tracker blog) [page](https://rowatcher.com/news/fish-it-reels-in-270k-players-inside-roblox-s-most-addictive-casual-game-of-2026): Show rare drops as '1 in X' odds to make each rare catch feel like a jackpot.
- 2026-00-00 GosuGamers [page](https://www.gosugamers.net/entertainment/news/78741-animal-hospital-becomes-roblox-s-latest-horror-sensation-here-s-everything-you-need-to-know): Add a visible streak/progression counter (Total Shifts shown above avatar in lobby) to give players bragging rights and reasons to return, similar to 99 Nights in the Forest.
- 2026-00-00 GosuGamers [page](https://www.gosugamers.net/entertainment/news/78741-animal-hospital-becomes-roblox-s-latest-horror-sensation-here-s-everything-you-need-to-know): Use a two-currency model: earned in-match money for upgrades and permanent collectable currency for unlocks, as Animal Hospital does with Money and Animal Coins.
- 2026-00-00 Player.One [page](https://www.player.one/robloxs-steal-egg-beating-some-platforms-biggest-games-164086): Design for short sessions; reported average playtime is about 15 minutes, so something interesting should happen within a few minutes.
- 2026-01-05 ItsZyonic [2:43](https://youtu.be/4-ge-E9WvM4?t=163): Add progression and unlocks (tools, upgrades, levels, daily chest, leaderboards) to give players depth and reasons to return.
- 2026-01-09 TheDankDeveloper [2:44](https://youtu.be/BYhhm9o3H_E?t=164): Gate new zones behind a purchased rod; verify unlock logic in playtests, since the AI-written gating broke in this test.
- 2026-01-09 TheDankDeveloper [5:59](https://youtu.be/BYhhm9o3H_E?t=359): Give fish rarity tiers with chances and coin values so rarer catches feel rewarding.
- 2026-01-09 TheDankDeveloper [10:09](https://youtu.be/BYhhm9o3H_E?t=609): Add a direct-hit bonus (+50% gold) and milestone popups to reward skilled play.
- 2026-01-13 AlvinBlox [2:51](https://youtu.be/BjyJlN1npdw?t=171): Build the core loop around a broadly relatable activity (e.g. gardening) that is satisfying even for players who don't do it in real life.
- 2026-01-13 AlvinBlox [9:25](https://youtu.be/BjyJlN1npdw?t=565): Keep player control simple (e.g. only choose where to plant) while still allowing customization so it does not feel too complex.
- 2026-01-13 AlvinBlox [9:25](https://youtu.be/BjyJlN1npdw?t=565): Design the economy so most players have plenty of currency; drive engagement through rare RNG drops instead of wealth grinding.
- 2026-01-23 Erthlord [2:55](https://youtu.be/69IBB6O4o4c?t=175): Add a second map when a single map makes the game repetitive and players get bored after a few plays.
- 2026-01-28 bubDEV [4:21](https://youtu.be/2lPsxPtj2mM?t=261): Successful games are good by Roblox standards: they appeal to the algorithm and have strong, engaging core loops that keep players returning.
- 2026-02-07 Cumin [6:03](https://youtu.be/ROo_Xpms4ok?t=363): Give players a reason to fight enemies, e.g. enemies blocking the path. Don't let trivial grunts endlessly spawn while objectives can be skipped.
- 2026-02-07 Cumin [9:29](https://youtu.be/ROo_Xpms4ok?t=569): Avoid enemies spawning behind players in areas already passed. It reads as a bug and makes the objective feel pointless.
- 2026-02-07 Cumin [10:09](https://youtu.be/ROo_Xpms4ok?t=609): Make objective failure logic match the displayed objective. Here the win fired even after failing, so the objective text and the logic disagreed.
- 2026-02-07 Cumin [6:44](https://youtu.be/ROo_Xpms4ok?t=404): Make level-ups meaningful. If XP levels give no stat or unlock benefit, players see progression as fake.
- 2026-02-07 Cumin [12:11](https://youtu.be/ROo_Xpms4ok?t=731): Make sure every ability works and has real impact. A bugged ability wastes a slot and players notice quickly.
- 2026-02-18 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/studio-beta-avatarabilities-library-for-a-luau-based-character-controller/4403904): Tune movement via GroundController Friction/FrictionWeight and TurnSpeedFactor for snappier or looser ground feel.
- 2026-02-18 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/studio-beta-avatarabilities-library-for-a-luau-based-character-controller/4403904): Set AirController MoveMaxForce and TurnMaxTorque to control air acceleration and turning; toggle MaintainLinearMomentum/MaintainAngularMomentum to keep position on moving platforms.
- 2026-02-18 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/studio-beta-avatarabilities-library-for-a-luau-based-character-controller/4403904): Use Labels and Rules (RequiresToActivate, RequiresToContinue, BlocksWhileActive, CancelsOnActivation) for layered abilities instead of if/else state chains.
- 2026-02-18 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/studio-beta-avatarabilities-library-for-a-luau-based-character-controller/4403904): Use ExclusiveGroup with priority to make mutually exclusive abilities (e.g. Locomotion group) so higher priority overrides lower, such as FallingDown (60) over Running (20).
- 2026-02-18 RoWatcher (tracker blog) [page](https://rowatcher.com/news/the-brainrot-invasion-how-absurd-meme-games-conquered-roblox-s-charts): Reward within seconds, add limited-time characters and scheduled admin events, and keep theft as a constant threat to create urgency and emotional stakes.
- 2026-02-25 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/new-analytics-dimensions-get-insight-into-your-players%E2%80%99-spending-behaviors/4438963): Active Spenders must play at least 10 minutes per day to earn the Daily Engagement reward; if average playtime is near or below 10 minutes, increase engagement.
- 2026-02-28 GnomeCode [1:45](https://youtu.be/HYBG2AtUBes?t=105): Give RPG currency a clear sink in the next step (shop) so earned gold has a purpose.
- 2026-03-00 RoWatcher (tracker blog) [page](https://rowatcher.com/news/top-10-roblox-games-by-players-march-2026-data-breakdown): Use player-to-player interaction (trading, social play) as the primary engagement loop rather than relying only on developer-produced content.
- 2026-03-00 RoWatcher (tracker blog) [page](https://rowatcher.com/news/top-10-roblox-games-by-players-march-2026-data-breakdown): Layering objectives beyond pure PvP is cited as deepening session length for anime fighters (Jujutsu Shenanigans).
- 2026-03-02 1_Bruce [2:37](https://youtu.be/9ZoyhYqBkcI?t=157): Play several games in the genre before building one; you need to understand how incremental/stat-multiplier loops work first.
- 2026-03-02 1_Bruce [5:20](https://youtu.be/9ZoyhYqBkcI?t=320): Make each stat multiply the previous one (e.g. rebirth doubles multiplier), so upgrades compound visibly.
- 2026-03-02 1_Bruce [7:39](https://youtu.be/9ZoyhYqBkcI?t=459): Use weighted rarity tables for random rewards (common to mythical) with configurable chances and luck modifiers.
- 2026-03-02 1_Bruce [10:26](https://youtu.be/9ZoyhYqBkcI?t=626): Add a secondary currency (tokens, 1-in-50 per button press) to gate purchases of higher-tier rooms.
- 2026-03-02 1_Bruce [6:18](https://youtu.be/9ZoyhYqBkcI?t=378): Consider tiered multipliers with stacking formulas, e.g. 2x per extra stat level, to keep scaling predictable.
- 2026-03-02 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/why-your-technically-superior-game-isn%E2%80%99t-succeeding-on-roblox-and-why-the-algorithm-ignores-it/4461606): Make the core loop explainable in one sentence to a stranger; if you can't, the game already has friction.
- 2026-03-02 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/why-your-technically-superior-game-isn%E2%80%99t-succeeding-on-roblox-and-why-the-algorithm-ignores-it/4461606): Give instant feedback on every interaction (explosion, currency gain, animation, number increase) to create short reinforcement loops.
- 2026-03-02 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/why-your-technically-superior-game-isn%E2%80%99t-succeeding-on-roblox-and-why-the-algorithm-ignores-it/4461606): Put enjoyment before effort: avoid making players grind before they experience any reward, since retention drops sharply otherwise.
- 2026-03-09 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-we-are-improving-home-this-year/4502571): Build for the signal relevant to your genre: deep-play games may have fewer repeat sessions. Focus on making the game fun rather than optimizing against unpublished weights.
- 2026-03-14 DitchyDevelopments [0:46](https://youtu.be/O9fIqTik9ik?t=46): Nerf overpowered mechanics and remove overlapping ones; a dash that carries further on second use and combo-able slide jumps made movement feel broken.
- 2026-03-14 DitchyDevelopments [1:27](https://youtu.be/O9fIqTik9ik?t=87): Cap max speed (here 70 studs/sec) so tracking and collision systems can keep up with chained movement.
- 2026-03-14 DitchyDevelopments [3:20](https://youtu.be/O9fIqTik9ik?t=200): Give hiders/seekers ongoing rewards (coins per tag, coins for surviving 30 seconds) on top of round win/loss rewards to sustain progression.
- 2026-03-14 DitchyDevelopments [3:20](https://youtu.be/O9fIqTik9ik?t=200): Fix map exits where players fall off and get stuck; add climbable spots and timed off-path penalties instead of letting players soft-lock.
- 2026-03-14 Creators on the Blox [46:14](https://youtu.be/VyBSvWSIkao?t=2774): Separate optional lore from core gameplay so casual players can ignore story while lore fans can read it; the two shouldn't be required together.
- 2026-03-14 CaberJaber [8:17](https://youtu.be/XEA41eXTfoA?t=497): Build risk-reward core loop: let players steal from others while leaving their own base exposed, so stealing feels tense and rewarding.
- 2026-03-27 Creators on the Blox [52:36](https://youtu.be/LWaw0KNwxyM?t=3156): Design for player expression and sandbox freedom: let players find their own routes and optimize playstyle rather than enforcing a closed rule set.
- 2026-03-27 Creators on the Blox [1:00:08](https://youtu.be/LWaw0KNwxyM?t=3608): Games with user-made maps or content (e.g., Ball and Axe, Super Golf map editors) keep players hooked by giving infinite replayability.
- 2026-04-03 ArTheDev [6:20](https://youtu.be/CnZEnFVAFSw?t=380): Store day length and day increment (24 / dayLength) in config to drive a day-night cycle via Lighting.ClockTime from the same server loop.
- 2026-04-08 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/full-release-the-future-of-character-movement-character-controller-library/4565267): Use per-ability toggles in Avatar Settings to gate movement, e.g. disable climbing in certain levels or jumping until a player unlocks it.
- 2026-04-08 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/full-release-the-future-of-character-movement-character-controller-library/4565267): Tune TurnSpeedFactor, RollSpeedFactor, AirController.MoveMaxForce and controller Balance at runtime via Luau to adjust turning, air control and knockdown difficulty.
- 2026-04-08 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/full-release-the-future-of-character-movement-character-controller-library/4565267): To get legacy feel, disable MaintainLinearMomentum and MaintainAngularMomentum on AirController and set GroundController FrictionWeight 100, Friction 2.
- 2026-04-18 ArTheDev [8:05](https://youtu.be/Iy9xuT1bpY4?t=485): Keep drop rates, pools and rarity weights in one config ModuleScript so tuning rarities means editing numbers, not rewriting logic.
- 2026-04-18 ArTheDev [12:11](https://youtu.be/Iy9xuT1bpY4?t=731): Use weighted random selection: sum all weights, roll math.random up to the total, and return the item where the running sum passes the roll.
- 2026-04-19 TheDankDeveloper [11:10](https://youtu.be/Ufr0SVq-o98?t=670): Add simple, visible multipliers like weather (2x growth speed) and pets (money multiplier) to give the core loop variety.
- 2026-04-27 rasdev [0:41](https://youtu.be/M-GCMK1Tryk?t=41): Define a core loop: a main action plus a reward, then repeat. If you can't identify it in 10 seconds, the game lacks one.
- 2026-04-27 rasdev [2:02](https://youtu.be/M-GCMK1Tryk?t=122): Make every session show forward growth: add level systems, rebirths or prestige so the player feels moved up each session.
- 2026-04-30 rasdev [2:01](https://youtu.be/G15TVsyC61g?t=121): Lengthen sessions: add stages to obbies, slow progression in simulators, add content to roleplay games, and add AFK mechanics where waiting makes sense.
- 2026-04-30 rasdev [2:01](https://youtu.be/G15TVsyC61g?t=121): Make the game feel endless; players leave as soon as it feels like it ends.
- 2026-04-30 rasdev [2:41](https://youtu.be/yFKY2FlwHjE?t=161): Remove loading screens, use instant matchmaking and seamless loops so players stay in a flow state instead of being pulled out.
- 2026-04-30 rasdev [3:23](https://youtu.be/yFKY2FlwHjE?t=203): Build and validate the core mechanic first; apply psychology hooks only after the core loop is actually fun.
- 2026-05-00 StudioKrew (agency blog, GGAID data) [page](https://studiokrew.com/blog/top-roblox-games-may-2026/): Idle/cozy loops (plant, wait, harvest, sell) scale when paced and priced for short 5-minute sessions and need no reflexes.
- 2026-05-00 StudioKrew (agency blog, GGAID data) [page](https://studiokrew.com/blog/top-roblox-games-may-2026/): For obbies, a random generated course, a tight timer and a soft competitive layer give replayability that sustains a game for years (Tower of Hell).
- 2026-05-00 StudioKrew (agency blog, GGAID data) [page](https://studiokrew.com/blog/top-roblox-games-may-2026/): In RPGs keep early hours forgiving and make optional depth genuinely optional so progression depth coexists with session length (Blox Fruits).
- 2026-05-03 rasdev [5:35](https://youtu.be/q0lIbkUC2r8?t=335): Build a core loop: action, reward, repeat. Keep progress visible so the game does not feel the same 10 minutes in as at the start.
- 2026-05-09 Creators on the Blox [38:25](https://youtu.be/JsiEVtWLwnA?t=2305): Puzzles players can skip via bugs (random code entry) undermine the reward; test puzzle solutions for exploits before release.
- 2026-05-10 Lnor Roblox [0:40](https://youtu.be/QOS7dQowyRk?t=40): Turn enemy kills into collectible, levelable pets with rollable stats to make combat feed a long-term build loop.
- 2026-05-11 Tizzy [10:01](https://youtu.be/BNdHkeKRUdE?t=601): Study the core loop, progression, D1/D7 retention hooks, then monetisation and player feedback before copying a game.
- 2026-05-13 Tizzy [1:49](https://youtu.be/RQe-MLgY_Zc?t=109): Kids are forgiving; a playable, unbroken game with an in-demand theme can succeed without AAA polish.
- 2026-05-18 rasdev [5:52](https://youtu.be/N3L63uyLyQ8?t=352): Define a clear core loop (play to earn XP and currency, upgrade, return for daily rewards, repeat) before building.
- 2026-05-25 TheDankDeveloper [2:45](https://youtu.be/uYuiCJrAcmI?t=165): Add a safe-zone part and have consumables spawn outside it, so the safe zone creates a risk/reward loop.
- 2026-05-25 TheDankDeveloper [3:26](https://youtu.be/uYuiCJrAcmI?t=206): Reward time survived per second and pause the counter inside the safe zone, so players must balance risk against progress.

- 2026-05-29 Tizzy [8:54](https://youtu.be/0pMW1T0rdUI?t=534): Make other players' progress visible at spawn (social flex element); hidden progress behind walls limits the game.
- 2026-06-06 janisjanis01 [6:39](https://youtu.be/CltiB53QTHw?t=399): Speed-up toggles can break game logic; update enemy speed whenever the speed value changes.
- 2026-06-07 MoseyTube [0:41](https://youtu.be/2rLYGu0v490?t=41): Make the core loop understandable in about 30 seconds with no tutorial; simple loops hook players faster and keep session time high.
- 2026-06-07 MoseyTube [2:04](https://youtu.be/2rLYGu0v490?t=124): Keep progression moving fast (dollars to millions to billions) so there is always a next upgrade or milestone, never a long grind wall.
- 2026-06-07 MoseyTube [2:45](https://youtu.be/2rLYGu0v490?t=165): Reveal content slowly in layers (managers, teleporter, rebirth, hidden buttons) so players stay curious about what comes next.
- 2026-06-07 MoseyTube [4:36](https://youtu.be/2rLYGu0v490?t=276): Include at least one mechanic that creates a shared moment with other players in the server.
- 2026-06-10 Tizzy [0:18](https://youtu.be/HFcegpPPvpI?t=18): Add sound and visual effects plus camera shake to every core action; action-and-response feel drives average playtime.
- 2026-06-10 MoseyTube [2:07](https://youtu.be/esZQX5COI_E?t=127): Give idle plot elements a purpose: replace a dead placeholder pad with a reward mechanic (wheel spin for cash every hour) that players return to.
- 2026-06-12 GosuGamers [page](https://www.gosugamers.net/entertainment/news/78614-grow-a-garden-2-has-launched-on-roblox-here-s-everything-new-added-in-this-sequel): Offline crop growth plus a harvest-and-sell loop keeps the core loop progressing between sessions.

- 2026-06-15 rasdev [0:41](https://youtu.be/7VhmLFkhwPU?t=41): Target sessions of about 10+ minutes as a beginner, per the speaker's engagement benchmark.
- 2026-06-15 Tizzy [3:46](https://youtu.be/zoz3PSMqets?t=226): Borrow proven mechanics from other games in your genre; he added a base-lock feature from Stealing Brains-style games that Oil Empire lacked.
- 2026-06-16 VuukStudios [4:15](https://youtu.be/catPKgFIZbQ?t=255): Aim for at least 10-12 minutes of playtime on release; 20+ minutes for a big game, since low playtime stops the algorithm pushing the game.
- 2026-06-16 VuukStudios [8:23](https://youtu.be/catPKgFIZbQ?t=503): Build in a social factor (trading, viewing others' bases); avoid solo-only games, which rarely survive long.
- 2026-06-19 LovJak [4:14](https://youtu.be/ImcICq5XfL8?t=254): Build growth as a staged model swap (1 to 6) with a ProximityPrompt on the final stage to make harvesting clear.
- 2026-06-19 Yann [4:54](https://youtu.be/zWeDpmrjhUM?t=294): Build core systems first and make them feel right; dependent systems (shops, inventory) only make sense after the systems they rely on exist.
- 2026-06-19 Yann [6:03](https://youtu.be/zWeDpmrjhUM?t=363): Cut systems that take too long, are unaffordable, or don't serve the core loop; iterate without drifting far from the original scope.
- 2026-06-20 Tizzy [14:23](https://youtu.be/pu2o80prNV8?t=863): Measure time-to-complete the core game (his was ~2.5 hours) and ship content updates when players finish too fast for D7.
- 2026-06-22 rasdev [6:28](https://youtu.be/TqjyNV9La5c?t=388): Keep the MVP minimal (e.g. 1-4 weeks solo): simple PvP, co-op survival, basic inventory, trading, level progression.
- 2026-06-22 rasdev [7:09](https://youtu.be/TqjyNV9La5c?t=429): Define a core loop explicitly (action, reward, upgrade, return) since it is what keeps players hooked.
- 2026-06-24 Cumin [11:26](https://youtu.be/2IdxQNOGoqA?t=686): Tick status effects and buff durations at the end of the affected unit's turn, not the start. Start-of-turn ticking can kill or waste buffs before the player acts.
- 2026-06-24 Cumin [12:06](https://youtu.be/2IdxQNOGoqA?t=726): Avoid stacking energy costs and cooldowns on the same action. Use one time-based gate so turns aren't wasted on waiting.
- 2026-06-24 Cumin [12:47](https://youtu.be/2IdxQNOGoqA?t=767): Give players a persistent resource or varied strategies so combat rewards decisions instead of repeating the same opening every battle.
- 2026-06-24 Cumin [13:28](https://youtu.be/2IdxQNOGoqA?t=808): Make losses traceable to a decision, so a death feels like 'that attack cost me the run' rather than attrition.
- 2026-06-24 Cumin [14:48](https://youtu.be/2IdxQNOGoqA?t=888): Make bosses use different mechanics from basic encounters, not just higher HP.
- 2026-06-24 Cumin [16:12](https://youtu.be/2IdxQNOGoqA?t=972): Reduce downtime in turn-based multiplayer: waiting for each player's turn then each enemy's turn makes long sessions feel like nothing happens.
- 2026-06-24 Cumin [17:34](https://youtu.be/2IdxQNOGoqA?t=1054): Consider simultaneous move selection in multiplayer turn-based combat: everyone queues actions, visible to the team, and turn order resolves them.
- 2026-06-24 Cumin [18:16](https://youtu.be/2IdxQNOGoqA?t=1096): In multiplayer turn-based combat, have all players act together, then all enemies, rather than one turn per player.
- 2026-06-29 Tizzy [8:58](https://youtu.be/4ms4cp2mrtM?t=538): Make each system readable by reusing a mechanic already proven in a top Roblox game so players arrive already knowing how to use it.
- 2026-06-29 MoseyTube [2:01](https://youtu.be/Kh_XKYw8ocU?t=121): Make the core loop understandable within about 30 seconds of spawning; players who don't get it leave fast, tanking session time.
- 2026-06-29 MoseyTube [2:29](https://youtu.be/Kh_XKYw8ocU?t=149): Speed up early progression so something unlocks or a number jumps every few minutes; the speaker saw session times rise after doing this.
- 2026-06-29 MoseyTube [3:34](https://youtu.be/Kh_XKYw8ocU?t=214): Reveal content gradually so there is always something unseen ahead, which keeps players playing longer.
- 2026-06-30 Raul [0:39](https://youtu.be/vnpCimQkHoQ?t=39): Adding new weapons did not get noticed; players kept using old guns. Check whether new content changes player behaviour before investing heavily.
- 2026-07-04 Generalist Programmer [3:05](https://youtu.be/DirIakVx7eQ?t=185): Store the cash value on each dropped part as an IntValue so upgrades only need a higher-value dropper, not new collector logic.

- 2026-07-04 Duckable [1:21](https://youtu.be/eRhFPgj8tc4?t=81): Copy proven loops from current top games (e.g. Slime RNG) and re-skin them; the creator switched from tycoon to RNG after seeing Slime RNG hit #1.

- 2026-07-08 rasdev [1:21](https://youtu.be/fPT2GCkK1QM?t=81): Build a core loop: action, reward, upgrade/progress, repeat, with rebirth for long-term return reasons.
- 2026-07-13 Tizzy [2:03](https://youtu.be/nggNvUILQ1I?t=123): Pick mechanics that are simple and intuitive but deep. Prefer depth over complexity so strategy is never a solved strategy.
- 2026-07-13 Tizzy [7:42](https://youtu.be/nggNvUILQ1I?t=462): Add PvP so every session differs. PvE games like Build to Survive Tsunami get solved within 5 minutes, per the creator.
- 2026-07-13 Tizzy [15:23](https://youtu.be/nggNvUILQ1I?t=923): Don't copy genre mechanics like auto-roll blindly. Auto-roll let kids reach end-game in ~3 hours and wrecked the economy. Remove it.
- 2026-07-13 Tizzy [19:36](https://youtu.be/nggNvUILQ1I?t=1176): Use level design to add juking options. A central island with climbable vines expands steal tactics. Plan timed biome changes and events.
- 2026-07-14 MoseyTube [3:02](https://youtu.be/-PoyiiGhoNo?t=182): Watch real players for 5 minutes each to find frustration points, slowdowns and boring moments; those moments are your product ideas.

- 2026-07-20 Tizzy [13:09](https://youtu.be/LZOohZHsJus?t=789): Build depth from one simple mechanic. Build a Base and Steal's base-raid loop forces constant layout optimisation, and Sandwich Game uses one RNG roll.
- 2026-07-20 Tizzy [14:15](https://youtu.be/LZOohZHsJus?t=855): Make every feature's cause and effect obvious: press this button, this happens. Simple scales, fancy fails.

- 2026-07-28 Tizzy [13:46](https://youtu.be/FnsIL4zPCQg?t=826): Pick one simple, easy-to-learn core mechanic with depth (e.g. voxel building in Build a Plane) so players create varied content.
