- 2026-09-18 revolver agz [16:26](https://youtu.be/j3L0K1yZjms?t=986): Use level-gated map indicators (red flash) to guide players to the right farming zone for their level.
- 2026-09-18 revolver agz [22:27](https://youtu.be/j3L0K1yZjms?t=1347): Dungeons with points-based shop and RNG chests give a mid-game grind loop tied to progression items.
- 2026-09-18 revolver agz [28:02](https://youtu.be/j3L0K1yZjms?t=1682): Random world events (e.g. wild horse spawns announced by NPC chat) reward players who watch for them.
- 2026-09-19 rasdev [3:07](https://youtu.be/tErJHe9wQn0?t=187): Packaging alone won't retain players; the gameplay itself must be genuinely fun.
- 2026-09-19 SimTek Game Development [4:13](https://youtu.be/w0n23oW0690?t=253): Set Camera.CameraType to Scriptable to take control from Roblox's default camera scripts; set it back to Custom when done.
- 2026-09-22 rasdev [4:49](https://youtu.be/ILhTEWqz2kU?t=289): Build systems that grow without maintenance. CAC updated automatically as Roblox added catalog items, so the game needed little upkeep.
- 2026-09-23 Tizzy [6:04](https://youtu.be/CRCcsYEB_6A?t=364): Use time-based restock (e.g. spawns refresh every 5 minutes) to create FOMO and urgency, as Steal a Egg borrowed from Grow a Garden's stock refresh.
- 2026-09-23 Tizzy [4:42](https://youtu.be/CRCcsYEB_6A?t=282): Create tension with a timer or a chaser. Ride a Pet uses an egg cracking timer; Steal an Egg and Surf and Rescue add chasing monsters or sharks.
- 2026-09-23 Tizzy [7:37](https://youtu.be/CRCcsYEB_6A?t=457): Use a pressure-valve level design: players run to grab a reward, decide whether to keep going, then release tension at a safe zone. Escape Tsunami popularised this.
- 2026-09-23 Tizzy [5:23](https://youtu.be/CRCcsYEB_6A?t=323): Add PvP to a PvE chase to intensify tension. Steal an Egg added player-vs-player to the monster-chase loop of Escape Tsunami-style games.
- 2026-09-23 Tizzy [3:22](https://youtu.be/CRCcsYEB_6A?t=202): Decide where RNG is shown: pre-acquisition (Kick a Lucky Block, rolls visible on kick) or post-acquisition (Ride a Pet, roll when egg returns to base).
- 2026-09-23 Tizzy [10:21](https://youtu.be/CRCcsYEB_6A?t=621): Add a skill check (tap as a bar fills) to a core action to differentiate, as Kick a Lucky Block did with its kick mechanic.
- 2026-09-23 Tizzy [10:21](https://youtu.be/CRCcsYEB_6A?t=621): Vary progression: Escape Tsunami and Steal an Egg use get faster and collect pets, Kick a Lucky Block uses get stronger, Dig and Clean uses better shovels and collects treasure.
- 2026-09-23 Tizzy [2:39](https://youtu.be/CRCcsYEB_6A?t=159): Most simulator hits share the core loop: leave base, get a thing, bring it back to base. Build your loop around that proven pattern.
- 2026-09-23 Tizzy [4:01](https://youtu.be/CRCcsYEB_6A?t=241): Level design is a differentiator. Ride a Pet's open exploration map (inspired by Creatures of Sinaria) was innovative versus the common linear layout.
- 2026-09-23 Tizzy [17:03](https://youtu.be/CRCcsYEB_6A?t=1023): Look beyond the building blocks for retention: first-five-minute progression pacing, retention mechanics, collection expansion and mutations.
- 2026-09-23 VuukStudios [8:21](https://youtu.be/d5YHwCqwgYM?t=501): Include social hangout elements (view others' pets/items), rarity-based grinding, and player-vs-player competition; he says single-player games fail on Roblox.
- 2026-09-23 Lnor Roblox [1:12](https://youtu.be/gSFxStWqmgM?t=72): Make progression visibly feel like growth: new islands and placeable decorations with bonuses so new players feel stronger.
- 2026-09-23 Lnor Roblox [4:12](https://youtu.be/gSFxStWqmgM?t=252): Hide rare outcomes until revealed; showing rarity before opening killed curiosity. Remove forced walking and cooldowns between actions.
- 2026-09-25 kra_DeZ [1:34](https://youtu.be/ZradA-cygP0?t=94): Add a clear finale mechanic: he replaced a boring final showdown with a one-on-one duel for last two players, after which the game started growing.
- 2026-09-26 rasdev [4:51](https://youtu.be/0_dR_081Hq8?t=291): Build a clear core loop: gather by day, prepare and defend at night, get rewarded by surviving to the next cycle and improving your base.
- 2026-09-30 Roblox Creators [28:28](https://youtu.be/hohwBQGQO7c?t=1708): Try unconventional ideas, but kill mechanics that players don't click with; their random crafting-table roguelike idea failed after about 50 players.
- 2026-10-03 VuukStudios [8:05](https://youtu.be/4vuXAQt0PuQ?t=485): Sell the final outcome: frame the game as a bridge to a dream state and show players what they will become after weeks of play.
- 2026-10-03 VuukStudios [11:33](https://youtu.be/4vuXAQt0PuQ?t=693): Create an in-game problem (e.g. a slow growth system) and sell the solution (speed-up purchase).
- 2026-10-03 MonarchDev [2:32](https://youtu.be/bbCmt98O9MA?t=152): Give the core loop a clear risk decision: cash out now or wait for a bigger payout.
- 2026-10-06 TheDankDeveloper [0:40](https://youtu.be/eExtO_uF6Jg?t=40): Add a skill element (power bar, perfect hits, combo streak +10% score) so outcomes feel less random.
- 2026-10-06 TheDankDeveloper [1:22](https://youtu.be/eExtO_uF6Jg?t=82): Give players a progression sink early: upgrades bought with score (multipliers, speed, dartboards) to drive repeat play.
- 2026-10-10 mats-dept2 [0:00](https://youtu.be/Y5QtYzd95v8?t=0): Define the most addictive ~30-second core loop before writing any Studio code; the video's example is sneak, hack, steal, sprint to escape.
- 2026-10-10 mats-dept2 [0:30](https://youtu.be/Y5QtYzd95v8?t=30): Mix player modes so empty servers still work: solo players face AI opponents while friends trigger a race-to-escape.
- 2026-10-10 mats-dept2 [1:13](https://youtu.be/Y5QtYzd95v8?t=73): Give players a choice when a challenge is instant-death, such as hack a breaker box or sprint-jump past, so it doesn't feel unfair.
- 2026-10-10 mats-dept2 [1:42](https://youtu.be/evyIBvylLdc?t=102): Playtest and cut mechanics that slow pacing; a slide-under-laser mechanic was dropped because it made controls messy, and focusing on dash and jump improved feel.
- 2026-10-10 mats-dept2 [2:28](https://youtu.be/evyIBvylLdc?t=148): Make the finale a visible payoff; a helicopter escape that just faded to black was replaced by a dramatic takeoff animation.
- 2026-10-11 rasdev [7:28](https://youtu.be/e4o-ECkzDoI?t=448): Build social play into the game; solo games perform worse, so make friends part of the experience.
- 2026-10-12 Roblox [4:49](https://youtu.be/LIKiUlkTqZc?t=289): Keep players physically close in small shared spaces so they see and chat with each other; Fisch's Moosewood dock and Steal a Brainrot bases follow this.
- 2026-10-12 Roblox [16:08](https://youtu.be/LIKiUlkTqZc?t=968): Set goals that feel distant but achievable: make the next island or goal visible from the start area so players are motivated to go for it.
- 2026-10-12 Roblox [18:53](https://youtu.be/LIKiUlkTqZc?t=1133): Show players far stronger gear from the start (e.g., a high-tier rod next to a basic one) so they want to chase upgrades.
- 2026-10-12 Roblox [3:47](https://youtu.be/QvNAMmGqdd4?t=227): Dynamic behavior should only trigger on defined events and be limited to an allowlist of actions; core game systems (health, combat, inventory, rewards) stay in your code.
- 2026-10-12 Roblox [21:21](https://youtu.be/QvNAMmGqdd4?t=1281): For quick reactions, use the human-like model; for longer-range planning or simple puzzles, start with behavior-tree logic before adding human-like agents.
- 2026-10-12 Roblox [11:01](https://youtu.be/bkRZeTZAvLw?t=661): Build meaningful progression with a goal to chase and new unlocks, not a repetitive loop.
- 2026-10-12 Roblox [10:24](https://youtu.be/qLP05zvMsW0?t=624): Use randomness to multiply content runway. World Zero gave cosmetics random colors (~16 billion combos) so repeat earns differ and drive trading and grinding.
- 2026-10-12 Roblox [14:32](https://youtu.be/qLP05zvMsW0?t=872): Front-load XP and progression into the first hour so light players get most of the daily gain; heavy players progress slowly unless they buy a booster.
- 2026-10-12 Roblox [15:55](https://youtu.be/qLP05zvMsW0?t=955): Build level curves from time: mob kill time times mobs per level, plus travel, shopping and social time. Put estimates in a spreadsheet, then playtest to compare.
- 2026-10-12 Roblox [21:46](https://youtu.be/qLP05zvMsW0?t=1306): Define each class by a one-to-two sentence purpose, and compare purposes side by side in a document before building to avoid homogeneous classes.
- 2026-10-12 Roblox [22:28](https://youtu.be/qLP05zvMsW0?t=1348): Place your game on a spectrum from hyperrealistic to superpowers to decide how far you can stylise (cinematic realism like John Wick).
- 2026-10-12 Roblox [23:52](https://youtu.be/qLP05zvMsW0?t=1432): Make players explore a new area once, then give fast travel (menu or waystones). Do not make players sit through long travel every time.
- 2026-10-12 Roblox [28:31](https://youtu.be/qLP05zvMsW0?t=1711): Lean into emergent behavior: allow item dropping and PvP so players form gangs and clans. Turn blocking and tolls into social hooks.
- 2026-10-12 Roblox [16:29](https://youtu.be/vumAuOqSmNA?t=989): Build an immediate, satisfying moment-to-moment mechanic first, then wrap it in long-term progression and narrative that can be extended with content.
- 2026-10-12 Roblox [20:35](https://youtu.be/vumAuOqSmNA?t=1235): Avoid fine manual-dexterity mechanics like clicking moving targets; they alienate older players while kids tolerate them.
- 2026-10-12 Roblox [22:13](https://youtu.be/vumAuOqSmNA?t=1333): Let veteran and new players play together: allow item trading and revive so strong players can carry weaker ones.
- 2026-10-12 Roblox [23:34](https://youtu.be/vumAuOqSmNA?t=1414): Test risky choices early with A/B tests and segment results by age; a camera choice that hurt overall numbers was neutral for 18+.
- 2026-10-14 Tizzy [7:55](https://youtu.be/_JyiKfNNqls?t=475): Balance progression on a fresh alt account, not your own save. Log the timestamp of each unlock (e.g. minute 1 treadmill, minute 5 zone 3) to spot walls.
- 2026-10-15 LovJak [1:22](https://youtu.be/YXcV0Ba0RJc?t=82): Build the core loop first: pick up item, carry to placement, earn money, unlock upgrades. Add systems one at a time around that loop.
- 2026-10-15 LovJak [2:04](https://youtu.be/YXcV0Ba0RJc?t=124): Progression felt too fast in playtest, so an unlock gate was added. Tune pacing early rather than letting players blow through content.
- 2026-10-15 LovJak [2:46](https://youtu.be/YXcV0Ba0RJc?t=166): Scale threat with progression: if guards stay slow, fast players trivialise the game. Add guards per new area and raise difficulty to protect replayability.
- 2026-10-16 MonarchDev [2:32](https://youtu.be/hPWJlFwp5Xs?t=152): Build the core loop first and test it solo; if one player can do it alone, fix that before adding anything else.
- 2026-10-17 rasdev [6:06](https://youtu.be/DtdGtvALhCA?t=366): Growth from any channel fails if the game isn't fun; quality comes first.
- 2026-10-18 Duckable [4:21](https://youtu.be/EOUSVENUkr0?t=261): Build the core loop first: roll/buy eggs, hatch into zombies, place in enclosures that generate money, sell extras. Then add systems around it.
- 2026-10-20 rasdev [5:14](https://youtu.be/53OBGXktIPY?t=314): Show numbers going up dramatically (huge currency gains) to make progress feel satisfying.
- 2026-10-21 MonarchDev [12:31](https://youtu.be/3bUysm7IFxg?t=751): Ship a working core loop first: the video's top generator lacked missions, so nothing was left to do and it lost the 'is there anything to do' criterion.
- 2026-10-21 TheDankDeveloper [5:30](https://youtu.be/Mftq7v8nAcs?t=330): Core loop to copy first: steal an egg, run from the chicken boss, place egg to hatch, earn cash per second, then upgrade speed to reach the next zone.
- 2026-10-22 Deconstructor of Fun [55:31](https://youtu.be/oU3oz6Ddz7g?t=3331): Prioritise sandbox systems with long engagement (mastery, exploration, weapon progression) over cosmetics, which add no gameplay loop; they are a weak use of content budget.
- 2026-10-23 TheDankDeveloper [11:39](https://youtu.be/5S_MYGBtmbM?t=699): Add in-round money and an ability shop to give players a reason to keep playing after rounds.
- 2026-10-23 tef [7:06](https://youtu.be/OfObWnrucRY?t=426): Make high-tier gear expensive so players must grind for it, and balance the grind with cheaper chances (spins) to keep it feeling worthwhile.
- 2026-10-25 janisjanis01 [6:52](https://youtu.be/A2QD-eT3IG0?t=412): Balance new towers/powers early: the mortar was too strong, then did zero damage because it scaled off base DPS with no turrets present.
- 2026-10-25 janisjanis01 [7:34](https://youtu.be/A2QD-eT3IG0?t=454): Expect every new power to be overpowered on first pass and plan a nerf pass; small QoL like a back-to-plot button had outsized impact.
- 2026-10-25 janisjanis01 [8:15](https://youtu.be/A2QD-eT3IG0?t=495): Late-game units (swords, basic blocks) became pointless after certain waves; add upgrade/scaling so older content stays relevant.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/configs): Use configs instead of hard-coded constants for boss health, XP gain, item prices so you can tune live without a new version.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/battle-royale-series/cleanup-and-reset): Structure the match as a game loop: intermission, prepare, match, end status, cleanup, reset, then repeat, so the game runs indefinitely.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/adventure-game-series/finishing-the-project): Tune sell price per item between 50 and 200, multiplied by item count, so the sell loop feels rewarding.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/adventure-game-series/finishing-the-project): Set upgrade capacity increase to 10-15 and upgrade cost to 500-1000 to keep upgrades challenging but fair.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/adventure-game-series/finishing-the-project): Vary item placement and add obstacles to collection so item gathering stays fun rather than trivial.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/adventure-game-series/create-the-map): Define the game loop as a cycle of mechanics (e.g. explore, harvest, sell, upgrade) before building; each loop step should be a distinct player action.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/game-design/prototyping): Prioritise prototyping the core loop, the central gameplay the whole game is built on.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/game-design/prototyping): Work out game rules in detail, e.g. respawn timer length, respawn location, and factors that change how fast players rejoin the action.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/adventure-game-series/collect-items): Make harvested items disappear for a few seconds so players explore for more instead of re-clicking one object.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/battle-royale-series/finishing-the-project): Make the lobby social with mini-games such as physics spheres or mini obstacle courses to keep players engaged during intermission.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/battle-royale-series/landing): Build a round-based game loop that runs in the background and drives match states; separate concerns into modules (game loop, player management, timers).
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/battle-royale-series/landing): Track players in arrays and update them as players start, win, or leave matches.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/curriculums/gameplay-scripting/add-rounds): Track team points in a separate attribute store rather than only the Teams service, because a leaving player's contribution is deducted from team score on disconnect.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/curriculums/gameplay-scripting/add-rounds): Set a clear round objective shown at the top of the screen so players know what the team must do to win.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/curriculums/gameplay-scripting/add-rounds): Use rounds with clear start and finish points so players can measure progress and get periodic equal footing, especially in team games.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/curriculums/gameplay-scripting/add-rounds): Keep the objective limit in a single shared config module so the UI and server both read the same value and can be tuned quickly.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/curriculums/gameplay-scripting/add-rounds): Reset all leaderboard and team points to zero at the start of each round loop before sorting players into teams.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/use-case-tutorials/ui/proximity-prompts): Set HoldDuration above zero for actions that should feel deliberate (e.g. picking a lock); PromptTriggered fires only after the hold completes.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/the-mystery-of-duvall-drive/develop-a-moving-world): Decide which movement tool fits by need: physics for interactables, TweenService for scripted motion, animations for shared motion, particles for ambient effects.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/fundamentals/coding-2/parameters-practice-buttons): Give players feedback when they touch a button (color change, sound) so they know it is being interacted with.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/characters/character-controller-library): Do not disable Running; doing so prevents characters from moving along the ground.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/characters/character-controller-library): If FallingDown is enabled, always keep GettingUp enabled; otherwise characters can trip but never get back up.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/characters/character-controller-library): Use Input Mode Press for discrete actions (dash, attack, throw), Hold or Toggle for sustained actions (sprint, aim, block, crouch), Hold with Repeats for repeated jumping.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/characters/character-controller-library): Do not put Rule.Input into StartsWhen or RunsWhile; it throws an error. Let Mode control input-driven start/stop.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/characters/character-controller-library): Use OutlivesInputWhile on Hold/Toggle abilities to keep an action running past release under a world condition (e.g. crouch while a ceiling is overhead).
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/fundamentals/coding-6/coding-concept-algorithms): Break large game logic into smaller functions that call each other, e.g. a main function calling pickClothes() and putOnClothes(), to keep systems manageable.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/game-design/introduction-to-quest-design): Structure quests as objective + quantity + reward; larger quantities raise difficulty, and harder quests should offer more valuable rewards.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/game-design/introduction-to-quest-design): Tie quests to the core loop so players naturally earn and spend resources; scarce or effort-heavy sources hurt economy participation and retention.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/game-design/introduction-to-quest-design): Use quest-based season passes with reward tiers tied to core-loop activities, showing progress UI against the season deadline.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/game-design/introduction-to-quest-design): Use achievements/badges for challenging alternate goals (e.g., unusual gameplay combos) to re-engage experienced players between updates.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/game-design/introduction-to-quest-design): Use quests to surface under-discovered features in large worlds or complex games; a quest can be the first time players learn a location exists.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/game-design/introduction-to-quest-design): Add optional flavor text to quests to deliver narrative and tie rewards thematically to the story.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/experience-notifications): Make notifications personalized to the recipient's in-game activity; avoid generic advertising-style messages.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/experience-notifications): Never gate gameplay behind enabling notifications; players must be able to progress without opting in.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/battle-royale-series/coding-the-game-loop): Split the battle royale game loop into three phases: intermission, competition, and cleanup & reset, all inside one main while loop.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/workspace/cframes): Use CFrame:ToWorldSpace for offsets and rotations relative to an object's own orientation, not fixed world coordinates, when the target's position or facing can vary.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/game-design/core-loops): Structure every game's core loop in three steps: minute-to-minute interaction, most repeated set of actions, and a progression engine.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/game-design/core-loops): Identify the most frequently repeated actions; these are often the defining mechanic and become the core of the loop.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/game-design/core-loops): Always include a progression system; without one the game becomes repetitive, boring and shallow.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/game-design/core-loops): Make progression feed back into the earlier steps, so upgrades boost future exploration and combat capabilities.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/creator-programs/jumpstart): Build deep systems: metagame systems, skillful challenges and meaningful progression are what keep players returning, per Roblox's criteria.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/resources/coding-concept-algorithms): Build game mechanics from small algorithms: e.g. a lava hazard that deducts 5 health per second and respawns the player at 0 health.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/publishing/vr-guidelines): Use Roblox default camera scripts (CameraModule child scripts) for VR; they receive ongoing updates and comfort-setting support, unlike custom cameras.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/game-design/liveops-planning): Design event rewards so they don't distort the economy; e.g. a fishing tournament boosts currency earning, so reward types must be intentional.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/engagement): Add a progression system to give users a clear path to goals and make content last longer.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/engagement): Keep enough content available upfront so progression does not gate off the fun for new users' first session.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/game-design/season-pass-design): Tie season pass missions to every system and content type in the experience so players learn systems and have varied things to do.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/game-design/season-pass-design): Make missions manageable so they don't consume a player's entire daily playtime; offer varied tasks for different playstyles.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/game-design/season-pass-design): Start with about ten season tiers; keep the count short enough that the season isn't daunting at the start.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/game-design/season-pass-design): Scale tier rewards to the XP effort required; include wanted but not overly valuable items like consumables, small currency, temporary buffs, or small cosmetics.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/game-design/season-pass-design): Require players to claim rewards via a button in the season pass UI rather than auto-granting, and show an icon badge when rewards are uncollected.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/game-design/season-pass-design): Never require hard currency for missions; this excludes free players and forces premium players to spend more.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/game-design/season-pass-design): Reset missions daily and unlock new missions daily to encourage daily engagement and prevent fast consumption of season content.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/game-design/season-pass-design): Vary mission difficulty from easy to hard, and keep categories broad with at least three across the whole experience.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/game-design/season-pass-design): Keep daily missions achievable; if players regularly fail them, interest in the season pass wanes.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/game-design/season-pass-design): Make the season pass highly visible: large colorful buttons, a visible timer, themed HUD graphics, and first-login messages after changes.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/use-case-tutorials/scripting/basic-scripting/score-points): Frame the game as a competitive survival loop: score per second alive, reset on death, so players compete for the highest score.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/use-case-tutorials/scripting/basic-scripting/score-points): Add a lobby to teleport users in together, and announce round winners, to improve multiplayer rounds.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/build-it-play-it-galactic-speedway/customize-and-share): Give each vehicle distinct strengths and weaknesses (e.g., slower base speed with faster boost, or very fast but slow to turn) so multiple options feel unique.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/custom-events): Check whether one option dominates a loop (e.g. one plant type strongly preferred) and rebalance to avoid repetitiveness and economy imbalance.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/curriculums/gameplay-scripting/detect-hits): Choose physically simulated hit checks for projectiles affected by gravity or wind (e.g. dodgeball); use instantaneous raycasts for near-infinite-speed beams (e.g. laser tag).
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/funnel-events): If a funnel step has the largest drop-off, add contextual indicators guiding players to that action, or require it before exploring the rest of the game.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/npc-kit): Set DestroyOnDeath to false if you want NPC ragdolls to persist after death.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/fundamentals/coding-4/create-a-timed-bridge): Pair loops with player interaction (touch a button) to create timed gameplay moments, like a limited-time crossing that drops players who are too slow.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ai/build-with-assistant): Verify in playtest: spawn room, fair jump gaps, checkpoint respawn updates, kill bricks respawn correctly, win zone triggers.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/curriculums/gameplay-scripting/implement-blasters): Trigger weapon actions on Enum.UserInputState.Begin, not End; delayed release makes blasts feel unresponsive.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/characters/character-controller-library/quick-start): Tune movement feel by scaling GroundController.MoveSpeedFactor and TurnSpeedFactor, and set AccelerationTime (e.g. 0.2s) and DecelerationTime (e.g. 0.4s) for slight easing.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/curriculums/core/scripting/create-player-hazards): Make hazards visually obvious: keep the real water visible and make the invisible hazard part Transparency 1 so the water itself reads as the danger.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/game-design/subscription-design): Only offer a monthly trickle of items if core loop and content are deep enough to track players from new to veteran status.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/game-design/onboarding-techniques): Trigger tutorials just in time (on first encounter of a feature) instead of front-loading them in onboarding, to reduce cognitive load and speed up reaching fun.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/feature-packages/engagement-rewards): Write time thresholds as multiplication (e.g. 2 * 60 * 60) so values are readable; use tiny values only for testing.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/battle-royale/pickup-system): Rarity has no formula; define rarity tiers in RarityConfiguration to visually signal item value (Common to Special by default).
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/fundamentals/coding-3/traps-with-if-statements): Check for a Humanoid before acting on a touched part so decor is ignored; touched parts may be a limb or accessory, so check otherPart.Parent for the Humanoid.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ui/3D-drag-detectors): Use custom constraint functions to snap drag motion to a grid or enforce piece-movement rules, since DragDetectors have no built-in snapping.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/lesson-plans/roblox-developer-lesson): Design the core loop as collect, sell, upgrade: a tool harvests items, items sell for gold, gold buys upgrades that increase collection.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/lesson-plans/roblox-developer-lesson): Tune starting gold and space variables to keep the game challenging but not frustrating or punishing.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/lesson-plans/roblox-developer-lesson): Plan the game on paper first (game design document with simple shapes) before building in Studio.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/modules/event-sequencer): Perform audio and animation operations on the client side, as in a typical game, and keep schema configurations in both client and server modules where needed.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/modules/event-sequencer): Omit OnEndScene for endless scenes such as post-show venues and set TimeLength long enough to cover all configurations.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/experiments): Test onboarding flows, progression systems, control schemes and custom matchmaking as engagement experiments measured by playtime and D1/D7 retention.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/workspace/streaming/techniques): Do not gate loading screen dismissal on a spatial map instance; signal readiness after the character spawns and nearby area streams in, or call AddReplicationFocus at spawn.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/lesson-plans/roblox-developer/landing): Build a basic loop of gather resources, purchase items, upgrade spaces, and sell items for an adventure game.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/build-it-play-it-island-of-move/animating-parts): Animate objects on interaction (press, hit, pet, pickup) to give players immediate feedback that their action did something.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/fundamentals/coding-3/powerups-with-if-statements): Limit repeated power-ups with a cooldown (task.wait(1) then re-enable) so players get a controlled, rewarding boost instead of runaway stats.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/feature-packages/season-passes): Feed mission completions into the season by calling SeasonPasses.addProgressToSeason(player, xp) from your mission completion handler.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/feature-packages/season-passes): Give each tier an upperBoundXP threshold and both a freeReward and premiumReward so players see progression for free and paid tracks.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/feature-packages/season-passes): Keep rewardId values numeric and increasing across tiers so tiers display in the order you define them.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/edu-templates/edu-lesson-plan-template): Teach game design by having students change variables like starting gold or spacing to make a game challenging but fun.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/build-it-play-it-island-of-move/personalize-the-game): Tune growthModifier (1 = small movement needed per level, 3 = much more) to control how fast players level up.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/build-it-play-it-island-of-move/personalize-the-game): Raise startMoveSpeed (e.g. 7 to 30) to let players begin fast, changing the early-game feel.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/build-it-play-it-island-of-move/personalize-the-game): Adjust speedBoostPerLevel to set how much WalkSpeed each level adds.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/build-it-play-it-mansion-of-wonder/experienced-landing-page): Use scripts to track each player's current goal and progress so the tutorial advances correctly.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/game-design/design-for-roblox): Build social interaction into gameplay (parties, trading) so players invite friends; single-player games often struggle to build and retain an audience.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/game-design/design-for-roblox): Prioritise fun over visual fidelity; younger users are less sensitive to graphics and stay if they enjoy the game.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/game-design/design-for-roblox): Design UI and UX mobile-first; bind common actions to gamepad buttons and keyboard keys if targeting console or PC.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/game-design/design-for-roblox): Replicate common Roblox UI patterns (e.g. inventory slots along the bottom) so experienced users understand the interface intuitively.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/use-case-tutorials/vfx/use-particles-for-explosions): Reuse the same burst setup with different textures and counts for other effects, such as sparkles for collectibles or impact bursts for projectiles.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/use-case-tutorials/scripting/basic-scripting/fading-trap): Make fading platforms fade gradually before becoming passable so players have a chance to jump off instead of feeling cheated.

- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/fundamentals/coding-3/give-points): Store repeated values like colors and point amounts in variables at the top of the script so they are easy to reuse and tune.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/modules/emote-bar): Mega emotes form when 3-50 nearby players perform the same emote simultaneously; defaults are emoteMinPlayers 3 and emoteMaxPlayers 50, tune them to your player density.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/chat/examples/group-chat-tags): Store player status as attributes (SetAttribute) so it can be reused for area access or bonus experiences elsewhere in the game.

- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/retention): Make core loop steps clear and fun, balance challenge to avoid frustration and boredom, and reward loop completion with feedback, currency, items or achievements.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/discovery-faq): If retention is low, fix core gameplay first; once retention is strong, improve monetization to increase Home impressions.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/publishing/console-guidelines): Make sure every UI element is reachable with basic D-pad, select and back navigation; console navigation is sequential.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/publishing/console-guidelines): Keep key actions to only a few button presses; one-button-at-a-time navigation makes extra steps costly with a controller.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/publishing/console-guidelines): Use progressive disclosure to move advanced or rarely used features to secondary screens; console players go in and out of screens rather than one dense screen.

Keep Getting Up enabled whenever Falling Down is enabled, or characters trip and never recover.

- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/battle-royale/the-storm): Shrinking storm damage forces players closer together, which helps battle royale matches end in a reasonable time.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/battle-royale/the-storm): Set move_scale to 0 so each stage's storm stays fully inside the previous stage's bounds, keeping shrink predictable.

- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/rewarded-video-playbooks/incremental-sim): Offer a welcome-back reward that doubles offline earnings on re-entry.

- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/developer/planning-for-educational-settings): Build core loops that require players to demonstrate understanding to progress; give students voice and choice so rote memorisation does not solve problems.

- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/feature-packages/missions): Reward players for interacting with the game's various systems to surface them and increase engagement.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/feature-packages/missions): Give missions an empty tasks list to create instantly claimable rewards.

- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/game-design/onboarding): Keep XP thresholds low for early levels so players level up quickly and feel progression immediately.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/game-design/onboarding): Use Configs to tune XP thresholds and rewards live without restarting servers or pushing a full update.

- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/curriculums/core): Build a simple core loop: collect coins, trade them for jump power, and use that power to reach higher platforms toward a goal.

- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/get-started/strategies): Ensure enough content is available upfront so new users can have fun in their first session when gating progression.

- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/game-design/balance-virtual-economies): Give events a unique event currency spent only in a special event shop to keep the main economy untouched; this is the safest approach.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/game-design/balance-virtual-economies): Use resource sinks such as limited-time cosmetics, sequential event spending, or event shops to remove excess currency from circulation.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/game-design/balance-virtual-economies): Aim for event sources and sinks elevated during the event but moving closely together to keep balance.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/chat/in-experience-text-chat): Build custom commands with TextChatCommand parented to TextChatService; set CreateDefaultCommands/CreateDefaultTextChannels to true for defaults.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/the-mystery-of-duvall-drive/immersive-narrative): Decide what story/info to convey first, then choose how the world communicates it; keep diegetic over UI overlays to preserve immersion.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/the-mystery-of-duvall-drive/main-design-requirements): Use game states to gate progression: the player can only pick up, place, or trigger a seal during the matching state.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/adventure-game-series/buying-upgrades): Close the core loop with a purchasable upgrade (bigger bag) that increases harvest per trip and therefore gold earned.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/game-design/ui-ux-design): Show only contextually relevant buttons and HUD elements (e.g. Sprint/Tackle without ball, Deke/Pass with ball) to avoid clutter.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/game-design/ui-ux-design): Minimize steps in key flows (e.g. avatar equip) to keep friction low; map each flow as a flow chart to find pain points.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/game-design/ui-ux-design): Give feedback for every action: hit reactions, button hover/pressed states, purchase sound effects, progress bars for objectives.
- 2026-10-30 Roblox Creators [27:32](https://youtu.be/gy_fAtIkqfo?t=1652): Balance for both casuals and competitive players by including gimmicky, easily countered weapons with distinct silhouettes and roles, instead of many near-identical snipers.
- 2026-10-30 Roblox Creators [29:35](https://youtu.be/gy_fAtIkqfo?t=1775): Give each weapon a clear identity so players choose by preference rather than comparing stats.
